### [1] [Information-Gain Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20664)
*Chenxi Li,Xueyang Feng,Songyang Liu,Chenxu Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Chaozhuo Li,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出on-policy distillation中的"证据盲监督"问题，并给出基于检索后信息增益的校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度搜索任务中，学生智能体需迭代检索外部证据；标准on-policy distillation仅依赖前序上下文监督每个查询，无法判断哪个查询能返回有用证据，导致监督与证据效用脱节。

Method: 按交互轮次重分组OPD目标，识别出预算先验（按token量分配监督）与方向先验（偏向教师偏好的token）；提出方法用检索后信息增益（IG）校准这两个先验：将监督重新分配到信息关键轮次，并用检索证据的效用校准查询偏好。

Result: 实证发现首个搜索轮次占总绝对信息增益51.1%但仅获18.0%监督预算；43.7%查询的教师奖励与检索后信息效用符号不一致。六个多模态搜索基准上，2B和4B学生平均准确率分别提升3.4和1.7个百分点，4B学生在MMSearch与BrowseComp-VL上超过其8B教师。

Conclusion: 证据盲监督源于OPD的隐式先验，用检索后信息增益校准可有效缓解该问题，显著提升小模型深度搜索能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as a promising approach for transferring the capabilities of large agents to compact students. In deep search, where agents iteratively retrieve external evidence, a teacher's preference among plausible queries before retrieval may not predict which queries will return useful evidence. Standard OPD nevertheless supervises each student query from the preceding context alone, inducing a failure mode we term \textbf{\textit{evidence-blind supervision (EBS)}}. To understand this, we regroup the OPD objective by interaction turns and reveal two implicit priors: a \textit{budget prior} that allocates supervision by token volume, and a \textit{direction prior} that favors tokens preferred by the teacher, neither of which accounts for the evidence returned by the current query. Our empirical analysis shows that both priors contribute to EBS in practice: the first search turn accounts for 51.1\% of total absolute information gain but receives only 18.0\% of the supervision budget, while 43.7\% of search queries have teacher rewards that disagree in sign with post-retrieval information utility. To mitigate this, we propose \ours, which uses post-retrieval information gain (IG) to calibrate both priors: reallocating supervision toward information-critical turns while calibrating query preferences using the utility of retrieved evidence. Experiments on six multimodal search benchmarks show that \ours improves average accuracy by 3.4 and 1.7 percentage points for 2B and 4B students over standard OPD, enabling the 4B student to surpass its 8B teacher on MMSearch and BrowseComp-VL.

</details>


### [2] [Idea2Prior: Auditable Paper Retrieval and Ranking from Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20857)
*Shitian Yang,Ruijie Tao,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出可审计文献检索系统（基于 PaperArena），融合多源发现、身份核验、面向方面的摘要评估、语义引文扩展与联合 Expert 排序；检索模式最多返回 5 篇论文。历史对比中 Hit@5 为 53.3%，高于 ChatGPT（43.3%）与 Gemini（36.7%）Deep Research，优势集中于 EASY/MEDIUM，HARD 上 ChatGPT 领先；平均耗时 8 分 53 秒，API 样本均价 $0.0945。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非正式研究想法出发检索论文，仅靠主题词匹配不足：相关工作可能使用不同术语，且其关键区分机制往往不出现在摘要中。因此需要一个可审计、能超越表面关键词的“想法→论文”检索系统。

Method: 系统构建于 PaperArena 之上，集成五个模块：多源发现（multi-source discovery）、文献身份验证（identity verification）、基于方面的摘要评估（facet-based abstract assessment）、语义引文扩展（semantic citation expansion）、联合 Expert 排序（joint Expert ranking）。检索专用模式从最多 60 篇候选短名单中排序至多 5 篇，使用完整可用摘要与从五次固定获取尝试中本地选出的 PDF 证据，不生成长篇报告；另提供可选的实现分析与引文演化工具。

Result: 在 EASY、MEDIUM、HARD 各十问的历史对比中，按文档化统计口径，系统 Hit@5 为 53.3%，ChatGPT web Deep Research 为 43.3%，Gemini web Deep Research 为 36.7%；优势集中在 EASY 与 MEDIUM，HARD 由 ChatGPT 领先。所选运行平均耗时 8 分 53 秒，用户自报的网络端平均为 5.2 与 4.7 分钟。另一组九个查询的 API 样本平均每任务 $0.0945（历史成本观测，非与网页订阅成本的配对比较）。成对 Top 5 覆盖率介于 6%–34%，说明返回证据集存在差异。

Conclusion: 结果表明该系统有助于“想法→论文”的定向检索；但通用网络研究、论坛检索与更广泛的相关性评估仍未解决，留作开放问题。

Abstract: Finding a paper from an informal research idea requires more than matching topic words: relevant work may use different terminology, and its distinguishing mechanism may be absent from the abstract. We present \system{}, an auditable literature-search system built on PaperArena. It combines multi-source discovery, identity verification, facet-based abstract assessment, semantic citation expansion, and joint Expert ranking. A retrieval-only mode ranks up to five papers from a shortlist of at most 60, using complete available abstracts and locally selected PDF evidence from five fixed acquisition attempts, without generating a long-form report. In a historical comparison covering ten queries from each of EASY, MEDIUM, and HARD, aggregate reported Hit@5 is 53.3\% for \system{}, 43.3\% for ChatGPT web Deep Research, and 36.7\% for Gemini web Deep Research under the documented accounting convention. The advantage is concentrated in EASY and MEDIUM; ChatGPT leads on HARD. Selected \system{} runs average 8 minutes 53 seconds, compared with user-reported web averages of 5.2 and 4.7 minutes. A separate nine-query API sample averages \$0.0945 per task; this is a historical cost observation, not a matched comparison with web subscription costs. Pairwise Top 5 coverage ranges from 6\% to 34\%, showing differing returned evidence sets. The system also exposes optional implementation analysis and citation-evolution tools. These results support targeted idea-to-paper retrieval while leaving general web research, forum search, and broader relevance evaluation open. https://github.com/ystok66/IDEA2PRIOR

</details>


### [3] [TransX Technical Report: Scaling Sequence Transduction for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20913)
*Da Xu,Liyan Fang,Divya Venugopalan,Sunny Hsu,Xukai Wang,Rishav Roy Chowdhury,Cindy Liang,Nishant Satya Lakshmikanth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransX 是一个面向工业推荐系统的序列转导框架与配套基础设施，通过解耦序列编码、行为记忆与候选条件融合，实现模型-算力分配的显式控制与执行摊销，在训练吞吐、服务成本与效果上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐模型的容量增长通常带来服务成本近似成比例上升，缺乏对"模型-算力分配"的显式控制与执行摊销机制，导致扩展性受限。作者希望打破"服务成本随模型容量线性增长"的固有约束。

Method: 提出 TransX 序列转导框架：将序列编码、行为记忆、候选条件融合拆分为可独立扩展的组件。训练端将因果轨迹计算跨行为前缀摊销，并把记忆状态扇出至候选；服务端将批量 prefill、近线刷新与在线预测解耦，依赖紧凑的行为记忆 KV。在点击与转化上训练，并对共享行为接口做检索与偏好排序的后训练，另离线探索生成式推荐。

Result: 观察到各向异性扩展收益：固定记忆分辨率与融合容量下，编码器扩展在中度深度后趋于饱和；增加监督信号会将分配前沿推向候选条件融合；模型容量增长时，保持质量所需的记忆分辨率大致稳定。线上 A/B 中，核心 TransX 提升 CTR 6.02%、转化 4.45%；后训练的检索与偏好排序变体分别提升 CTR 2.96%、转化 1.33%。同训练条件下监督目标吞吐提升 84%；服务解耦使总服务成本较共置服务降低近 80%，在相同总成本下可达 HSTU 类基线约 5 倍的单请求模型 FLOPs；相同单请求 FLOPs 下，单张 H200 的请求吞吐约为 4 倍。

Conclusion: 通过框架与基础设施协同设计，TransX 证明在线服务成本不必随推荐模型容量成比例增长，为工业推荐的可扩展部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present TransX, a sequence-transduction framework and co-designed infrastructure for scaling industrial recommendation with explicit control over model-compute allocation and execution amortization. TransX separates sequence encoding, behavior memory, and candidate-conditioned fusion into independently scalable components. Training amortizes causal trajectory computation across behavioral prefixes and fans out memory states to candidates; serving disaggregates bulk prefill, nearline refresh, and online prediction through compact behavior-memory KV.
  We observe anisotropic scaling returns: encoder scaling may saturate beyond moderate depth at fixed memory resolution and fusion capacity; increasing supervision shifts the allocation frontier toward candidate-conditioned fusion; and the memory resolution needed to preserve quality remains roughly stable as model capacity grows. We train TransX on click and conversion, post-train its shared behavioral interface for retrieval and preference ranking, and explore generative recommendation offline.
  In separate production A/B experiments, the core TransX model improves CTR by 6.02% and conversion by 4.45%, while post-trained retrieval and preference-ranking variants improve CTR by 2.96% and conversion by 1.33%, respectively, over corresponding production baselines. Under matched training conditions, the training infrastructure increases supervised-target throughput by 84%. In serving, disaggregation reduces TransX's total serving cost by nearly 80% versus colocated serving at matched traffic and SLO, enabling approximately 5X the per-request model FLOPs of the HSTU-style production baseline at the same total serving cost. At matched per-request model FLOPs, TransX delivers approximately 4X higher request throughput per H200. These results show that online serving cost need not grow proportionally with recommendation model capacity.

</details>


### [4] [Learning When to Think: Utility-Guided Adaptive Reasoning for Efficient Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21030)
*Yingtao Luo,Kangyi Zhao,Shiyi Du,Yubo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AURA直接监督中间推理状态，训练轻量效用估计器自适应调整推理深度，在BRIGHT上取得34.7 nDCG@10，并将延迟从4.6秒降至0.76秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理条件嵌入可通过逐步细化语义约束提升检索效果，但额外推理可能引入过度泛化或语义噪声的抽象，削弱检索特异性。现有方法主要依赖最终检索结果监督推理，对推理精炼过程本身缺乏指导。

Method: AURA根据中间推理状态对相关文档与困难干扰项的区分能力，直接监督这些中间状态；并基于前缀条件嵌入训练一个轻量级效用估计器，在推理时自适应调整推理深度（自适应停止）。

Result: 在推理密集型BRIGHT基准上，AURA-4B平均nDCG@10达34.7，每查询使用2.8个推理步和96个生成token；在相同部署条件下，自适应停止将延迟从4.6秒降至0.76秒。MS MARCO上的结果进一步表明该专门化不会显著损害常规域内检索。

Conclusion: 对推理过程的中间状态进行直接监督，并结合自适应深度控制，可在提升推理密集型检索性能的同时显著降低延迟，且不显著影响常规域内检索。

Abstract: Reasoning-conditioned embeddings can improve retrieval by progressively refining semantic constraints. However, additional reasoning may introduce over-generalized or semantically noisy abstractions that weaken retrieval specificity. Existing methods largely supervise reasoning through final retrieval outcomes, providing limited guidance over the refinement process itself. We propose AURA, a framework that directly supervises intermediate reasoning states according to how well they separate relevant documents from hard distractors. AURA further trains a lightweight utility estimator over prefix-conditioned embeddings to adapt reasoning depth at inference time. On the reasoning-intensive BRIGHT benchmark, AURA-4B achieves 34.7 average nDCG@10 while using 2.8 reasoning steps and 96 generated tokens per query. Under matched deployment conditions, adaptive stopping reduces latency from 4.6 to 0.76 seconds relative to full-trajectory reasoning. Results on MS MARCO further suggest that this specialization need not substantially degrade conventional in-domain retrieval.

</details>


### [5] [RecSoup: Efficient Model Merging Approach for Controllable Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21220)
*Zidong Jia,Guofu Xie,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RecSoup，一种基于专用模型合并的轻量两阶段可控多目标推荐方法，实现推理时动态权衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控多目标推荐方法依赖输出级重排或参数生成模块，前者限制了可行权衡范围，后者带来高昂训练与推理成本。模型合并虽有潜力，但直接应用于推荐系统面临缺乏共享线性区域与跨目标干扰的挑战。

Method: RecSoup采用两阶段策略：首先通过同时考虑目标特定重要性与跨目标相关性的剪枝策略，缓解目标间参数干扰；随后通过可学习的偏好到系数映射进行自适应参数合并，实现推理时动态权衡控制，无需联合重训练或额外生成模块。

Result: 理论上证明RecSoup在有效性与效率上优于参数生成范式，可无平滑性约束地完全遍历Pareto前沿。多基准实验表明其提升了Pareto前沿质量并实现有效可控性，推理时延比参数生成方法快165--228倍。

Conclusion: RecSoup以轻量两阶段模型合并方式有效解决可控多目标推荐中的权衡控制问题，兼顾效果与效率。

Abstract: Recommender systems often need to balance multiple conflicting objectives. Controllable Multi-Objective Recommendation (CMOR) enables dynamic adjustment of objective trade-offs at inference time without retraining, adapting to shifting user or system requirements. Existing CMOR approaches typically rely on output-level reranking or parameter-generating modules, which either limit the scope of feasible trade-offs or incur substantial training and inference cost. Model merging provides a promising alternative by reusing multiple objective-specific models via parameter combination. However, its direct application to recommender systems is challenging due to the lack of a shared linear regime and cross-objective interference. To address these issues, we propose RecSoup, a lightweight two-stage approach for effective CMOR via specialized model merging. RecSoup first mitigates parameter interference across objectives through a pruning strategy that jointly considers objective-specific importance and cross-objective relevance. It then performs adaptive parameter merging via a learnable preference-to-coefficient mapping, allowing dynamic trade-off control at inference time without joint retraining or additional generative modules. Theoretically, we prove that RecSoup outperforms the parameter generation paradigm in effectiveness and efficiency, as it can fully navigate the Pareto front without smoothness constraints. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that RecSoup achieves improved Pareto front quality and effective controllability while delivers 165--228x faster inference-time latency than parameter generation approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/35798642/RECSOUP.git.

</details>


### [6] [VoteGEO-Bench: Measuring the Impact of Generative Engine Optimization on Electoral Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21330)
*Shiqi Zhang,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出VoteGEO-Bench基准，研究生成引擎优化（GEO）在选举信息场景下的风险，覆盖2024年美国大选与2026年日本众议院选举，评估11种网页改写策略对生成引擎引用与曝光的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于获取选举信息，但外部网页内容如何塑造其呈现的政治信息尚不清楚；GEO通过改写网页提升其在生成回答中的可见度，其在投票场景中的潜在风险缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建VoteGEO-Bench基准，整合选举相关查询、历史网页与选民画像，覆盖2024年美国大选和2026年日本众议院选举；先分析生成引擎引用的来源分布与跨天可见性，再在4类共11种改写策略下评估目标指定、跨引擎与跨选举有效性以及选民画像的作用。

Result: 引用多来自低成本的自有与开放平台来源，部分网页可连续数天保持可见；未指定目标的改写很少提升目标曝光，指定目标的改写更常见效；优化页面结构或以论证强化立场的策略收益高于补充证据或改动措辞；两种最强策略在美国各类画像上收益普遍为正，但画像条件化无一致影响；显性党派身份造成的基线曝光差距可大于改写单页面的收益，党派性隐含时基线变化取决于引擎对该信号的响应强度。

Conclusion: GEO可被用于操纵生成引擎的选举信息呈现，其效果依赖是否指定目标与具体策略类型，且可能被结构化身份信号放大；需关注其在投票场景中的信息操纵风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for election information, yet little is known about how external web content shapes the political information they present. Generative engine optimization (GEO) rewrites web content to improve its visibility in generated responses. To investigate its potential risks in voting scenarios (VoteGEO), we introduce VoteGEO-Bench, a benchmark covering the 2024 U.S. presidential election and the 2026 Japanese House of Representatives election, combining election-related queries, historical webpages, and voter personas. We first analyze sources cited by generative engines and find that many citations come from lower-cost owned and open-platform sources, while some webpages remain visible across days. We then evaluate 11 rewriting strategies in 4 categories, examining target specification, effectiveness across engines and elections, and the role of voter personas. Rewriting without specifying a target rarely increases target exposure, whereas target-specified rewriting more often does. Strategies that improve page organization or strengthen a position through argumentation yield greater gains than adding supporting evidence or changing wording. For the two strongest strategies, gains remain broadly positive across evaluated U.S. personas, but persona conditioning shows no consistent effect on these gains. Explicit partisan identities can create baseline exposure gaps larger than the gain from rewriting one webpage. When partisanship is implicit in the voter profile, baseline shifts depend on how strongly the engine responds to that signal.

</details>


### [7] [Aligning LLMs with Sparse Retrieval via Adaptive Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21877)
*Tongna Chen,Minghan Li,Guodong Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的稀疏检索框架，通过输入自适应的稀疏对齐（动态α截断）与检索微调，在保持检索效果的同时大幅降低索引存储和查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM语义表示能力强，但直接用于一阶段检索时延迟和存储成本高；SPLADE等神经稀疏检索器效率高，但所用编码器容量有限、表示能力不足。目标是在两者间取得平衡。

Method: 提出输入自适应的稀疏对齐框架：引入动态α截断策略，依据SPLADE表示的累积激活质量选择输入特定的监督支撑集，引导LLM保留显著词汇信号、抑制低信息激活，不依赖固定的支撑预算；随后用对比学习、知识蒸馏与稀疏正则化进行检索微调。

Result: 在MS MARCO及6个BEIR数据集上，相比SPLADE++有提升，与LLM-based稀疏检索器效果相当；相比Lion-sp，索引存储减少6.4倍，端到端查询延迟降低超11倍。

Conclusion: 该方法在效果与效率间取得有利权衡，验证了动态自适应稀疏对齐在LLM稀疏检索中的有效性。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) offer strong semantic representations, their application to first-stage retrieval incurs substantial latency and storage costs. Neural sparse retrievers such as SPLADE provide efficient retrieval but rely on relatively compact encoders with limited representational capacity. To bridge this gap, we propose an LLM-based sparse retrieval framework with input-adaptive sparse alignment. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic $α$-truncation strategy that selects input-specific supervision supports based on the cumulative activation mass of SPLADE representations. This strategy guides the LLM to preserve salient lexical signals while suppressing less informative activations, without relying on a fixed support budget. Subsequent retrieval fine-tuning combines contrastive learning, knowledge distillation, and sparsity regularization to improve retrieval effectiveness. Experiments on MS MARCO and six selected BEIR datasets demonstrate improvements over SPLADE++ while maintaining competitive effectiveness against LLM-based sparse retrievers. Compared with Lion-sp, our method reduces index storage by 6.4-fold and end-to-end query latency by over 11-fold, achieving a favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [8] [GACC-Bench: Benchmarking Goal-Adaptive Context Compression Ability across Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21893)
*Weiting Liu,Zhiyu Pan,Shuhong Wang,Zhenquan Yao,Jungang Li,Li Zhao,Jian Jiang,Jianfeng Feng,Ai Han,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 GACC-Bench，一个用于评估大语言模型"目标自适应上下文压缩"能力的基准，涵盖 500 个目标与 2,571 个问题，并要求压缩器在下游问题未知的情况下依据高层目标保留信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的 LLM 应用需要在具体下游问题尚未确定之前就压缩上下文，而现有压缩评测通常以已知问题或通用信息量为导向，无法衡量模型能否依据指定的高层目标自适应地保留目标相关信息。

Method: 构建 GACC-Bench：对每个源上下文，按共享的下游目标对源自原文的问题进行分组，从而导出若干高层目标；压缩器输入为源上下文、目标与上下文长度预算，评测问题被隐藏；随后由固定的 reader 使用同一压缩上下文逐一回答问题；数据覆盖 7 个集合的 281 个文档与智能体轨迹上下文，共 500 个目标、2,571 个问题，全部经人工专家审核。

Result: 对 10 个 LLM 压缩器的评测显示，即使最优模型与全上下文性能之间仍存在显著差距，尤其在"支持某目标下每个问题均正确回答"这一指标上差距突出；与目标无关的压缩相比，目标条件化能稳定提升准确率，而错误目标条件化则会降低准确率。

Conclusion: GACC-Bench 揭示当前 LLM 在目标自适应上下文压缩上仍远未达标，并证明目标条件化的方向性效应（匹配提升、错配损害），为后续研究提供了标准化评测与开源资源。

Abstract: Context compression in long-running applications of large language models (LLMs) must preserve goal-relevant information, often before specific downstream questions are known. We introduce GACC-Bench, a benchmark for Goal-Adaptive Context Compression that evaluates how reliably LLMs adapt information retention to specified high-level goals. For each source context, we derive one or more high-level goals by grouping source-grounded questions according to shared downstream objectives. The benchmark contains 500 goals and 2,571 questions over 281 document and agent-trace contexts from seven collections spanning diverse domains, with all instances reviewed by human experts. The compressor receives a source context, a goal, and a context-length budget, with the associated evaluation questions withheld. A fixed reader answers these questions individually using the same compressed context. Our evaluation of ten LLM compressors reveals substantial gaps to full-context performance even for the best-performing model, particularly in supporting correct answers to every question associated with a goal. Compared with goal-agnostic compression, target-goal conditioning consistently improves accuracy, whereas mismatched-goal conditioning reduces it. GACC-Bench is available at https://github.com/weiting24/GACC-Bench.

</details>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [9] [Harnessing MPI mutations for AI error detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20616)
*Asia Auville,Tim Jammer,Eric Petit,Pablo de Oliveira Castro,Emmanuelle Saillard,Mihail Popov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MPI突变数据集（MMD）与AI检测方法：用AST-T5嵌入训练紧凑MLP检测MPI错误，最高达92.0%准确率；并评估Claude Sonnet 4.5提示策略，整体多分类准确率73.9%、缺陷召回率87.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI错误难以识别：动态工具准确但需profiling、开销大；静态分析开销低但准确率不足。LLM/AI为提高静态分析精度并保持低开销提供了新途径，但现有方法难以基准测试、过于通用、且未针对高性能计算场景适配。

Method: 构建MMD数据集：从GitHub数千开源项目提取MPI程序，经排序与过滤后用合成代码突变注入贴近真实开发者错误的缺陷；基于AST-T5嵌入训练紧凑多层感知机（MLP）；采用MPI-BugBench（MBB）分类体系；并针对Claude Sonnet 4.5设计不同提示策略与agentic流水线。

Result: MLP检测多种MPI错误（如CallOrdering、InvalidParameter）准确率达92.0%，部分场景优于专家提示的Qwen-3 Coder LLM，并与成熟动态验证工具具竞争力。Claude Sonnet 4.5经工程化提示后InvalidParam检测准确率由84.8%提升至93.6%，超过动态工具ITAC（87.0%）；完整agentic流水线在MBB上整体多分类准确率73.9%，缺陷召回率87.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动方法可在低开销下高效准确检测真实MPI应用的错误，MMD可作为基础支撑大规模验证工作，用于深入研究各类MPI错误。

Abstract: MPI errors are challenging to identify despite the significant number of expert verification tools. Dynamic tools (i.e., requiring profiling) are computationally expensive and accurate in error detection, whereas static analysis (i.e., operating at source code or compilation) is computationally cheap but less accurate. Interestingly, the recent success of AI and LLMs offers an alternative to increase static analysis accuracy while preserving its low overhead. Yet current methods remain difficult to benchmark, too general, and poorly adapted to the specific challenges of high-performance computing. In this paper, we investigate how AI-powered tools can efficiently and accurately detect errors in real-world MPI applications. We propose a novel MPI Mutated Dataset (MMD), constructed from MPI programs extracted from thousands of open-source GitHub projects. After sorting and filtering these files, we inject errors that realistically emulate developers' mistakes using synthetic code mutations. We leverage the dataset to train different AI models and assess their generalization capabilities against standard verification tools. We train compact multilayer perceptrons (MLP) with AST-T5 embeddings (i.e., transformer only encoder network) to detect diverse MPI errors (from the MPI-BugBench (MBB) classification) including CallOrdering or InvalidParameter, achieving detection accuracies up to 92.0%. In some cases, our method outperforms the expert prompted Qwen-3 Coder LLM and provides competitive results compared to established dynamic verification tools. We further evaluate Claude Sonnet 4.5 across different prompting strategies, showing that engineered prompts improve InvalidParam detection accuracy from 84.8% to 93.6%, surpassing the dynamic tool ITAC (87.0%). The full agentic pipeline achieves 73.9% overall multi-class accuracy and 87.6% bug detection recall on MBB. This paves the way for large-scale verification efforts that can use our dataset as a foundation to thoroughly investigate each MPI error in real world applications.

</details>


### [10] [Developer Experiences with Cursor AI-native IDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20618)
*Haoran Xue,Gias Uddin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过分析Cursor官方论坛14,714条帖子（历时两年多），揭示开发者对AI原生IDE的评估摩擦：71%讨论集中于上下文索引、模型路由、智能体工作流等关键机制，负面体验集中于可用性、定价与可靠性，且摩擦焦点随时间从准入与设置转向定价、稳健性与持续工作流可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的IDE日益成为实际开发工具，但开发者如何评估其作为工作环境、何种摩擦影响采用、以及哪些体验维度在日用中最重要，仍缺乏研究。既有证据多基于受控任务、小样本访谈与短时观察，难以反映纵向、社区驱动的真实使用经验与工作流漂移。

Method: 对AI原生IDE代表Cursor开展大规模纵向研究，以官方公共论坛为数据来源（用户报告缺陷、请求功能、讨论日常使用），采集并分析超过两年内的14,714条论坛帖子。

Result: 讨论以工作流关键机制为主导（71%），如上下文索引、模型路由与智能体工作流，这些既是感知价值也是挫败感的主要来源。负面体验集中在可用性、定价与可靠性，包括安装与设置、使用限额与消耗、崩溃与延迟。随时间推移，摩擦焦点从早期准入与设置障碍转向定价、稳健性与持续工作流可靠性。

Conclusion: AI IDE的采用与持续使用受工作流核心机制与运维性因素（定价、稳健性、可靠性）共同制约；开发者体验的关键矛盾在于同一机制既创造价值又引发摩擦。研究提示AI IDE设计需在能力集成的同时重视长期可依赖性与成本可持续性。

Abstract: AI-powered integrated development environments (IDEs) are increasingly positioned as practical tools for software development, integrating AI assistance into everyday activities such as editing, refactoring, and debugging. However, how developers evaluate AI IDEs as working environments, what frictions shape their adoption, and which aspects of the experience matter most in everyday use remain underexplored. Prior evidence on adoption and use is mainly controlled tasks, small interview samples, and short observation windows, which offer limited visibility into longitudinal, community-driven experience and the workflow drift that emerges in everyday practice. To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale longitudinal study of Cursor, a prominent AI-native IDE, through its official public forum, where users report bugs, request features, and discuss everyday use. Our analysis covers 14,714 forum posts collected over more than two years. We find that discussion is dominated by workflow-critical mechanisms (71%) such as context indexing, model routing, and agent workflows, which are also the main sources of both perceived value and frustration. Negative experiences center on usability, pricing, and dependability, including installation and setup, usage limits and consumption, crashes, and latency. Over time, the focus of friction shifts from early access and setup barriers toward pricing, robustness, and sustained workflow reliability.

</details>


### [11] [HPCAgent-Bench: A Framework for Sound Benchmarking of Code-Optimizing Agents, Harnesses, and Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20673)
*Yakup Koray Budanaz,Lorenzo Paleari,Marcin Copik,Alexandru Calotoiu,Timo Schneider,Benjamin Chen Ming Choong,Shriram Chandran,Lars Hulsbergen,Levi Widmer,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 HPCAgent-Bench，一个包含689个内核、覆盖循环级优化、HPC代码与机器学习模型的编码智能体优化评测基准，支持跨编程模型、硬件与节点配置，并引入干预效度与加权token成本两项指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准固定单一加速器、编程模型或运行框架，无法隔离各组件贡献，也无法判断新增技能与工具是否物有所值，缺乏对智能体优化能力的可靠、可比较评估。

Method: 框架用NumPy一次性指定内核，自动派生C与Fortran参考实现，通过统一C ABI或Python接口接收提交，可在CPU/GPU、单节点/多节点上运行；外部评审器用隐藏模糊输入校验正确性并计时，封堵利用测试系统的路径。同时定义干预效度（配对运行衡量加速比、成功率与成本）和加权token成本（对输入、缓存输入、输出token按显式权重计算）。

Result: 智能体相较自动并行器最高获得4.2倍加速；优化可迁移至另一台机器，93%–99%无设备特定代码的答案保持正确，逐内核加速比秩相关rs≥0.84。在40组匹配配置中，干预仅在2次显著改变加速比，但token成本变化最高达3.0倍。

Conclusion: token权重决定节省是否显著，因此公开权重并发布全部token计数作为成本报告标准；HPCAgent-Bench是首个全面、广泛且加固的智能体优化评估基准，提供透明的成本度量。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used to optimize code, but existing benchmarks fix one accelerator, programming model or harness, so they cannot isolate what each component contributes or show whether added skills and tools repay their cost. We present HPCAgent-Bench with 689 kernels in three tracks: loop-level optimization challenges, kernels extracted from HPC codes, and machine-learning models. The framework specifies each kernel once in NumPy, derives C and Fortran references, accepts submissions through a common C ABI or Python interface, and runs them on CPUs or GPUs, on one or multiple nodes. An external judge checks correctness on hidden fuzzed inputs and times each submission, closing paths agents use to exploit the testing system. We introduce two metrics for reporting results: \textit{intervention efficacy} measures an intervention, such as adding a tool, through paired runs on speedup, success rate and cost, and \textit{weighted token cost} reports input, cached-input and output tokens under explicit weights. Agents reach up to $4.2\times$ speedup over automatic parallelizers, and their optimizations transfer to another machine: $93$--$99\%$ of answers without device-specific code remain correct, and per-kernel speedups keep a rank correlation of $r_s\ge0.84$. Across 40 matched setups, interventions significantly change speedup only twice but token cost by up to $3.0\times$. Because token weighting decides whether a saving is significant, we state the weights and release every token count as a standard for cost reporting. We provide the first comprehensive, broad and hardened benchmark for the sound evaluation of agentic optimization, with transparent cost metrics.

</details>


### [12] [Robotic-supported Data Loss Detection in Android Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20807)
*Davi Freitas,Breno Miranda,Juliano Iyoda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 R-DLD：一种机器人辅助的 Android 应用数据丢失检测基础设施，通过经济材料搭建的机器人直接与手机传感器交互，实现非侵入、更真实的测试；在 77 个随机选取的应用中检出 341 个数据丢失问题，开发者回复率 100%，其中 89.55% 确认问题，35.82% 已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机应用开发面临两大测试挑战：(i) 硬件与操作系统版本多样，需跨多配置测试；(ii) 测试须以非侵入方式（模拟终端用户交互）进行，而人工执行昂贵、繁琐且易出错。机器人自动化被认为可应对设备多样性与非侵入测试需求，并能实现快速、准确、可重复的测试，从而减少人工耗时、加速开发周期。

Method: 提出 R-DLD（Robotic-supported Data Loss Detection），一种机器人辅助的 Android 应用数据丢失检测基础设施。该方案通过机器人直接与智能手机传感器交互，实现非侵入且更贴近真实使用场景的测试；机器人采用低成本材料构建，以降低在测试环境中的部署门槛。

Result: 在实证评估中，R-DLD 于从 Android 应用商店随机选取的 77 个应用中成功识别出 341 个数据丢失问题。所有报告的缺陷均获得开发者回复，其中 89.55% 确认存在数据丢失问题，35.82% 的问题随后得到修复。

Conclusion: R-DLD 证明了机器人辅助方式在 Android 应用数据丢失检测中的有效性：可在真实应用集合上大规模发现真实缺陷，并获得开发者较高比例的问题确认与后续修复，说明该低成本、非侵入的机器人测试基础设施具备实用价值与推广潜力。

Abstract: Smartphones have become integral to modern life due to their diverse applications. However, the development of applications for these devices faces significant challenges, primarily due to two factors: (i) the diverse hardware and operating system versions that require testing across multiple configurations; and (ii) the imperative activity of testing the smartphone in a non-invasive way (similar to an end-user interaction), which is expensive, tedious and error-prone as it is usually carried out manually. In this context, robotic automation has emerged as an effective solution for addressing the diversity of smartphone systems and versions, and in performing some testing tasks non-invasively. In addition, automated robots facilitate quick, accurate, and repetitive testing, thus enabling developers to validate their applications across various configurations effectively. This results in a less invasive verification and in a significant reduction in time spent on manual testing, thus accelerating the development cycle. Our work proposes R-DLD (Robotic-supported Data Loss Detection), a robot-assisted infrastructure for data loss detection in Android applications, offeringless invasive and more realistic tests by interacting directly with smartphone sensors. The robot is constructed using cost-effective materials, facilitating its adoption in testing environments. In our empirical evaluation R-DLD successfully identified 341 data loss issues in 77 randomly selected apps from an Android store. All reported bugs received responses from the developer, with 89.55% confirming the data loss problems, while 35.82% have being subsequently fixed.

</details>


### [13] [Skip the Compressor: Coding Agents Fix More Issues When They Pick Whole Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21073)
*Xiaocong Yang,Rishit Chatterjee,Himanshu Udupi,Vikram Ramavarapu,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在相同token预算下，让编码智能体逐字看到被选中文件的完整内容（精确文件选择），比压缩token或检索代码片段更能提升问题解决率；简单选择器CQ2-CARE超越三种压缩器、三种检索器及两种专为问题解决训练的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体面对的代码仓库常远超其上下文窗口容量。现有提示压缩器与检索器各有缺陷：检索器按与问题的相似度排序代码，而非按"是否必须修改"排序，常漏掉需要编辑的文件；压缩器虽保留文件却删改文本，导致智能体无法逐字复现待编辑代码行，补丁难以应用。修补问题同时需要两样东西：必须修改的文件，以及它们原样的代码。

Method: 提出简单选择器CQ2-CARE：在固定token预算下，将预算全部用于选择以何种文件原样（verbatim）展示，而非用于裁剪token或检索代码块。在300个近期SWE-rebench问题上，对每个问题施加相同的单问题预算来拟合上下文，并用基准测试对每个补丁进行评分；对比三种压缩器、三种检索器以及两种为问题解决训练的方法（SweRank、SWE-Pruner）。

Result: 使用GPT-5.6与Claude Opus 5时，CQ2-CARE的问题解决率分别为43.7%和46.7%，比三种压缩器中最佳者高出6.3个百分点，比三种检索器中最佳者分别高出9.3和12.7个百分点；并超过SweRank（高0.7和1.7个百分点）与SWE-Pruner（高2.0和7.3个百分点）。

Conclusion: 基线方法的缺陷在于：检索器常漏掉待编辑文件，压缩器保留文件却改动其文本，导致能成功应用的补丁更少。精确文件选择无需压缩模型，并原样呈现仓库中的每一行代码，使模型能够引用其要编辑的代码，从而在同等token预算下取得更高的解决率。

Abstract: A coding agent works on repositories that can be far larger than its context window can hold. Prompt compressors and retrievers offer solutions to this problem, but patching an issue needs two things at once: the files that must change, and their code exactly as written, since the model must reproduce the lines it edits. Retrievers rank code by its similarity to the issue rather than by whether it must change, and compressors delete text to fit the budget. We show that at an equal token budget, an agent resolves more issues when the budget goes to choosing which files to show verbatim, as our simple selector CQ2-CARE does, than when it goes to pruning tokens or retrieving chunks. We fit every context to the same per-issue budget on 300 recent SWE-rebench issues and grade each patch with the benchmark's tests. With GPT-5.6 and Claude Opus 5, CQ2-CARE resolves 43.7% and 46.7% of the issues, 6.3 points more than the best of three compressors with either model, and 9.3 and 12.7 points more than the best of three retrievers. CQ2-CARE even resolves more issues than two methods trained for issue resolution, SweRank (by 0.7 and 1.7 points) and SWE-Pruner (by 2.0 and 7.3). Our analysis shows the defects of the baseline methods: retrievers often miss the files to edit, and compressors keep them but alter their text, so fewer patches apply. Exact-file selection needs no compression model and shows every line as the repository has it, so the model can quote the code it edits.

</details>


### [14] [Plausible Is Not Enough: Trust and Evidence in AI-Authored Pull Request Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21144)
*Md Shamimur Rahman,Khairul Alam,Banani Roy,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对31国239名从业者的配对问卷调查，发现AI撰写PR的评审在初始信任、证据要求与验证实践上均不同于人类PR，本质上是一个"证据-归属"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI撰写的Pull Request（AIPR）在合并前必须赢得评审者信任，但评审者是否对AIPR沿用与人类PR（HPR）相同的评审标准尚不清楚，这直接影响代码评审工具与智能体工作流的设计。

Method: 对来自31个国家的239名从业者进行问卷调查，采用同一受访者内匹配的HPR与AIPR问题设计，比较信任水平、证据要求与验证实践。

Result: 64.9%受访者初始信任AIPR更低；更少依赖作者履历与子系统熟悉度，转而重视人类提示者专业能力与变更规模；55.2%认为对AIPR仅凭自身对关键场景的推理即可批准（HPR为28.5%），对测试覆盖率依赖更少；61.1%对AIPR证据要求更高；残余不确定性下拒绝批准比例由19.4%（HPR）升至43.9%（AIPR）；更常采用部分重实现验证非平凡逻辑；归属不清导致暂停评审的比例由14.6%升至29.3%。开放题指出缺乏可靠上下文与人类问责时验证"看似合理代码"的困难。

Conclusion: AIPR评审应被界定为证据-归属问题而非单纯的正确性检查。评审者需将生成变更与经验证行为及负责维护者关联；评审工具与智能体工作流应暴露来源信息、区分生成声明与人类验证证据，并在批准前让不确定性可见。

Abstract: Code review is where AI-authored pull requests (AIPRs) must earn reviewer trust before integration, yet it is unclear whether reviewers apply the same criteria used for human-authored pull requests (HPRs). We surveyed 239 practitioners from 31 countries using matched HPR and AIPR questions to compare trust, evidence requirements, and verification practices within respondents. Most respondents (64.9%) reported lower initial trust in AIPRs. They relied less on author track record and subsystem familiarity, emphasizing instead human prompter expertise and bounded change size. For approval, respondents more often considered their own reasoning through critical cases sufficient for AIPRs than HPRs (55.2% versus 28.5%), while relying less on test coverage. Overall, 61.1% reported higher evidence requirements for AIPRs, and the share who would block approval under residual uncertainty increased from 19.4% for HPRs to 43.9% for AIPRs. Respondents prioritized test inspection, project context, and local execution, and more often selected partial reimplementation to verify non-trivial logic. Unclear ownership was also more likely to pause AIPR review (29.3% versus 14.6%). Open-ended responses pointed to the difficulty of validating plausible code without reliable context and human accountability. These findings frame AIPR review as an evidence-ownership problem, not merely a correctness check. Reviewers need to connect generated changes to validated behavior and responsible maintainers. Review tools and agentic workflows should expose provenance, distinguish generated claims from human-validated evidence, and make uncertainty visible before approval.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond Confidence: Rethinking Uncertainty Calibration in Deep Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21248)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了代码深度模型在缺陷预测与漏洞检测任务中的置信度校准与不确定性度量，发现现有指标在严格精度要求下对选择性预测指导有限，并提出面向决策的可靠性评估视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在代码相关任务中表现出潜力，置信度校准技术被提出以提升模型可靠性，但其在具有严格精度要求的真实任务中是否有效仍不明确。因此作者希望探究两个问题：一是现有的度量指标（置信度分数与不确定性指标）是否能有效指导选择性预测；二是现有校准技术能否改进这些指标，从而更好识别模型预测可能成功或失败的案例。

Method: 作者对缺陷预测和漏洞检测任务进行了系统性研究，使用了七种代码模型，评估了十三种不确定性信号和三种广泛使用的后处理校准方法，并对校准指标与选择性预测性能进行联合分析。

Result: 研究揭示三项主要发现：(1) 即使强不确定性信号也可能校准不佳，在高置信度端分离度有限，限制了选择性预测的实际效果；(2) 标准后处理校准器通常能提升校准分数，但并不稳定地转化为更好的选择性预测行为；(3) 常用校准指标可能与选择性预测性能不一致，说明需要联合评估二者。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持一种面向决策的代码深度模型可靠性视角：在校准分数能够支撑“接受—延迟（accept–defer）”部署之前，必须与选择性预测质量及保留区域风险一同评估。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown significant promise in code-related tasks. While confidence calibration techniques have been proposed to improve model reliability, their effectiveness in real-world tasks with strict accuracy requirements remains uncertain. In this paper, we investigate two key questions: first, whether existing measurement metrics, including confidence scores and uncertainty metrics, are effective for guiding selective prediction; and second, whether existing calibration techniques can improve these metrics to better identify cases where model predictions are likely to succeed or fail.
  We conduct a systematic study on defect prediction and vulnerability detection using seven code models, evaluating thirteen uncertainty signals and three widely used post-hoc calibration methods. Our analysis reveals three major findings: (1) even strong uncertainty signals can remain poorly calibrated and provide limited separation at the high-confidence end, constraining selective prediction in practice; (2) standard post-hoc calibrators often improve calibration scores but do not consistently translate into better selective prediction behavior; and (3) commonly used calibration metrics can disagree with selective prediction performance, underscoring the need to evaluate both jointly. Moreover, our findings motivate a decision-oriented view of reliability in deep code models, where calibrated scores must be evaluated together with selective-prediction quality and retained-region risk before they can support accept--defer deployment.

</details>


### [16] [When CONTRIBUTING Files Fall Short: Revising OSS Contribution Guidelines with Repository Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21316)
*Kai Zheng,XinGuo Zhou,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 ContribAlign 框架，通过类别引导的证据整合自动修订开源项目的 CONTRIBUTING 文件，在分类覆盖诊断、冲突检测与生成质量上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源贡献指南常存在信息不完整或过时的问题；现有研究虽刻画了新手的信息需求与文档缺口，但修订指南需要从分散来源中恢复并验证项目特定知识，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 通过定性分析构建包含六大贡献类别、30 种细粒度指导类型的分类体系；用分类器诊断原始指南对六类的覆盖情况，再基于分类体系收集与组织项目特定证据，过滤检测到的冲突后，利用大语言模型生成类别级修订内容。

Result: 分类器加权 F1 达 0.764，较现有分类器提升 0.156；验证器检测事实冲突的精确率为 97.3%，估计召回率为 91.5%；人工评估显示 ContribAlign 在原始指南与三个基线中整体质量最高，在有用性、新手友好性和完整性上有显著提升。

Conclusion: ContribAlign 能够帮助维护更准确、更有用的贡献指南。

Abstract: Contribution guidelines help newcomers participate in open-source software projects, yet often contain incomplete or outdated information. Existing studies characterize newcomers' information needs and documentation gaps, but revising these guidelines requires recovering and validating project-specific knowledge from scattered sources. We present ContribAlign, a framework for automatically revising CONTRIBUTING files through category-guided evidence integration. A qualitative analysis yields a taxonomy of 30 fine-grained guidance types across six contribution categories. ContribAlign uses a classifier to diagnose coverage of the six categories in the original guidance, then uses the taxonomy to collect and organize project-specific evidence. It filters detected conflicts and uses a large language model to generate category-level revisions. The classifier achieves a weighted F1 score of 0.764, improving on the existing classifier by 0.156, while the verifier detects factual conflicts with 97.3% precision and 91.5% estimated recall. Human evaluation shows that ContribAlign achieves the highest overall quality among the original guidelines and three baselines, with significant improvements in usefulness, beginner-friendliness, and completeness. These results suggest that ContribAlign can help maintain more accurate and useful contribution guidelines.

</details>


### [17] [SWE-at-Scale: Scalable Issue Synthesis from Historical Bug Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21359)
*Silin Chen,Zihan Zhang,Xiaodong Gu,Jiale Huang,Yuling Shi,Han Li,Zhao Shan,Chengcheng Wan,Zhensu Sun,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWE-at-Scale 框架，通过逆向工程真实修复来自动合成逼真的软件工程任务，实现可扩展、多样且具挑战性的评测任务生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编码智能体能力增强，需要更广泛多样的可验证 SWE 任务用于稳健评测。但真实任务受限于问题报告的自然频率和人工标注的高成本；现有合成方法主要依赖大模型预训练知识与启发式提示，难以捕捉真实、复杂的软件故障机制。

Method: 核心思路是历史修复蕴含 bug 形成的深层机制知识，将修复反转即把"修复监督"转化为基于真实开发场景的"缺陷诱导监督"。框架通过逆向工程真实修复学习 bug 模式，并将其迁移到新的代码上下文中，反复采样多样的 bug 类型以构建可执行 SWE 任务。

Result: 实验表明，SWE-at-Scale 相比现有合成基线能生成更多样、更逼真的 SWE 任务，且对当前最先进编码智能体构成更大挑战。它可从单一代码上下文生成不同 bug 类型，bug 多样性即使每文件生成多达 200 次仍稳健扩展。

Conclusion: SWE-at-Scale 将有限真实修复转化为可复用监督信号，实现逼真、多样、具挑战性的 SWE 任务的可扩展合成。

Abstract: As coding agents become increasingly capable, robust evaluation requires a broader and more diverse supply of verifiable software engineering (SWE) tasks. However, real-world tasks are constrained by the natural frequency of issue reporting and the substantial cost of manual curation. Furthermore, existing synthetic approaches rely primarily on the pretrained knowledge of large models coupled with heuristic prompting, often failing to capture the authentic, complex mechanics of software failures. To exponentially scale SWE task production, we introduce SWE-at-Scale, a framework that automatically synthesizes realistic issues by reverse-engineering real-world software fixes. Our key insight is that historical fixes encapsulate deep mechanistic knowledge of bug formation: inverting a fix transforms repair supervision into bug-induction supervision grounded in actual development scenarios. SWE-at-Scale transfers these learned bug patterns to novel code contexts, repeatedly sampling diverse bug typologies to construct executable SWE tasks. Extensive experiments show that SWE-at-Scale generates more diverse and realistic SWE tasks than current synthesis baselines while posing greater challenges to state-of-the-art coding agents. Notably, SWE-at-Scale can generate distinct bug types from a single code context, with bug diversity scaling robustly even up to 200 generations per file. Overall, SWE-at-Scale turns finite real-world fixes into reusable supervision for scalable synthesis of realistic, diverse, and challenging SWE tasks.

</details>


### [18] [Agentic Dependency Transformations: Lessons from an Exploratory Case Study of Flask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21372)
*Kaya Kitada,Yoshiki Higo,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"依赖转换"（dependency transformation）这一新兴概念，即AI编码智能体改变仓库处理外部依赖方式的方法，并通过Flask探索性案例研究初步归纳出五条经验教训与五项研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件高度依赖第三方库，维护者需在复用收益与依赖带来的维护成本及风险之间权衡。现有研究多聚焦于依赖管理本身，缺乏对AI智能体主动改变依赖处理方式（如本地重实现）的系统考察，因此需要提出并探索"依赖转换"这一新方向。

Method: 作为emerging-results论文，作者以Flask为对象开展探索性案例研究，设计实验考察可用上下文如何影响智能体的依赖发现过程及其对依赖的迭代式转换。

Result: 案例研究归纳出五条初步经验教训，并据此提出包含五个部分的研究议程，阐明依赖转换作为新兴研究方向的定位。

Conclusion: 依赖转换是一个值得深入的新兴研究方向，其发展需要明确的转换契约、溯源约束、保持性要求以及独立验证准则。

Abstract: Modern software relies heavily on third-party libraries, requiring maintainers to balance the benefits of reuse against dependency-related maintenance and risk. We define dependency transformation as an emerging approach in which an AI coding agent alters how a repository handles external dependencies. Local reimplementation is one transformation strategy that recreates required functionality as local code; dependency transformation is broader and may include other strategies. In this emerging-results paper, we report an exploratory case study of Flask to explore dependency transformation strategies. We design experiments that show how available context shapes an agent's dependency discovery and iterative transformation of dependencies. The case yields five preliminary lessons. These lessons and the five-part research agenda position dependency transformation as an emerging research direction and motivate explicit transformation contracts, provenance constraints, preservation requirements, and independent validation criteria.

</details>


### [19] [The Role of Agentic Instruction Categories for Code Generation: A Study of Five Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21421)
*Warren Gerald Polandra,Yoshiki Higo,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估五类agent指令（仓库概览、函数描述、实现细节、测试、重构步骤）的全部31种非空组合，在Gemma 4与GPT-5.4两种LLM及五个真实Python库上对agent文件生成与代码生成的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级agent文件（如AGENTS.md）被广泛用于传递项目知识与开发者意图，但尚不清楚哪些类别的指令能高效生成agent文件并提升代码生成准确性。

Method: 设定五类指令（Repository Overview、Function Description、Implementation Detail、Testing、Reconstruction Steps），枚举全部31种非空组合；使用Gemma 4与GPT-5.4两个LLM，在5个人工编写的Python库上开展实验，围绕agent文件生成与代码生成两个研究问题（共五个子问题）进行评测。

Result: agent文件生成的token用量不由指令数量决定，且在不同模型与库间差异显著；多数AI生成库可成功编译，但功能正确性有限——Gemma 4无任何变体通过全部测试，GPT-5.4有12.99%变体全通过。Function Description使Gemma 4部分通过率最高，Reconstruction Steps使GPT-5.4全通过率最高；在结构相似性上，Reconstruction Steps最常对应与原库在Lines of File和总Cyclomatic Complexity上最接近的结果，Testing与Reconstruction Steps在File Count和Function Count上共同出现频率最高。

Conclusion: 指令数量并非效率或正确性的决定因素，指令类别与模型协同影响生成效果；不同模型的最优指令类别不同，结构相似性与功能正确性的最优指令组合也存在差异。

Abstract: Agentic coding systems increasingly rely on repository-level agent files, such as AGENTS.md, to communicate project knowledge and developer intent. However, it remains unclear which categories of agent instructions enable efficient agent-file generation and accurate code generation. We evaluate five instruction categories: Repository Overview, Function Description, Implementation Detail, Testing, and Reconstruction Steps, across all 31 non-empty combinations using two Large Language Models (LLMs), Gemma 4 and GPT-5.4, and five human-written Python libraries. Our study addresses two research questions, comprising five sub-questions, on agent file generation and code generation. Results show that the number of instructions alone does not determine token usage for agent-file generation, with substantial variation across models and libraries. Although most AI-generated libraries compiled successfully regardless of the instruction combination, functional correctness remained limited. No Gemma 4 variant passed all tests, whereas 12.99% of GPT-5.4 variants did. Function Description achieved Gemma 4's highest partial-pass rate, while Reconstruction Steps achieved GPT-5.4's highest full-pass rate. For structural similarity to human-written code, Reconstruction Steps was most frequently associated with libraries closest to the originals in Lines of File and total Cyclomatic Complexity, while Testing and Reconstruction Steps were jointly most frequent for File Count and Function Count.

</details>


### [20] [Poison Valleys: Diagnosing Path-Dependent Failure in LLM Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21514)
*Veljko Skarich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过合成案例追踪代码修复智能体失败的上游根源，发现将错误概念从假设移到实施计划会显著提高失败率，且不同错误概念产生可区分的义务违反模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码修复智能体失败时，最终测试只能表明补丁错误，却无法指出是哪一步早期承诺导致错误或哪个软件义务失败，缺乏从上游错误概念到具体失败形式的可测量关联。

Method: 在两个合成Python案例（PersistQueue与Dijkstra）上进行1,844次模型请求；定义貌似合理但错误的软件机制，将其注入模型的假设、实施计划或两者之中；用Oracle和针对性诊断坐标评估生成代码。

Result: 在匹配实验配比后，将错误概念从假设移到计划使PersistQueue失败率增加32.2%、Dijkstra增加28.9%；不同错误概念产生不同且依赖模型的义务违反模式，但也可能收敛到相同行为。

Conclusion: 建立了从上游错误概念到代码机制再到失败形式的可测量联系，表明错误概念所处阶段影响失败概率，且失败模式具有一定的可区分性。

Abstract: When code-repair agents fail, final tests reveal that a patch is wrong but not which earlier commitment caused it or which software obligation failed. Poison Valleys traces this path in 1,844 model requests on two synthetic Python cases: PersistQueue adds atomic batch claiming to a disk-backed queue, while Dijkstra adds source-sensitive filtering to shortest-path search. We define plausible but incorrect software mechanisms, place each in a model's hypothesis, implementation plan, or both, and evaluate the generated code with an Oracle and targeted diagnostic coordinates. Moving the misconception from hypothesis to plan increased failure by 32.2% in PersistQueue and 28.9% in Dijkstra after matching the experimental mix. Different misconceptions produced distinct, model-dependent patterns of violated obligations, although several could converge on the same behavior. This creates a measured connection from upstream misconception to code mechanism and failure form. Code, data, and demo: https://poisonvalleys.org.

</details>


### [21] [Realization Divergence in Multi-Provider Quantum Computing: A Pre-Registered Study of What Survives Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21714)
*Jouni Peltonen,Vlad Stirbu,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"实现分歧"(realization divergence)概念,度量同一量子源电路在不同量子软件栈下被实现与运行时的可观测差异,并通过跨三家供应商、四台设备的硬件实验进行系统研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序在运行前需经过编译器、转译器与厂商服务,但"所写"与"所跑"之间的距离很少被量化,缺乏跨供应商、跨设备的可比性与软件质量度量。

Method: 在三个供应商、四台设备上开展硬件研究,采用预注册预测与明确标注的探索性分析及修正相结合的方式。编译实验设定指定放置策略,考察嵌入半径与电路规模同双比特开销的相关性;在固定最低索引策略下,比较8比特与12比特Bernstein-Vazirani电路在IQM Emerald与Garnet上的执行分歧;以全连接IonQ作为对照;在Rigetti Cepheus上开展拓扑感知的原生放置后续实验;并进行跨设备代的接口可观测性比较。

Result: 编译实验中嵌入半径与双比特开销的Spearman相关系数为0.805,显著高于电路规模的0.492。固定最低索引策略下,8比特Bernstein-Vazirani电路在IQM Emerald上的执行分歧大于12比特电路,而在IQM Garnet上次序相反,两项比较在两次重复中均可复现且置信区间不重叠。该次序反转在全连接的IonQ对照上不存在。Rigetti Cepheus的拓扑感知原生放置后续实验未发现任一提交路径在正确答案概率上有一致优势,但也未确立两条路径的等价性。Rigetti的支持操作列表在更换原生纠缠器的设备代际间保持不变。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种依赖放置的解释,但未分离出单个硬件噪声机制;结构性比较依赖度量权重、放置方式与设备代际。供应商接口限制可观测性,论文将组合工具链中的静默接口失效进行归类,并给出工具链边界处的检查方法,把有效的跨供应商比较与具体软件质量实践相连接。

Abstract: Quantum programs pass through compilers, transpilers and vendor services before execution. However, the distance between what was written and what runs is rarely measured. We call this distance realization divergence: the observable difference in how an identical source circuit is realized and behaves across quantum software stacks. We report a hardware study across three vendors and four devices, combining pre-registered predictions with explicitly identified exploratory analyses and amendments. In a compilation experiment with assigned placement policies, embedding radius correlates with two-qubit overhead at Spearman 0.805, against 0.492 for circuit size. Under a fixed lowest-index policy, an eight-qubit Bernstein-Vazirani circuit exhibits greater execution divergence than a twelve-qubit circuit on IQM Emerald. The opposite ordering occurs on IQM Garnet. Both comparisons replicate across two repetitions with disjoint confidence intervals. These results support a placement-dependent explanation, without isolating individual hardware noise mechanisms. The same ordering inversion is absent on the all-to-all IonQ control. On Rigetti Cepheus, a follow-up using topology-aware native placement found no consistent advantage in correct-answer probability for either submission track, but did not establish equivalence between the tracks. Structural comparisons depend on metric weighting, placement and device generation. Provider interfaces also limit observability: Rigetti's supported-operation list remained unchanged across a device generation that replaced the native entangler. We classify silent interface failures in composed toolchains and identify checks at toolchain boundaries, connecting valid cross-provider comparisons with concrete software-quality practices.

</details>


### [22] [CodeHealth Predicts the Token Cost of Agentic Code Review: A Pre-Registered Study of 465 Agent-Authored Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21914)
*Lo Gullstrand Heander,Markus Borg,Emma Söderberg,Christofer Rydenfält*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 代码健康度（CodeHealth）越高，多智能体 LLM 评审与修复的 token 成本越低（每点降低 3.5%–6.1%），且该效应不由 PR 规模驱动，可归因于文件规模的调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织日益将代码评审委托给按 token 计费的多智能体 LLM 流水线，低代码质量会增加人工维护成本，但其对智能体评审成本的影响尚未被量化。作者旨在检验基线 CodeHealth 是否能在 PR 足迹之外预测智能体评审与修复的 token 开销。

Method: 预注册观察性研究：从 AIDev 的 246 个仓库中抽样 465 个智能体编写的 PR，按 CodeHealth 与足迹联合分层并施加仓库上限。使用固定的五智能体评审-修复流水线，以三种不同 LLM 处理全部 PR。对每个 LLM 拟合混合模型：log(token) 对 CodeHealth 与 log(PR 足迹) 回归，并设仓库随机截距。

Result: 三种 LLM 的 CodeHealth 系数均为负，95% CI 均排除零；每提升一个 CodeHealth 点对应 3.5%–6.1% 的 token 成本下降。CodeHealth 与 PR 足迹无关，故 PR 规模不能解释该关联。三种注册气味中两种可预测修复成本；批量修复后 CodeHealth 中位数不变。探索性分析将关联定位于评审阶段：智能体在不健康代码上迭代更多，但并未写出更多评论。控制被评分文件规模后关联被吸收，作者将其解释为文件规模的中介效应。

Conclusion: 一个针对人类可维护性校准的质量指标同样能预测多智能体评审与修复的推理成本。单点效应温和且不由 PR 规模承载；因此重构的收益在智能体评审的代码上依然成立，但前提是重构改变结构与文件规模，而不仅改变评分。

Abstract: Context: Organizations increasingly delegate code review to multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipelines billed per token. Low code quality raises human maintenance effort, but its effect on agent review cost has not been measured.
  Objective: We test whether baseline CodeHealth predicts the token cost of agentic review and fix beyond pull request (PR) footprint.
  Method: In a pre-registered observational study, we sampled 465 agent-authored PRs from 246 repositories in AIDev, jointly stratified by CodeHealth and footprint under repository caps. A frozen five-agent review-and-fix pipeline processed every PR with three different LLMs. Per LLM, we fitted a mixed model of log tokens on CodeHealth and log PR footprint, with a repository random intercept.
  Results: For all three LLMs the CodeHealth coefficient was negative, with 95% CI excluding zero; each CodeHealth point was associated with 3.5% to 6.1% lower token cost. CodeHealth was unrelated to PR footprint, so PR size does not explain the association. Two of three registered smells predicted fix cost; batch fixing left median CodeHealth unchanged. Exploratory analyses located the association in the review stage, where agents iterated more on unhealthy code without writing more comments. Adjusting for the size of the scored files absorbed the association, which we interpret as mediation by file size.
  Conclusions: A quality metric calibrated against human maintainability also predicts the inference cost of multi-agent review and fix. The effect is modest per point and not carried by PR size, so the refactoring case extends to agent-reviewed code when it changes structure and file size, not only the score.
  Data Availability: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22127920

</details>


### [23] [Benchmarking and Enhancing Design-Aware Code Review in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21925)
*Zhenhao Zhou,Kai Yu,Ying Wang,Zhiqiang Yuan,Junhao Zeng,Yijian Wu,Chong Wang,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出设计感知代码评审(design-aware code review)问题，并构建DesignLore方法与DesignReviewBench基准，通过挖掘历史评审讨论中的设计知识来指导代码评审，在权衡检测任务上F1由0.213提升至0.259（相对提升21.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟软件系统中的代码评审不仅需要判断局部正确性，还要求评审者识别变更带来的设计权衡、比较替代方案，并判断其与项目特定约束的兼容性。然而现有自动化评审方法主要聚焦缺陷与正确性，沉淀在历史评审讨论中的设计知识难以复用，导致设计感知评审缺乏系统化支持。

Method: 提出DesignLore知识增强方法：从历史评审讨论中抽取设计决策，将其组织为自适应设计分类体系(adaptive design taxonomy)，并复用相关历史知识来指导代码评审。同时构建DesignReviewBench基准，该基准源自真实补丁—评审历史记录，用于系统性评估设计感知代码评审能力。

Result: 评估表明设计感知评审对当前编码智能体仍具挑战性，最强基线在设计权衡检测上的F1仅为0.213；DesignLore达到F1 0.259，相对提升21.6%，并在不同智能体系统与骨干模型上均取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了设计感知评审的重要性，并证明恢复与复用历史设计知识以支撑该任务具有可行性和潜力。

Abstract: Code review often requires reasoning beyond local correctness. In mature software systems, reviewers must recognize design trade-offs introduced by a proposed change, compare alternative solutions, and reason about their compatibility with project-specific constraints. We refer to this problem as design-aware code review. However, existing automated review approaches primarily focus on defects and correctness, while the design knowledge accumulated in historical review discussions remains difficult to reuse.
  To address these challenges, we present DesignLore, a knowledge-augmented approach that extracts design decisions from historical review discussions, organizes them into an adaptive design taxonomy, and reuses relevant historical knowledge to guide code review. We further construct DesignReviewBench, a benchmark derived from real patch-review histories for systematically evaluating design-aware code review. Our evaluation shows that design-aware review remains challenging for current coding agents, with the strongest baseline achieving an F1 of only 0.213 on design trade-off detection. In comparison, DesignLore achieves an F1 of 0.259, a 21.6% relative improvement, and consistently delivers gains across different agent systems and backbone. These results highlight the importance of design-aware review and demonstrate the potential of recovering and reusing historical design knowledge to support it.

</details>


### [24] [Stateful Testing for Deep Learning Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22003)
*Jiatao Ge,Ruixiang Qian,Yibo Hu,Yanzhou Mu,Yinglong Zou,Jiawei Liu,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rondo，一种针对深度学习编译器状态性缺陷的有状态测试方法。它将测试用例建模为"模型+调用序列"对，利用 MCMC 采样变异调用序列，并通过三重差分预言机区分无编译执行、状态重置的编译执行与保留状态的编译执行。在四种深度学习框架、24 种模型架构上，Rondo 检测到 117 个缺陷（含 35 个状态性缺陷），提交 39 份报告，开发者确认 35 份并修复 18 个缺陷，报警精度达 99.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习编译器采用惰性编译并在多次调用间缓存编译产物，保留的编译器状态可能导致后续调用出错，即使单独看该调用是正确的。对 PyTorch、JAX、TensorFlow 中 26 个公开报告的缺陷分析表明，检测此类缺陷需要"先建立编译器状态、再触发失败"的调用序列。而现有测试方法多聚焦于单次调用，难以暴露这类状态性缺陷。

Method: Rondo 将有状态测试用例表示为"模型+调用序列"对。它通过变异调用序列，并使用由编译行为引导的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器，偏向于探索罕见观测到的编译行为。其核心是三重差分预言机，比较三种执行方式：不做编译的执行、编译执行但重置编译器状态、编译执行且保留编译器状态。对确认的状态性缺陷，Rondo 在保持输入与程序状态不变的前提下，分别以保留状态和重置状态重放同一调用进行确认，随后在保留原始缺陷的条件下缩短调用序列。

Result: 在四种深度学习框架、24 种常见模型架构上的评估中，Rondo 共检测到 117 个缺陷，其中 35 个为状态性缺陷。研究者提交了 39 份缺陷报告（含 18 份状态性缺陷），开发者确认了 35 份（含 16 份状态性缺陷），并修复了 18 个缺陷（含 11 个状态性缺陷）。与对比方法相比，Rondo 取得最高的行为覆盖率和编译器 Python 源码行覆盖率，报警精度达 99.26%。此外，Rondo 缩短了 117 条调用序列中的 44 条，这些序列的平均长度减少 83.34%。

Conclusion: Rondo 有效揭示了深度学习编译器中因跨调用保留状态而引发的状态性缺陷，其 MCMC 引导的序列变异与三重差分预言机在缺陷检测、覆盖率和报警精度上均表现优异，并能自动缩短触发序列。该方法已在多种主流框架和模型上验证了实用性，发现的缺陷获得开发者广泛确认与修复，表明有状态测试是提升深度学习编译器可靠性的重要方向。

Abstract: Modern deep learning compilers compile models lazily and cache artifacts across invocations. Retained compiler state can make a later invocation incorrect even when it is correct in isolation. An analysis of 26 publicly reported issues in PyTorch, JAX, and TensorFlow shows that detecting such bugs requires sequences that first establish compiler state and then expose a failure. We present Rondo, a stateful testing method that represents each test case as a model paired with an invocation sequence. Rondo mutates invocation sequences and uses a Markov chain Monte Carlo sampler guided by compilation behavior to favor rarely observed behaviors. Its three-way differential oracle compares execution without compilation, compiled execution with compiler state reset, and compiled execution with state retained. It confirms stateful bugs by replaying the same invocation with state retained and reset while preserving inputs and program state, then shortens sequences while preserving the original bug. We evaluate Rondo on four deep learning frameworks using 24 common model architectures. Rondo detects 117 bugs, including 35 stateful bugs. We submit 39 bug reports, including 18 for stateful bugs; developers confirm 35 reports, including 16 for stateful bugs, and fix 18 bugs, including 11 stateful bugs. Rondo achieves the highest behavior coverage and compiler Python source-line coverage among the compared methods, with 99.26% alarm precision. It shortens 44 of 117 invocation sequences, reducing the lengths of these 44 sequences by 83.34% on average.

</details>


### [25] [WEAVE: Weaving Real-World Code Evolution into Dependency-Structured Tasks for Benchmarking and Training Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22039)
*Weihao Li,Zihang Xu,Zhongqiu Wang,Songwei Yu,Wenqiang Zhu,Yuechen Luo,Jingxuan Xu,Yanan Wu,Haoyang Huang,Ken Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出WEAVE框架，将已验证的PR历史转化为可执行、依赖结构化的多PR任务，并引入Graph Credit度量以支持细粒度进度评估和强化学习奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实软件工程中的变更（功能、迁移、系统性修复）往往跨越多个PR，需在仓库层面协同修改。但现有可执行仓库级基准多以单一issue或PR为任务单元，多PR设置又依赖粗粒度聚合和结构无关评估，掩盖了变更间依赖关系，限制了长周期代码智能体的进度度量与学习信号。

Method: WEAVE利用仓库校准的类型化变更位点（typed change loci），将相关PR组织为有界DAG，并将每个DAG实例化为统一的可执行任务。同时提出Graph Credit——一种确定性、基于测试的度量，衡量依赖一致的部分进度，并支持稠密、可验证的强化学习奖励。

Result: 在任务构建、智能体评估、监督微调和强化学习等实验中，WEAVE能够生成连贯的可执行任务，相比扁平化评分实现更忠实反映依赖结构的进度度量，并为代码智能体训练提供有效监督和可验证奖励。

Conclusion: WEAVE通过将多PR历史结构化为依赖DAG并提供图级进度度量，弥补了单PR基准与真实多PR开发之间的差距，为长周期代码智能体的评估与训练提供了更合理、可验证的框架。

Abstract: Real-world software engineering rarely consists of isolated changes. Features, migrations, and systematic fixes often unfold across multiple pull requests (PRs), requiring coordinated modifications across a repository. Yet most executable repository-level benchmarks retain a single issue or PR as the task unit, while existing multi-PR settings rely on coarse aggregation and structure-agnostic evaluation. This mismatch obscures dependencies among changes and limits progress measurement and learning signals for long-horizon code agents. We introduce WEAVE, a framework that transforms verified PR histories into executable, dependency-structured tasks. WEAVE leverages repository-calibrated typed change loci to organize related PRs into bounded DAGs and instantiate each as a unified executable task. We further introduce Graph Credit, a deterministic, test-grounded metric that measures dependency-consistent partial progress and supports dense, verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning. Experiments on task construction, agent evaluation, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning show that WEAVE yields coherent executable tasks, enables more faithful progress measurement than flat scoring, and provides effective supervision and verifiable rewards for training code agents.

</details>


### [26] [Are We Testing the Wrong Quantum Bugs? A Study of Fault Representation Gaps in Quantum Software Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22089)
*Asif Mohammed Saad,Ahsanul Ameen Sabit,Jonah Brandenburgh,Jiyuan Wang,Qian Zhang,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文审计了4个量子变异测试工具和3个精选缺陷基准，并从10个量子软件仓库收集410个真实缺陷，构建56个缺陷族分类法进行比较。结果显示变异工具仅覆盖20个缺陷族，精选基准额外覆盖15个，而86.3%的真实缺陷属于两者均未覆盖的缺陷族，主要涉及编译器变换、类型处理和输入验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件依赖编译器、中间表示和执行框架，这些组件的故障可能改变原本正确的量子程序行为。然而当前量子测试技术多采用变异生成的故障和精选缺陷基准进行评估，二者是否能反映现代量子软件中真实遇到的故障尚不清楚，因此需要实证审计其代表性。

Method: 审计4个量子变异测试工具与3个精选缺陷基准，并从10个量子软件仓库收集410个经核实的真实缺陷；将三个来源的故障统一归入包含56个缺陷族的分类法中，比较变异算子、精选基准与近期真实缺陷之间的覆盖情况。

Result: 变异工具支持20个缺陷族，精选基准额外增加15个，但410个真实缺陷中仅56个（13.7%）属于二者覆盖的缺陷族，其余354个（86.3%）属于两者均缺失的缺陷族；最大的未覆盖族为编译器变换（66个缺陷）、类型处理（53个）和输入验证（47个）。

Conclusion: 现有变异算子主要修改电路和程序结构，而许多真实缺陷源于处理它们的编译器、表示和框架接口。弥合这一差距需要超越电路级故障，转向测试编译器变换中的语义保持、表示边界的正确性以及软件组件间的交互；本研究为构建能捕捉这些机制并保留复现所需上下文的缺陷模型与基准提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Quantum software relies on compilers, intermediate representations, and execution frameworks that transform programs before execution. Faults in these components can change the behavior of otherwise correct quantum programs. Yet quantum testing techniques are often evaluated using mutation-generated faults and curated bug benchmarks. We investigate how well the faults represented by these artifacts reflect those encountered in modern quantum software. We audit four quantum mutation-testing tools and three curated bug benchmarks, and collect 410 verified bugs from ten quantum software repositories over two years. We classify faults from all three sources into a common taxonomy of 56 bug families and compare their representation across mutation operators, curated benchmarks, and recent real-world bugs. The mutation tools support 20 bug families, and curated benchmarks add 15 more. Yet only 56 of the 410 real-world bugs (13.7%) belong to families represented by either source; the remaining 354 (86.3%) belong to families absent from both. The largest unrepresented families concern compiler transformations (66 bugs), type handling (53), and input validation (47). Unlike local gate or statement changes, these faults often depend on intermediate representations, compiler pipelines, input types, or dependency versions. Existing mutation operators primarily modify circuits and program constructs, whereas many real-world faults arise in the compilers, representations, and framework interfaces that process them. Addressing this gap requires looking beyond circuit-level faults toward testing semantic preservation across compiler transformations, correctness at representation boundaries, and interactions between software components. Our study provides empirical evidence to guide fault models and benchmarks that capture these mechanisms and preserve the contexts needed to reproduce them.

</details>
