Such accuracies are usually read out of the model's text by an answer extractor. A model that learns to write its answer where the extractor looks can therefore score higher without answering better. We measure how much of the gain reported by extractors copied from public code arises this way. We generate outputs from a base model and its RL model and score them twice: with those extractors, and with one extractor of our own that reads a stated answer whether it is in the answer box \boxed{} or in the text. On four released 7B SimpleRL base-RL pairs, 82% of the out-of-domain multiple-choice gain that these extractors report disappears under ours: it is a gain in answer format, not in answers. The base models already state most of their answers in text, and RL moves them into the box, where these extractors look first. Independent LLM readers, in place of our extractor, put the figure at 77%. With a prompt that itself asks for the box, our extractor puts it at 79%. On a 32B pair trained with another recipe, almost none of the reported gain is format gain: its base model boxes its answers when asked, and its RL model rarely does when not. A reported gain is partly a property of the extractor that read it, and re-scoring stored outputs with a second extractor shows how much, without re-running RL.

</details>


### [163] [KNOU-Flood: Advancing global streamflow forecasting with lead-aware probabilistic machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20640)
*Minjong Cheon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KNOU-Flood在AIFL开放配方基础上，保持单层LSTM循环核心与两阶段迁移学习不变，仅改变"信息组织方式"（更长输入窗口、lead-aware预报输入、加入GraphCast、以及fair CRPS训练的隐噪声集成），在2099个测试流域上显著超越AIFL复现基线并给出概率预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球径流预报对防洪至关重要，但基于再分析训练的数据驱动模型在由业务预报驱动时会损失技巧，且随预见期延长损失加剧。AIFL虽用两阶段迁移学习与确定性LSTM应对这一分布偏移，但存在两个缺陷：一是把单一预见期的预报喂入整个输入窗口（输入协议不当），二是未提供不确定性量化。作者希望探究在不改动其循环核心的前提下，该开放配方能被扩展到什么程度。

Method: 保留AIFL的单层LSTM与两阶段训练，仅重构其周围的信息：(1) 更长输入窗口（424天）；(2) lead-aware预报输入（按预见期匹配输入）；(3) 在ECMWF HRES之外引入GraphCast作为第二预报源，并保留原始通道；(4) 用fair CRPS训练的隐噪声（latent-noise）集成头实现概率预报。在与AIFL复现基线完全一致的数据、站点、划分和指标下对比，并做消融归因。

Result: 三个种子、2099个测试流域（2021–2023）：KNOU-Flood在LT1/LT10的中位KGE'为0.65/0.48，基线为0.62/0.34；在全部十个预见期、两种评分协议下均显著占优，也优于1024单元基线，并在留出流域上成立。相同权重下，仅因预报喂入方式不同，LT10的KGE'可为0.18或0.34，说明AIFL报告的多数预见期衰减是输入协议效应。增益贡献排序：概率头最大，其次GraphCast原始通道、更长窗口与混合微调。集成CRPS低于基线的概率版本，配合推理时dropout接近校准；对1.5年与2年一遇洪水检测优于所有基线，更稀有洪水的收益依种子而定，尾部加权变体改善了10年及以上事件。

Conclusion: KNOU-Flood是对开放配方的受控扩展，而非新架构；其核心洞见在于预见期技巧衰减很大程度源于输入协议而非循环结构本身，同时通过概率头与多源预报输入实现了更优的确定性与概率性能。

Abstract: Global streamflow forecasting supports flood preparedness, yet data-driven models trained on reanalysis lose skill when driven by operational forecasts, increasingly so with lead time. AIFL addresses this shift with a two-stage transfer-learning recipe and a deterministic LSTM, but it feeds one forecast lead time into the whole input window and gives no uncertainty. We ask how far this open recipe can be extended without changing its recurrent core. KNOU-Flood keeps a single-layer LSTM and the two-stage training and changes the information around it: a longer input window (424 days), lead-aware forecast inputs, GraphCast as a second forecast source next to ECMWF HRES, and a latent-noise ensemble trained with the fair CRPS. We compare against a re-implementation of the AIFL recipe (its code is not public) under identical data, stations, splits and metrics. Our contributions are threefold. First, over three seeds and 2,099 test basins (2021-2023), KNOU-Flood reaches a median KGE' of 0.65 at LT1 and 0.48 at LT10 against 0.62 and 0.34, with significant gains at all ten lead times under two scoring protocols, also against a 1024-unit baseline and on held-out basins (one seed each). Second, identical weights give an LT10 KGE' of 0.18 or 0.34 depending on how forecasts are fed, so much of the lead-time decay reported for AIFL is an input-protocol effect; the probabilistic head contributes most to the gain, followed by the raw GraphCast channels, the longer window and mixed fine-tuning. Third, the ensembles reach lower CRPS than probabilistic versions of the baseline, are nearly calibrated with inference-time dropout, and detect 1.5- and 2-year floods better than all baselines; the benefit for rarer floods depends on the seed, and an exploratory tail-weighted variant improves 10-year and larger events. KNOU-Flood is a controlled extension of an open recipe, not a new architecture.

</details>


### [164] [Spectral Indistinguishability and Learning-Curve Identifiability in Quadratic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20678)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究中心化二次网络的谱摘要与标量学习曲线，构造具有精确高斯一、二阶边缘的解析正输入密度，使完全四阶矩算子经正交映射纠缠、锚定谱测度一致，但因子化损失曲线因矩阵乘法不保形而出现差异；并给出对教师识别的样本复杂度、最优输入样本指数与精确全局恢复界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清"谱摘要/标量学习曲线"究竟能揭示中心化二次网络的哪些信息：谱测度一致是否意味着学习行为一致？标量损失曲线在何种条件下能识别初始预测器、教师谱与活跃维度？为此需要构造可控而又能产生差异的解析实例，并给出信息论与算法的样本复杂度刻画。

Method: 构造解析正输入密度，使其一、二阶边缘恰为高斯；分析四阶矩算子在对角教师代数上的正交纠缠；对带非零循环不变量的共同教师比较因子化损失曲线；以循环长度r构造O(ε^r)对比与ε^{-2r}、ε^{-2}的查询下界；提出等于配对梯度差的共享输入分数；构造发散辅助教师尺度在耦合1/log L下产生有限极限对比；对高斯输入与有理独立教师谱用精确标量损失曲线做识别；并在固定维度、不同无序教师和下给出维度最小的精确观测用于联合局部恢复，配合精确证书与全权重重放、数值逆检验。

Result: 锚定谱测度可完全一致而损失曲线仍不同，差异源于正交映射不保持矩阵乘法；循环长度r可任意大，对每个固定构造给出时间上一致的O(ε^r)对比；给定一对教师，含噪标量损失查询需ε^{-2r}量级、带标量梯度读数的有界初始化干预需ε^{-2}量级；共享输入分数达到最优输入样本指数且无需知道教师；辅助教师尺度族在耦合1/log L下具有非零有限极限对比；有理独立谱下，一条精确标量损失曲线可把初始预测器识别到符号共轭；在该类中、各坐标对角逆状态误差非零并校准时间与损失尺度时，还能识别未知教师谱与活跃维度（模同时旋转）；固定维度下不同无序教师和可用维度最小精确观测实现泛型联合局部恢复；已知教师时有有限精确全局界，活跃类在盲维度上限下亦然；精确证书与全权重重放验证构造，数值逆检验显示严重病态。

Conclusion: 谱摘要的相等并不蕴含学习曲线相同，标量损失曲线的可识别性依赖于教师谱的有理独立性等条件；识别代价随循环结构指数增长，而对干预类查询只需多项式代价，共享输入分数无需教师即可达最优样本指数；精确恢复在大循环/病态情形下极为困难，数值逆检验暴露严重条件数问题。

Abstract: We study what spectral summaries and scalar learning curves reveal about centered quadratic networks. We construct analytic positive input densities with exactly Gaussian one- and two-coordinate marginals. Their complete fourth-moment operators are intertwined by an orthogonal map fixing the entire diagonal teacher algebra, so the corresponding anchored spectral measures agree. For selected common teachers with a nonzero cycle invariant, their factorized loss curves differ because the map does not preserve matrix multiplication. Cycle length $r$, which can be arbitrarily large, gives $O(\varepsilon^r)$ contrast uniformly in training time for each fixed construction. For a supplied pair, noisy scalar loss queries require order $\varepsilon^{-2r}$ observations, while bounded initialization interventions with scalar gradient readouts require order $\varepsilon^{-2}$. A shared-input score, equal to a paired-gradient difference, attains the optimal input-sample exponent without knowing the teacher. An explicit family of diverging auxiliary teacher scales instead admits a nonzero finite limiting contrast at coupling $1/\log L$. Conversely, for Gaussian inputs and a rationally independent teacher spectrum, one exact scalar loss curve identifies the initial predictor up to sign conjugation. Within this rationally independent class, with nonzero diagonal inverse-state error in every coordinate and calibrated time and loss scales, it also identifies the unknown teacher spectrum and active dimension, modulo simultaneous rotations. For fixed dimension and distinct unordered teacher sums, dimension-minimal exact observations give generic joint local recovery. Finite exact global bounds hold for a known teacher and, on the active class, under a blind dimension cap. Exact certificates and full-weight replay verify the construction; numerical inverse checks reveal severe ill-conditioning.

</details>


### [165] [Diminishing Returns of Data in Sparse Autoencoders: Functional Behavior does not Guarantee Feature Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20680)
*Sambit Ghosh,Madelyn Mathai,R. Venkatesh Babu,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏自编码器（SAE）训练所需的最小激活数据量。通过在3个语言模型上训练4种SAE架构、预算从5M到500M token，发现功能收敛与特征可复现性对数据需求不同，且数据选择策略影响不同目标，并给出理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE通常需在数亿至数十亿激活token上训练，但这一规模是否必要尚缺乏研究。作者希望探究SAE所需激活数据量，以降低研究成本并提升可靠性。

Method: 在3个语言模型上，用5M至500M token的预算训练4种SAE架构。测量两类收敛：标准评测上的功能行为，以及学习特征的一对一可复现性。比较随机采样与多样性采样的影响，并进行理论分析，区分子采样训练的统计一致性与一对一特征恢复所需的更强可识别性和优化条件。

Result: 功能数据需求高度依赖指标：重建通常在较小预算下即可接近全量参考，而特征可复现性随数据增加持续提升。以Gemma-2-2B BatchTopK为例，严格匹配特征比例从5M token的2%升至50M的29%。随机采样更好保留原始激活分布并利于重建，多样性采样常提升AutoInterp。

Conclusion: 当预算与目标匹配时，SAE训练可更高效；特征层面的结论应由显式可复现性检查支撑，从而推动更廉价、更可靠的SAE研究。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are typically trained on hundreds of millions to billions of activation tokens, yet little work has tested whether this scale is necessary. We study how much activation data SAEs need by training four SAE architectures on three language models with budgets ranging from 5M tokens to a 500M-token reference. We measure two notions of convergence: functional behavior on standard evaluations and one-to-one reproducibility of learned features. Functional data requirements are strongly metric-dependent: reconstruction often approaches the full-data reference at substantially smaller budgets, whereas feature reproducibility continues to improve with data. For Gemma-2-2B BatchTopK, the fraction of strictly matched features rises from 2\% at 5M tokens to 29\% at 50M. Data selection also matters: random sampling better preserves the original activation distribution and generally favors reconstruction, whereas diversity-based sampling frequently improves AutoInterp. A theoretical analysis separates statistical consistency of subsampled training from the stronger identifiability and optimization conditions required for one-to-one feature recovery. Our results show that SAE training can often be made far more data-efficient when budgets are matched to the goal, and feature-level claims are backed by explicit reproducibility checks, paving the way toward cheaper and more reliable SAE research.

</details>


### [166] [TurboMamba: Faster Inference, Faster Training, and Better Prediction with Lexical Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20682)
*Hanxin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TurboMamba 是一种混合语言模型架构，通过全局 Mamba-3 与局部小 Transformer 的协同，结合三项轻量改进，在预测损失、训练吞吐和推理延迟上全面优于 Transformer、Mamba-3、Jamba 与 Zamba 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型架构难以同时兼顾快速训练、快速推理与高质量 token 预测。纯 Transformer 推理开销大，纯 Mamba 类模型局部预测能力有限，混合架构（如 Jamba、Zamba）虽有改进但仍存在效率与精度的权衡。作者希望设计一种同时在这三方面取得优势的架构。

Method: 提出 TurboMamba：每完成一个 token 块时运行一次全局 Mamba-3 模型；一个小型 Transformer 利用该全局上下文及块内更早的 token 预测下一个 token。三项轻量改进增强局部预测：直接访问前一块的最后 token、对下一 token 分数进行小幅修正、与近期 token 频率进行学习式混合。在公开 FineWeb-Edu 上对比 12 种小规模实现，参数量相当并共享训练预算上限，基线包括 Transformer、Mamba-3、Jamba 和 Zamba（后两者以 Mamba-3 替换原 Mamba 模块）。

Result: TurboMamba 在 12 个模型中取得最低平均测试预测损失（NLL）：比 Mamba-3 低 9.4%，比 Jamba 版本低 7.3%，比 Zamba 版本低 18.3%。端到端训练吞吐为 Mamba-3 的 2.60 倍、Jamba 版本的 2.81 倍、Zamba 版本的 4.82 倍。在批量 32 条序列、每条 256 token 提示 + 128 生成 token 的端到端批延迟测试中，分别比 Mamba-3、Jamba 版本、Zamba 版本快 3.12 倍、3.53 倍、6.21 倍。

Conclusion: TurboMamba 通过全局 Mamba-3 与小 Transformer 的层级协同及三项轻量局部预测改进，在小规模对照实验中同时实现了更低的预测损失、更高的训练吞吐与更低的推理延迟，验证了该混合架构在效率与精度上的综合优势。

Abstract: We introduce TurboMamba, a language-model architecture for faster inference, faster training, and better token prediction. A global Mamba-3 model runs once per completed token block. A small Transformer uses this global context and earlier tokens within the block to predict the next token. Three lightweight additions improve local predictions: direct access to the previous block's last token, a small correction to next-token scores, and a learned mixture with recent-token frequencies. We compare twelve small-scale implementations on public FineWeb-Edu, with comparable parameter counts and shared training-budget caps. The baselines include Transformer, Mamba-3, Jamba, and Zamba. The Jamba and Zamba versions use Mamba-3 in place of their original Mamba modules. TurboMamba achieves the lowest mean test prediction loss (negative log-likelihood, NLL) among all twelve models. Its mean NLL is 9.4% lower than that of Mamba-3, 7.3% lower than that of the Jamba version, and 18.3% lower than that of the Zamba version. End-to-end training throughput is 2.60x that of Mamba-3, 2.81x that of the Jamba version, and 4.82x that of the Zamba version. We measure end-to-end batch latency for a batch of 32 sequences, each with a 256-token prompt and 128 generated tokens. On this workload, TurboMamba achieves a 3.12x speedup over Mamba-3, a 3.53x speedup over the Jamba version, and a 6.21x speedup over the Zamba version.

</details>


### [167] [Causal Syntax Circuits in CodeLMs: Budget and Probe Decide What Looks Localised](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20683)
*Harsh Kumar,Tanmay Shailesh Joshi,Rahul Maity,Anusa Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以机制可解释性方法系统考察代码模型"默认写Python"这一偏好是否由模型内部一小群可定位组件产生，在55个模型-语言设置上运行统一流程，发现偏好规模大且随模型增大、经指令微调后更强，但其测量值主要取决于探针设计，且"是否局部化"高度依赖搜索预算等分析选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码模型在未指定语言时倾向于输出Python，但这一行为背后的机制尚不清楚。作者提出一个机制性问题：这种语言偏好是否由模型内部规模小、可定位的组件群产生，并希望通过多设置统一流程检验结论对分析选择的敏感性。

Method: 采用统一流水线：均值消融、激活修补、贪心电路抽取、随机对照显著性检验与激活引导；覆盖55个模型-语言设置，涉及10个开源代码模型（5个基座、5个指令微调）、3种目标语言（Rust、C#、PHP）、2套提示词、2种组件预算。

Result: 1）偏好显著、随模型规模增大，并在指令微调后保留甚至增强（每个指令微调检查点平均比基座高12%）；2）测得规模主要取决于探针：换成XLCoST平行函数后同一测量从约1.6降至约0.01；3）局部化与否取决于组件预算：预算12时15个设置中仅1个电路通过多重比较校正后优于随机对照，预算40时为6/15；4）小预算研究称"普遍失败"的干预有时有效：修补在2/45设置使Python提示偏好目标语法，引导在9/45改变最可能的下一个token；5）所有效应显著但方向背离目标语言的电路均来自电路抽取失败的设置，属诊断信号而非真实现象。

Conclusion: 关于某行为是否"局部化"的结论可能取决于论文通常不报告的分析选择（如探针、组件预算），作者公开完整结果网格以便改变与分析这些选择。

Abstract: Code models write Python when the prompt does not say which language to use. We ask a mechanistic question about that habit: is it produced by a small, findable group of components inside the model? We run one pipeline---mean ablation, activation patching, greedy circuit extraction, a random-control significance test, and activation steering---over 55 model-language settings spanning ten open code models (five base and five instruction-tuned), three target languages (Rust, C#, PHP), two prompt suites, and two component budgets. We report five findings. First, the preference is large, grows with model size, and survives instruction tuning: it is larger on every instruction-tuned checkpoint than on its base version, by 12% on average. Second, its measured size is mostly a property of the probe; replacing our aligned prompts with XLCoST parallel functions takes the same measurement on the same models from about 1.6 to about 0.01. Third, whether the preference looks localised depends on how many components the search may keep: with a budget of 12, 1 of 15 settings yields a circuit that beats size-matched random controls after multiple-comparison correction, and with a budget of 40, 6 of 15 do. Fourth, interventions that a smaller-budget study reports as uniformly failing do sometimes succeed: patching makes a Python prompt prefer target syntax in 2 of 45 settings, and steering changes the most likely next token in 9 of 45. Fifth, every circuit whose effect is significant but points away from the target language comes from a setting where circuit extraction failed, which makes such results a diagnostic rather than a phenomenon. The practical lesson is that a claim about whether a behaviour is localised can turn on analysis choices that papers rarely report. We release the full grid so those choices can be varied.

</details>


### [168] [Learning Topology-Aware Representations for Mechanics-Relevant TPMS Metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20684)
*Songlin Duan,Shuo Yang,Yujia Zheng,Tobin Filleter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种拓扑感知表征学习框架，将持续同调融入DMAE骨干网络，显式编码TPMS超材料的全局结构连通性，在分类、聚类与回归任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPMS机械超材料的力学分析与结构设计需要信息丰富的表征，但现有方法很少用与力学响应相关的全局结构连通性信息对学习到的表征进行显式监督。

Method: 在DMAE骨干中集成持续同调：采用持续同调引导的掩码策略，优先处理与持久拓扑特征相关的图像块；并引入辅助的持续同调解码器，促使隐表征保留关于连通分量、隧道和孔洞的拓扑特征数量信息。

Result: 在两个互补的TPMS数据集上，家族分类最高提升3.9%且线性探针表现最佳；无监督属性聚类分别提升26.7%与51.5%，同时回归性能保持R²=0.993。

Conclusion: 持续拓扑可作为复杂三维机械超材料表征学习的有效归纳偏置。

Abstract: Learning informative representations of mechanical Triply Periodic Minimal Surface (TPMS) metamaterials is important for mechanics analysis and structural design, yet existing methods have rarely explicitly supervised learned representations with information about global structural connectivity that is relevant to mechanical response. We propose a topology-aware representation learning framework that integrates persistent homology into a DMAE backbone to explicitly encode global structural connectivity. A persistence-guided masking strategy prioritizes patches associated with persistent topological features, while an auxiliary persistent-homology decoder encourages the latent representation to retain information about topological feature counts across connected components, tunnels, and voids. We evaluate the learned representations on two complementary TPMS datasets using family classification, mechanical-property classification, unsupervised clustering, and regression. Across the two datasets, our method demonstrates strong performance in both structural family and mechanical-property classification, improving family classification by up to 3.9% while achieving the best linear-probe performance. More notably, unsupervised property-clustering performance improves by up to 26.7% and 51.5% across the two datasets, while strong regression performance is retained with $R^2=0.993$. These results establish persistent topology as an effective inductive bias for representation learning of complex three-dimensional mechanical metamaterials.

</details>


### [169] [RoLaQ: Joint Rotation and Range Calibration for 2-Bit KV Caches in Multi-Head Latent Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20686)
*Shiyu Ji,Peng Ling,Chen Lin,Shupeng Li,Yixuan Wang,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoLaQ 是一种用于 MLA 键值缓存 2-bit 量化的离线联合旋转与范围校准方法，通过子空间内旋转优化及耦合量化范围学习，在多种模型与基准上取得最优平均精度，并在 DeepSeek-V2-Lite 上实现 3.96 倍 BF16 KV 容量与 2.51 倍解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA 将 KV 缓存内容压缩为每 token 的共享隐变量，便于低比特存储以支持长上下文推理；但在 2-bit 下，表示误差会同时扰动注意力匹配与 value 读出，且其影响依赖误差方向与重建尺度，因此需要针对性的旋转与范围校准来抑制误差。

Method: 提出 RoLaQ（Rotation-calibrated Latent Quantization），一种离线联合旋转与范围校准方法：在由冻结的 key/value 投影所定义的子空间内精炼共享旋转，并联合学习量化范围，使阈值与重建增益相互耦合以控制共享信号的响应；校准过程以预测匹配与注意力输出监督为引导，模型权重保持冻结。

Result: 在三个 MLA 模型与六个基准上，无论是否使用 token protection，RoLaQ 在对比的 2-bit 方法中均取得最高平均精度。在 DeepSeek-V2-Lite 上，基于 SGLang 的实现于相同显存预算下提供 3.96 倍 BF16 KV 容量，并在 256K 输入 token 时达到其 2.51 倍的解码吞吐。

Conclusion: RoLaQ 通过子空间旋转校准与耦合量化范围学习，有效缓解 MLA 隐变量 2-bit 量化的误差，在精度与效率两方面均优于现有 2-bit 方法，适用于长上下文推理场景。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) compresses the content of a key-value cache into a shared latent per token, making low-bit storage attractive for long-context inference. At two bits, errors in this representation perturb both attention matching and value readout, with effects that depend on error direction and reconstruction scale. We introduce RoLaQ (Rotation-calibrated Latent Quantization), an offline method for joint rotation and range calibration. RoLaQ refines a shared rotation within subspaces defined by the frozen key and value projections, and jointly learns quantization ranges whose thresholds and reconstruction gains are coupled to control the shared signal's response. Prediction matching and attention-output supervision guide calibration with model weights frozen. Across three MLA models and six benchmarks, RoLaQ achieves the highest average accuracy among the compared 2-bit methods both without and with token protection. On DeepSeek-V2-Lite, an SGLang implementation provides 3.96 times the BF16 KV capacity under the same memory budget and 2.51 times its decode throughput with 256K input tokens.

</details>


### [170] [On-Policy Residual Learning for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20687)
*Yifan Yu,Yuqing Jian,Jisen Li,Yuezhou Hu,Tianyi Zhang,Xiaoxia Wu,Rui-Jie Zhu,Junxiong Wang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Fan Lai,James Zou,Chenfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ORL,一种面向扩散语言模型(DLM)的 on-policy 残差学习框架,通过熵收缩目标与系统级协同设计,大幅提升速度-质量 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差上下文是释放 DLM 速度-质量 Pareto 前沿的关键,可在去噪步骤间传递预测分布。但如何有效学习残差上下文缺乏明确方法,既有工作未给出清晰的学习范式。

Method: 算法层面指出残差学习本质上是 on-policy 的,ORL 实时从模型自身产生的残差轨迹中学习,并引入熵收缩目标,使模型在反复访问自身预测时逐步锐化预测。系统层面针对精确残差监督需捕获、保留并重处理大量依赖轨迹的状态这一问题,协同设计异步残差捕获与传输、专用块扩散注意力以及打包的算力均衡训练,使其可大规模落地。

Result: 在由 Qwen3-4B 转换的 4B DLM 上,TPF 最高提升 6.2x,结合 dlmrt 引擎端到端吞吐提升约 6.9x,质量相当或更优。单 H100 单请求服务下,Pareto 前沿优于采用 EAGLE3 与 DFlash 投机解码的自回归模型,在匹配精度下比 DFlash 快 1.74x。迁移至 LLaDA-2.1-mini,仅用 36 H100 小时、4.2M 自生成 token(约为先前 LLaDA 方案 1/10 数据量),TPF 提升至多 1.5x、吞吐至多 3x;在 DiffusionGemma 上 TPF 提升 1.1-1.2x。结合 dlmrt 与 NVFP4 量化,单请求在单 B200 上达 5,046 tokens/s,八 B200 达 7,708,消费级 RTX 5090 达 2,788,质量相当。

Conclusion: 残差学习需按 on-policy 范式处理,ORL 通过算法与系统协同设计将这一机制实用化、大规模化,在多种 DLM 与硬件上同时改善 TPF、吞吐与速度-质量 Pareto 前沿,并具备良好的可迁移性与量化部署能力。

Abstract: Residual context is key to unlocking the speed-quality Pareto frontier of diffusion language models (DLMs) by carrying forward predictive distributions across denoising steps. Yet turning this seemingly simple mechanism into an effective learning paradigm is far from simple: there is no clear recipe for how residual context should be learned. We introduce ORL, a framework for on-policy residual learning in DLMs. Algorithmically, we identify that residual learning is inherently on-policy. ORL learns from model-induced residual trajectories on the fly and introduces an entropy-contraction objective that sharpens predictions as the model revisits its own predictions. System-wise, exact residual supervision requires capturing, retaining, and reprocessing many trajectory-dependent states. ORL co-designs the algorithm with asynchronous residual capture and transport, specialized block-diffusion attention, and packed compute-balanced training, making it practical at scale. On a 4B DLM converted from Qwen3-4B, ORL improves tokens per forward (TPF) by up to 6.2x and, with our dlmrt engine, end-to-end throughput by approximately 6.9x at comparable or better quality. In single-request serving on one H100, this mode achieves a stronger speed-quality Pareto frontier than autoregressive models with EAGLE3 and DFlash speculative decoding under both strict and relaxed verification, running 1.74x faster than DFlash at matched accuracy. ORL also transfers to existing DLMs: on LLaDA-2.1-mini, 36 H100-hours and 4.2M self-generated tokens, about 10x less data than prior LLaDA recipes, yield up to 1.5x higher TPF and up to 3x higher throughput at comparable quality, and on DiffusionGemma, ORL raises TPF by 1.1-1.2x. With dlmrt and NVFP4 quantization, ORL models decode a single request at up to 5,046 tokens/s on one B200, 7,708 on eight B200s, and 2,788 on a consumer RTX 5090, with comparable quality.

</details>


### [171] [Quantile-Guided Diffusion for Probabilistic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20739)
*Shadi Omari,Bashar Khoury,Assaf Schuster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将预测分位数作为预训练扩散预测器的分布控制信号，发现将分位数以残差形式注入去噪网络全过程效果最稳定，提出Control-TS框架，在八个基准上提升了骨干网络的CRPS，并引入CLPS指标评估生成轨迹的下游预测效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分位数预测能逐时间步总结不确定性，扩散预测器则通过采样未来轨迹刻画不确定性，二者信息表征方式不同。作者希望探索预测分位数能否作为扩散预测的分布控制接口，并厘清分位数如何整合进去噪网络才能有效引导预测。

Method: 研究不同分位数整合机制对引导预训练扩散预测器的影响，发现"全去噪网络残差控制"最有效；据此提出Control-TS，在冻结预训练骨干的前提下学习一条分位数控制通路，并设计条件线性预测得分（CLPS）以衡量生成未来轨迹的下游预测效用。

Result: 在八个基准上，Control-TS一致改善了骨干网络的CRPS，性能与最先进预测模型相当，在容量匹配对比中仍保持增益；进一步分析了增益对分位数粒度、预测器选择与预测质量的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 预测分位数可作为显式不确定性估计与扩散轨迹生成之间的有效接口，全网络残差式分位数控制是稳定的整合方式，CLPS补充了分布型指标以刻画生成轨迹的下游预测价值。

Abstract: Quantile forecasts summarize uncertainty at each future time step, while diffusion forecasters represent it through sampled future trajectories. We study how predicted quantiles can serve as distributional controls for diffusion forecasting. Specifically, we investigate how the mechanism of quantile integration affects its effectiveness in guiding a pretrained diffusion forecaster. We find that residual control throughout the denoising network yields the most consistent improvements. We instantiate this approach as Control-TS, which learns a quantile-control pathway while keeping the pretrained backbone frozen. Across eight benchmarks, Control-TS consistently improves the backbone's CRPS, achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art forecasting models, and retains its gains in capacity-matched comparisons. Further analyses examine how these gains depend on quantile granularity, predictor choice, and forecast quality. Finally, we introduce the Conditional Linear Predictive Score (CLPS), which complements distributional metrics by evaluating the downstream forecasting utility of generated futures. Together, these findings support predicted quantiles as an effective interface between explicit uncertainty estimates and diffusion-based trajectory generation.

</details>


### [172] [Vertical Sliced Wasserstein Distances for Meta-Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20743)
*Elaheh Akbari,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Kyungtae Han,Hamed Pirsiavash,Yikun Bai,Rocio Diaz Martin,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出垂直切片Wasserstein（VSW）距离，用于比较元测度空间 P(P(Ω)) 上的概率分布。VSW 通过连续测试函数投影内层测度，并用一维Wasserstein距离比较投影后的分布，具有可扩展性和良好理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习问题常需比较“观测本身即为概率测度”的概率分布，从而产生元测度空间 P(P(Ω))。然而该空间上的最优传输距离计算代价过高，难以实际应用，因此需要一种可扩展的替代度量。

Method: 提出VSW距离：不沿 P(Ω) 的传输几何进行切片，而是用连续测试函数将每个内层测度投影，再用一维Wasserstein距离比较所得的一维分布。理论上证明VSW是 P(P(Ω)) 上的有效度量，并刻画其弱收敛性。进一步推导经验 VSW_2^2 目标的粒子梯度。

Result: 理论上证明VSW是有效度量、可度量弱收敛，并具备与维度无关的统计收敛保证。实验表明相较现有元测度距离，VSW在精度和效率上均有优势；粒子梯度可用于基于梯度的元测度传输。

Conclusion: VSW为分布之分布的比较与优化提供了一种简单且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Many modern learning problems require comparing probability distributions whose individual observations are themselves probability measures, naturally giving rise to meta-measures in $\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{P}(Ω))$. However, optimal transport distances on this space can be prohibitively expensive to evaluate. We introduce the vertical sliced Wasserstein ($\mathrm{VSW}$) distance, a scalable metric on $\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{P}(Ω))$. Rather than slicing along the transport geometry of $\mathcal{P}(Ω)$, $\mathrm{VSW}$ projects each inner measure using continuous test functions and compares the resulting one-dimensional distributions with one-dimensional Wasserstein distance. We prove that $\mathrm{VSW}$ is a valid metric on $\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{P}(Ω))$, metrizes weak convergence, and admits dimension-independent statistical convergence guarantees. Empirically, $\mathrm{VSW}$ provides high accuracy and efficiency compared with existing distances for meta-measures. We further derive a practical particle gradient for the empirical $\mathrm{VSW}_2^2$ objective and demonstrate its use for gradient-based transport of meta-measures. $\mathrm{VSW}$ provides a simple and scalable alternative for comparing and optimizing distributions of distributions.

</details>


### [173] [Interpreting Language Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20748)
*Lucius Bushnaq,Dan Braun,Oliver Clive-Griffin,Bart Bussmann,Nathan Hu,Michael Ivanitskiy,Linda Linsefors,Lee Sharkey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗式参数分解（VPD），将语言模型参数分解为简单子组件，使得在对抗性选择的消融下仍能保持网络的输入-输出行为，从而实现对参数级计算机制的“电路”式分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性旨在揭示神经网络如何利用参数实现其算法。既有工作多集中于中间表示的结构，而对参数与非线性如何在这些表示上执行计算的理解进展甚少。本文试图填补这一空白，理解参数层面上的计算机制。

Method: 提出 vAdversarial Parameter Decomposition（VPD）方法：将神经网络参数优化分解为若干简单子组件，要求在大量子组件被消融（包括以对抗方式选择、旨在破坏行为的消融）时仍保持网络的输入-输出行为。这促使学到的子组件能以简短且机制忠实的方式描述网络行为，并可通过研究子组件之间交互序列来形成新型“电路”分析。

Result: VPD 能够找到一小组简单的、机制忠实的参数子组件，使得仅需少数子组件即可解释网络在任意输入上的行为，并支持对子组件交互序列产生输出的分析。

Conclusion: 虽然深入理解神经网络如何用参数计算行为仍需更多工作，但本工作提出了一条可行路径：识别一小组简单且机制忠实的子组件，作为后续机制分析的基元。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to uncover how neural networks use their parameters to implement their impressive neural algorithms. Although previous work has uncovered substantial structure in the intermediate representations that networks use, little progress has been made to understand how the parameters and nonlinearities of networks perform computations on those representations. In this work, we present a method that brings us closer to this understanding by decomposing a language model's parameters into subcomponents that each implement only a small part of the model's learned algorithm, while simultaneously requiring only a small fraction of those subcomponents to account for the network's behavior on any input. The method, adVersarial Parameter Decomposition (VPD), optimizes for decompositions of neural network parameters into simple subcomponents that preserve the network's input-output behavior even when many subcomponents are ablated, including under ablations that are adversarially selected to destroy behavior. This encourages learning subcomponents that provide short, mechanistically faithful descriptions of the network's behavior that should aggregate appropriately into more global descriptions of the network's learned algorithm. We study how sequences of interactions between these parameter subcomponents produce the network's output on particular inputs, enabling a new kind of 'circuit' analysis. While more work remains to be done to deepen our understanding of how neural networks use their parameters to compute their behavior, our work suggests an approach to identify a small set of simple, mechanistically faithful subcomponents on which further mechanistic analysis can be based.

</details>


### [174] [Optimal Hydra: Model Heterogeneous Federated Learning for LLMs using Data-Informed Hydra-Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20753)
*Gwen Legate,Rahaf Aljundi,Eugene Belilovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Optimal Hydra——首个面向LLM微调的模型异构联邦学习框架，结合全局层选择与客户端特定的注意力头选择，生成适配本地数据分布的Hydra-nets子网络，在三个数据集上显著优于基线并降低本地资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM性能提升依赖数据与算力增长；联邦学习可训练去中心化私有数据，但客户端资源受限，尤其模型规模扩大时共享的服务器-客户端架构不切实际。模型异构FL可按客户端能力适配模型大小，但既有工作局限于预训练场景，其子网络选择结论未必适用于微调；且此前方法不考虑客户端独特数据分布，忽略了为本地数据定制子网络结构的机会。

Method: 受注意力头在层内特征空间中表现任务依赖性的证据启发，提出Optimal Hydra：一个基于Transformer的异构联邦微调框架，将全局层选择与客户端特定的注意力头选择相结合，与本地数据分布对齐；显式利用客户端数据生成客户端特定子网络Hydra-nets。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，相比基线方法有显著提升，同时减少了本地资源需求。

Conclusion: Optimal Hydra是首个面向LLM微调的模型异构联邦学习方法，通过全局层与本地注意力头的协同选择实现客户端定制化子网络，在提升性能的同时降低客户端资源开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on vast amounts of data have demonstrated impressive performance over a range of tasks, but future performance gains will require increasing amounts of data and compute. Federated Learning (FL) facilitates training on decentralized private data, but client resource limitations can make training impractical, particularly as model sizes continue to expand. These resource constraints make the assumption of a shared server-client architecture impractical. Model heterogeneous FL offers a promising alternative by adapting model size to client capabilities; however, previous work is limited to the pre-training setting, and conclusions on sub-network selection do not necessarily hold for fine-tuning. Furthermore, prior approaches create client sub-networks without considering their unique data distributions, thereby overlooking the opportunity to tailor sub-network architecture to local data. Motivated by evidence that attention heads exhibit task dependency within layer defined feature spaces, we propose Optimal Hydra, a transformer based heterogeneous FL framework for fine-tuning that combines global layer selection with client specific attention head selection, aligned with local data distributions. Optimal Hydra makes explicit use of client data to produce client-specific sub-networks we call Hydra-nets. To our knowledge, this is the first model heterogeneous FL approach tailored to LLM fine-tuning. Experiments on three datasets show significant improvements over baseline methods while also reducing local resource demands.

</details>


### [175] [Semantic Alignment of EEG and Text: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Decoding Imagined Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20766)
*Shahryar G. Namdari,Francesco Iacomi,Riccardo Barbieri,Luca Mainardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将多模态对比学习框架用于EEG想象语音解码，把EEG试次与冻结文本编码器的语言表征对齐，在保持39类意图可分离性的同时获得语义结构化嵌入，并支持相似度检索与可靠性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统闭集分类器把语义类别当作独立标签，未显式利用语言意图之间的关系，输出灵活性受限。作者希望借助对比学习与语言表征对齐，突破固定类别解码的局限，实现更自然的脑机接口交互。

Method: 采用多模态对比学习框架，将EEG试次与冻结预训练文本编码器的语言表征在嵌入空间中对齐。EEG编码器使用复合目标训练：语义重加权EEG-句子对齐 + 基于文本原型的类别判别，以维持39类意图的可分离性。在Chisco想象语音数据集上对5名被试评估Top-k准确率、macro-F1、平衡成对匹配准确率(2v2)，并以最大EEG-文本余弦相似度(MaxCosine)作为解码可靠性指标，另用五条评估时语义轴和定性语义画廊探针(SGP)探究类别标签之外的语义结构。

Result: 对比框架取得与任务优化闭集分类器相当的解码性能，同时将EEG映射到冻结语言嵌入空间。MaxCosine能识别出Top-1和Top-5准确率更高的留出试次子集。五条评估时语义轴的平均一致性均高于50%随机基线，SGP定性地展示了与未见候选句的直接比较。

Conclusion: EEG-语言对齐在保持可比固定意图解码性能的同时，支持基于相似度的检索、内在可靠性排序以及更广语义结构的评估，表明对比式语言对齐是想象语音解码的有前景方向。

Abstract: Imagined speech decoding from EEG offers a promising route toward naturalistic brain-computer interfaces. Traditional closed-set classifiers treat semantic categories as independent labels and do not explicitly exploit relationships among linguistic intents, limiting output flexibility. We adapt a multimodal contrastive learning framework to imagined speech, aligning EEG trials with language representations in the embedding space of a frozen pretrained text encoder. An EEG encoder is trained with a composite objective combining semantically reweighted EEG-sentence alignment and text-prototype-based class discrimination, encouraging semantic structure while preserving separability among 39 intent categories. We evaluate the approach on the Chisco imagined speech dataset across five subjects, reporting Top-k accuracy, macro-F1, and balanced pairwise matching accuracy (2v2). We also investigate maximum EEG-text cosine similarity (MaxCosine) as an intrinsic indicator of decoding reliability and probe semantic structure beyond category labels using five evaluation-time semantic axes and a qualitative Semantic Gallery Probe (SGP) with previously unseen candidate sentences. The contrastive framework achieved decoding performance comparable to a task-optimized closed-set classifier while mapping EEG into a frozen language embedding space. MaxCosine identified subsets of held-out trials with higher Top-1 and Top-5 accuracy across subjects. Evaluation-time semantic axes showed mean agreement above the 50% chance reference across all five dimensions, and the SGP qualitatively illustrated direct comparison with unseen candidate sentences. Together, these findings suggest that EEG-language alignment can retain comparable fixed-intent decoding while supporting similarity-based retrieval, intrinsic reliability ranking, and evaluation of broader semantic structure.

</details>


### [176] [Selection-Safe Conformal Early Exit: Distribution-Free Coverage under Adaptive Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20773)
*Adria Binte Habib,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于前缀最大值的路径级（pathwise）共形校准方法：由前缀最大值构造单一终结路径分数并校准单一分位数，得到全部包含有效终结集的嵌套前缀预测集，从而在任意数据依赖的早退选择下保持选择性安全的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐早退层（layerwise）校准共形预测集时，一旦模型选择了一个数据依赖的退出点，覆盖率无法保持；而朴素的alpha/L（如Bonferroni）修正随退出数增多而过度保守，在退出数较多时甚至退化为全标签空间。因此需要一种无需多重检验修正、且对校准后任意停策略都成立的选择安全校准方案。

Method: 从各早退前缀分数中取前缀最大值，构造单一的终结路径分数；据此做一个split-conformal分位数校准，得到嵌套的前缀预测集，且每个前缀集都包含有效的终结集。该保证对任何保持终结集包含性的、label-free的校准后停策略同时成立，且无需alpha/L修正。理论上用“早退相对终结分数超出量”的经验尾部来界定路径最大值带来的效率损失；并给出实现含首次合格停策略、支持部署期预算变更而不重新校准。

Result: 在八个编码器-任务-骨干组合上，pathwise选择的宏平均覆盖率为0.901（逐层朴素选择为0.814），平均集大小1.41，延迟占比0.71。CIFAR-100、24退出ViT-L/16（n_cal=500）时，预算b=4的首次合格停策略覆盖率为0.902、集大小2.76、延迟占比0.57，同一校准可支持部署期预算变更而无需重拟合。n=100的退出数/校准规模联合扫描中，退出数从4增至24时pathwise集大小仅由3.02变到3.11，而Bonferroni在≥12个退出时退化为全标签空间；与MinSE有效选择在(n,L)=(100,24)的配对比较集大小差为0.80 [0.47, 1.13]。全词表解码器实验为非适用边界，集大小为7,162–9,620个token。

Conclusion: Pathwise校准在候选退出数相对校准集规模较多、校准后预算发生变化、以及浅层头能返回可用集的场景中最有价值；它消除了对alpha/L修正的依赖，避免Bonferroni式退化，而全词表解码器场景仍为其负向边界。

Abstract: Calibrating a conformal prediction set at each early exit does not preserve coverage once a data-dependent exit is selected. We construct one terminal path score from prefix maxima, calibrate a single split-conformal quantile, and return nested prefix sets that all contain the valid terminal set. The guarantee is selection-safe without an alpha/L correction and holds simultaneously for every post-calibration label-free stopping policy that preserves terminal-set containment. We also bound the efficiency cost of the path maximum by the empirical tail of the early-over-terminal score excess. Across eight encoder task-backbone cells, pathwise selection attains macro coverage 0.901 versus 0.814 for naive layerwise selection, with average set size 1.41 and latency fraction 0.71. On CIFAR-100 with a 24-exit ViT-L/16 (n_cal=500), first-eligible stopping at budget b=4 attains coverage 0.902, set size 2.76, and latency fraction 0.57, and the same calibration supports deployment-time budget changes without refitting. In a joint exit-count/calibration-size sweep at n=100, pathwise set size moves only from 3.02 to 3.11 as exits increase from 4 to 24, while Bonferroni degenerates to the full label space for 12 or more exits. Paired comparison against MinSE valid selection at (n,L)=(100,24) gives a set-size difference of 0.80 [0.47, 1.13]. Full-vocabulary decoder experiments remain a negative boundary, with sets of 7,162-9,620 tokens. Pathwise calibration is most useful when candidate exits are many relative to calibration size, when budgets change after calibration, and when shallow heads return actionable sets.

</details>


### [177] [Sliced Wasserstein Sketches with Error Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20788)
*Elaheh Akbari,James Liu,Keaton Hamm,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一维概率测度在二次Wasserstein距离下的自由权重M原子最优量化，并通过切片扩展到高维，提出误差分解与可计算证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何上有意义的紧凑表示对从概率分布中学习很重要，而现有切片Wasserstein压缩缺乏精确的误差分析与可计算保证。

Method: 采用L个共享投影切片，构造O(LM)比较时间的sketch；对最优sketch推导平方距离误差的精确分解（残差项与划分失配项）。

Result: 最优sketch在有限方向切片距离下达到O(M^{-1})误差（需(2+δ)矩条件），等质量sketch收敛更慢；每切片保均值，残差能量支持协方差恢复；实验显示显著降低存储且保持分类性能，并改善少样本泛化与端到端学习。

Conclusion: 最优M原子量化sketch为分布比较提供了紧凑、可证且实用的表示。

Abstract: Compact representations that support geometrically meaningful comparisons are valuable for learning from probability distributions. We study optimal, free-weight $M$-atom quantization of one-dimensional probability measures in quadratic Wasserstein distance and extend it to higher dimensions via slicing. Using $L$ shared projections yields a sketch with $O(LM)$ comparison time. Our main result is an exact decomposition of squared distance error into residual and partition-mismatch terms, yielding computable certificates for arbitrary centroid sketches. For optimal sketches, a finite $(2+δ)$ moment for some $δ>0$ guarantees $O(M^{-1})$ error in the finite-direction sliced distance, while equal-mass sketches can converge more slowly. Each slice preserves its mean, while storing residual energies enables covariance recovery from sufficiently many generic directions. Experiments on document, point-cloud, and flow-cytometry benchmarks show that optimal sketches substantially reduce storage while preserving the classification performance of uncompressed sliced distances. They also improve generalization from few labelled subjects and support end-to-end learning as compact pooling layers.

</details>


### [178] [EEG-Based Prediction of Music Enjoyment Using Deep Learning with Explainable Temporal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20795)
*Parshav Singla,Simranjit Kaur,Maanya Walia,Niharika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将音乐愉悦感预测建模为回归问题，在MUSIN-G数据集上比较六种深度学习模型，并分析TCN的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐愉悦体验主观且随乐曲展开而变化，理解其神经机制困难；以往EEG研究多将音乐情绪视为分类标签或依赖黑盒模型，缺乏连续愉悦感回归与可解释性分析。

Method: 基于MUSIN-G数据集（20名听者、128通道EEG及歌曲级愉悦评分），采用被试独立协议（15人训练与验证，5人测试），比较六种深度学习模型；使用梯度法与遮挡法解释，对全部留出窗口平均，识别每个两秒窗口内影响TCN预测最大的时间段。

Result: DeepConvNet、CNN-LSTM和GNN-CNN取得最低平均绝对误差（1-5量表上0.97-0.99）；TCN与评分相关性最高（Pearson r=0.21）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可复现的跨听者EEG愉悦感回归基准，并表明可解释性分析可补充标准评估指标。

Abstract: Understanding why listeners enjoy music is difficult because the experience is subjective and changes as a piece unfolds. Electroencephalography (EEG) offers a non-invasive way to study neural responses during listening, yet earlier work has mostly treated music emotion as a categorical label or relied on black-box models. We formulate enjoyment prediction as a regression problem on the public MUSIN-G dataset, which contains 128-channel EEG from 20 listeners together with song-level enjoyment ratings. Six deep learning models were compared under a participant-wise protocol, in which 15 listeners were used for training and validation and 5 were held out for testing. DeepConvNet, CNN-LSTM and GNN-CNN reached the lowest mean absolute error (0.97 to 0.99 on the 1 to 5 scale), while the temporal convolutional network (TCN) had the highest Pearson correlation with the ratings (r = 0.21). Gradient-based and occlusion-based explanations, averaged over all held-out windows, identify the time segments within each two-second window that most influence the TCN predictions. The study provides a reproducible cross-listener benchmark for EEG-based enjoyment regression and shows how explainability analysis complements standard metrics.

</details>


### [179] [Optimal Explicit and Implicit Regularization Strengths in High-Dimensional Continual Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20804)
*Gilad Karpel,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维情形下的持续线性回归，推导了每任务固定步数梯度下降训练的泛化损失闭式表达式，证明最优固定步长随任务数近似以 ln T/T 衰减，并揭示其与最优L2正则化的等价性、各向同性正则的最优条件及步长有限的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中顺序更新参数会引发灾难性遗忘，实践中步长与正则化等超参数设计缺乏理论指导。作者希望在高维线性回归这一可解析的框架下，明确持续学习泛化损失与步长、任务数、正则化之间的关系，从而为持续学习系统设计提供理论依据。

Method: 在高维持续线性回归设定下，学习者顺序观测多任务并逐步更新参数。作者针对每个任务执行固定步数梯度下降的情形，对任意教师模型推导泛化损失的显式闭式解；在此基础上分析最优固定步长随任务数的缩放规律，并与最优L2（各向同性）正则化的泛化损失进行比较。进一步将分析推广至带对角正则矩阵的广义岭回归，给出泛化损失的闭式刻画，并推导最优正则器为各向同性的充要条件，同时考察任务数趋于无穷时最优步长保持有限的机制。

Result: 得到任意教师下泛化损失的闭式表达式；证明单一教师及更一般的i.i.d.教师情形下，最优固定步长随任务数近似以 ln T/T 衰减；在该缩放下，泛化损失渐近等价于最优调参的L2各向同性正则化效果。对对角正则矩阵的广义岭回归给出泛化损失闭式刻画，并给出最优正则器为各向同性的充要条件。识别出一种最优步长在任务数趋于无穷时仍保持有限的机制，揭示任务可变性与隐式正则化之间的非平凡权衡，与i.i.d.教师缩放规律形成对比。线性模型与神经网络的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 持续线性回归中固定步数梯度下降的泛化行为可由闭式理论精确刻画，隐含的步长-正则化等价关系与非各向同性最优条件为持续学习系统的超参数设计提供了可操作的实践指导。

Abstract: We study continual linear regression in a high-dimensional regime, where a learner observes a sequence of tasks and updates its parameters sequentially. In this setting, we derive an explicit closed-form expression for the generalization loss of a model trained with gradient descent for a fixed number of steps per task, which holds for arbitrary teachers. We prove that the optimal fixed step size decays nearly inversely with the number of tasks, specifically as $\ln T / T$, for a single teacher and, more generally, i.i.d. teachers. Under this scaling, we show that the resulting generalization loss is asymptotically equivalent to that achieved by optimally tuned L2 (isotropic) regularization. Furthermore, we extend our analysis to generalized ridge regression with diagonal regularization matrices, obtaining a closed-form characterization of the generalization loss and identifying necessary and sufficient conditions under which the optimal regularizer is isotropic. Finally, we identify a regime where the optimal step size remains finite even as the number of tasks tends to infinity, thus revealing a non-trivial tradeoff between task variability and implicit regularization, in contrast to the i.i.d. teacher scaling. Our theoretical predictions are corroborated by experiments on both linear models and neural networks, providing practical guidance for the design of continual learning systems.

</details>


### [180] [Gibbs-Gap Policy Optimization for Off-Policy RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20814)
*Xuanchang Zhang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GGPO（Gibbs-Gap Policy Optimization），通过最小化同提示响应的 KL 调整奖励的 log-mean-exp 对比目标，摆脱 learner-to-behavior 重要性权重，缓解长响应训练中的高方差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励的强化学习常在由不同（可能陈旧）行为策略生成的响应上训练语言模型。用 learner-to-behavior 重要性权重纠正分布不匹配，会随响应变长而引入高方差。因此需要一种不依赖该类重要性权重、且能刻画参考模型刷新与有限数据效应的目标函数。

Method: 从 KL 正则化策略优化推导 GGPO：对同一提示下各响应的 KL 调整奖励做 log-mean-exp 对比最小化，不使用 learner-to-behavior 重要性权重。理论上证明其 population loss 恰为 KL 正则化最优性间隙，并在合适覆盖条件下给出 learner 单响应期望奖励与 behavior 策略 best-of-K 奖励的差距界（含原始有限组损失、正则化与有限训练数据项）。此外通过精确 Gibbs 目标复合刻画参考刷新，并在局部参考控制与 token 级行为失配下给出有限数据最终策略的奖励界。

Result: 消融显示参考刷新具有经验重要性：固定参考的 GGPO 在五基准问题加权平均上得 32.96，默认刷新方案得 46.58。数学推理实验表明，在训练-推理失配与固定离线数据下 GGPO 优于 GRPO 与 AGRO，并且在受控策略陈旧度增大时保持性能。

Conclusion: GGPO 为带可验证奖励的策略优化提供了一种无需 learner-to-behavior 重要性权重、方差更可控的方法，理论上与 KL 正则化最优性间隙对应，并依赖参考刷新以取得显著更优性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often trains language models on responses generated by a different, possibly stale behavior policy. Correcting this mismatch with learner-to-behavior importance weights can introduce high variance for long responses. We introduce Gibbs-Gap Policy Optimization (GGPO), which minimizes a log-mean-exp contrast of KL-adjusted rewards among responses to the same prompt, without learner-to-behavior importance weights. We derive GGPO from KL-regularized policy optimization: on policy, its population loss is exactly the KL-regularized optimality gap, and under suitable coverage the learner's single-response expected reward is at least the behavior policy's best-of-$K$ reward, minus terms for the raw finite-group loss, regularization, and finite training data. We characterize reference refresh through exact Gibbs-target composition and a finite-data final-policy reward bound under local reference control and tokenwise behavior mismatch. This refresh is empirically important: in an ablation, fixed-reference GGPO scores $32.96$ on the five-benchmark problem-weighted average, versus $46.58$ with the default refresh schedule. Experiments on mathematical reasoning further show that GGPO outperforms GRPO and AGRO under training-inference mismatch and on fixed offline data, while maintaining its performance as controlled policy staleness grows.

</details>


### [181] [O-RAN-Native Jamming Detection Under Network Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20816)
*Sneihil Gopal,Fernando J. Cintrón,Richard A. Rouil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统计集成方法(SEM)用于O-RAN网络中射频干扰检测，融合MAD基线检测、计数异常检测与突变检测加权投票，无需标注数据即可冷启动，在分布偏移下比机器学习和深度学习模型更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频干扰威胁蜂窝网络运行，O-RAN的E2接口暴露每UE遥测数据，使基于KPI的干扰检测无需专有硬件成为可能。但实际部署要求检测方法能跨异构网络泛化，而现有方法在分布偏移下性能下降。

Method: 提出SEM（统计集成方法），结合自适应MAD基线检测、基于计数的异常检测和突变检测，通过加权投票融合。SEM有冷启动模式（无需标注数据）和有监督模式（通过网格搜索离线精炼参数）。使用约25小时测试数据、8个异构5G环境日志，采用单环境和多环境留一(LOO)验证，与5个ML分类器和5个DL序列模型对比。

Result: 在训练中未遇到的分布偏移条件下，基于学习的模型倾向于丢失召回率并漏检干扰事件；而SEM即使在冷启动模式下也能保持检测能力，代价是虚警率较高。SEM的参数优化可映射到O-RAN的Non-RT RIC作为rApp进行持续精炼。

Conclusion: SEM在异构网络和分布偏移下比学习型模型更稳健，适合冷启动快速部署；随着标注数据语料充分，运营商可过渡到基于学习的替代方案，SEM参数优化可集成到Non-RT RIC实现持续改进。

Abstract: Radio-frequency jamming threatens cellular network operations in contested environments. The Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architecture exposes per-User Equipment (UE) telemetry through the E2 interface, enabling Key Performance Indicator (KPI)-based jamming detection without proprietary hardware. However, operational deployment demands detection methods that generalize across heterogeneous networks. We propose a Statistical Ensemble Method (SEM) combining adaptive Median Absolute Deviation (MAD)-based baseline detection, count-based anomaly detection, and abrupt change detection through a weighted voting system. SEM operates in two modes: a cold-start mode requiring no labeled data for immediate deployment, and a supervised mode where parameters are refined offline via grid search as labeled data accumulates. We evaluate SEM against five machine learning (ML) classifiers and five deep learning (DL) sequence models using approximately 25 hours of testbed data comprising logs from eight heterogeneous 5G environments, employing both single-environment and multi-environment leave-one-out (LOO) validation. Our analysis indicates that under conditions not encountered during training (distribution shift), learning-based models tend to lose recall and miss jamming events, whereas SEM, even in cold-start mode, maintains detection capabilities at the cost of false alarms. Furthermore, SEM's parameter optimization can be mapped to O-RAN's Non-RT RIC as an rApp for continuous refinement, allowing operators to transition to learning-based alternatives once corpus coverage is sufficient.

</details>


### [182] [Task-Phase-Conditioned Expert Routing Is Not a Reliable Proxy for Quantization Sensitivity in MoE FFN Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20826)
*Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究用MoE路由统计引导逐层混合精度量化，但重分配在各质量层级均未超过均匀基线，路由统计并非有效的逐层量化代价代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否利用MoE模型中逐层、逐任务阶段（提示/推理/响应）的专家路由统计，来指导路由专家FFN张量（down/gate与up）的逐层混合精度量化分配，以期在固定平均位宽预算下获得优于均匀量化的质量。

Method: 为Qwen-3.6-35B-A3B在ik_llama.cpp中构建路由捕获与性能剖析流水线，获取逐层路由统计；由路由熵/决断性、变异系数、Jaccard/阶段脆弱性及top-1专家强度构建综合敏感度分数；用坐标下降在固定平均位宽预算下搜索逐层分配。实验条件包括约4.0 bpw平坦、约2.7 bpw陡峭，以及约2.7 bpw随机分配作为对照。该流水线适用于标准top-k MoE架构。

Result: 在各质量层级内，重分配均未超过均匀基线：平坦区间统计上中性（z≈0.9）；陡峭层级中随机重分配与路由引导重分配差距在1个标准误以内，表明该质量档位的结构性损失源于强制降位与KLD尾部注入；激进预算条件下出现确定性损失（z>4 SE），并在三个独立权重向量上复现。

Conclusion: 路由行为统计作为逐层真实量化代价的代理指标缺乏验证，这是问题的深层根源；基于路由统计的逐层混合精度分配方法未能带来质量收益。

Abstract: We investigate whether per-layer, per-task-phase (prompt/reasoning/response) expert-routing statistics in a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model can guide layer-wise mixed-precision quantization of routed-expert FFN tensors (down/gate and up). To attain layerwise routing statistics, we build a routing-capture and profiling pipeline for Qwen-3.6-35B-A3B in ik_llama.cpp, derive a composite sensitivity score from routing entropy/decisiveness, coefficient of variation (CV), Jaccard/phase-fragility, and top-1 expert strength, and use coordinate descent to tune a layer allocation that respects a fixed average bits-per-weight budget. Notably, the profiling pipeline is applicable to standard top-k MoE architectures. Across independent experimental conditions (about 4.0 bpw flat, about 2.7 bpw steep, and about 2.7 bpw random allocation as a control), reallocation fails to outperform the uniform baseline within each quality tier. In the flat regime, reallocation is statistically neutral (z about 0.9). For the steep tier, the random reallocation performs within 1 SE of the routing-guided reallocation at the same tier, indicating a structural loss at this quality rung caused by mandatory downgrades and Kullback-Leibler Divergence (KLD) tail injection. This aggressive-budget condition shows a decisive loss (z greater than 4 SE) replicated across three independent weight vectors. We argue that the deeper issue is that routing-behavior statistics are an unvalidated proxy for true per-layer quantization cost.

</details>


### [183] [Amortizing Sequential Experimental Design in Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20832)
*Anita Kriz,Antonin Berthon,Nicolás Astorga,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何通过信息增益奖励显式训练LLM的推理过程，将序列贝叶斯实验设计（BED）摊销进LLM，从而提升其在实验设计任务中的自适应信息收集能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽具备广泛的预训练知识和较强推理能力，但在实验设计任务中难以自适应地收集信息。作者希望将序列贝叶斯实验设计的能力摊销到LLM中，通过显式训练引导动作选择的推理过程，使其具备可泛化的信息收集技能。

Method: 方法上，实验生成模型独立于LLM自身信念进行设定，从而可在选定模型下优化期望信息增益。研究探讨了多种摊销设计选择，包括替代的生成模型构建、奖励估计、假设空间设定、多步目标，以及与训练时和推理时BED基线相比的计算成本。

Result: 在Twenty Questions任务上，所得策略提升了信息获取与任务表现，在显著更低的计算成本下优于推理时和训练时BED基线，并能泛化到未见类别。此外，从Twenty Questions训练到的策略可正向迁移到真实临床对话基准Medi-Q。

Conclusion: 结果表明，信息收集能力可以作为LLM中一种可泛化的推理技能被学习。

Abstract: Despite their extensive pretraining knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, large language models (LLMs) struggle to adaptively gather information in experimental design tasks. We study how to amortize sequential Bayesian experimental design (BED) into an LLM by explicitly training the reasoning that guides action selection using information-gain rewards. Our approach specifies the experimental generative model independently of the LLM's own beliefs, allowing us to optimize expected information gain under a model of our choosing. We investigate the design choices for amortization, including alternative generative-model constructions, reward estimation, hypothesis-space specification, multi-step objectives, and computational costs relative to training- and inference-time BED baselines. On Twenty Questions, the resulting policies improve information acquisition and task performance, outperform inference- and training-time BED baselines at substantially lower computational cost, and generalize to unseen categories. We further demonstrate positive transfer from Twenty Questions training to Medi-Q, a real-world clinical dialogue benchmark. These results suggest that information gathering can be learned as a generalizable reasoning skill in LLMs.

</details>


### [184] [Denoising Score Matching in Diffusion Models: The Effect of Rank Constraints on Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20850)
*Anand Jerry George,Nicolas Macris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究rank约束的去噪分数匹配网络的泛化行为，通过实验与随机特征理论分析，揭示rank对训练/测试误差的差异化影响，并给出dimension of orthogonal complement作为rank threshold。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构偏置限制了扩散分数网络可表示的函数类，但其对泛化的影响尚不清晰。作者希望理解rank约束（bottleneck）如何影响分数估计的训练与测试误差，以及为何其正则化效果依赖于数据维度与样本量。

Method: 1) 数值实验：在嵌入球面支撑的数据上训练三层可训练网络，使用带rank约束非线性项与线性skip connection的分数网络进行denoising score matching；2) 理论分析：研究高斯数据支撑于低维子空间下的随机特征分数模型，在rank约束下刻画最优估计器（通过truncated SVD），并在n、p、D、r与ambient dimension d成比例增长时推导渐近精确的极限训练与测试误差，给出谱刻画。

Result: 实验发现：提高rank降低训练误差，但对测试误差的影响取决于样本量与数据维度。理论得到：谱刻画可区分改善经验拟合的eigenmodes与改善population accuracy的eigenmodes；并识别出数据子空间正交补的维度作为自然的rank threshold；理论learning curves定性复现了观察到的rank相关的overfitting现象。

Conclusion: 该工作为bottleneck或rank约束如何正则化分数估计提供了可处理的解释，说明其有效性依赖数据维度与样本量：过小rank欠拟合、过大rank过拟合，而数据子空间正交补维度可作为rank选择的自然阈值。

Abstract: Architectural biases restrict the class of functions that a diffusion score network can represent, but their effect on generalization remains poorly understood. We study denoising score matching with score networks having a rank constrained non-linear term and a linear skip connection. Numerical experiments with trainable three-layer networks on data supported on an embedded sphere show that increasing rank lowers training error, but its effect on test error depends on sample size and data dimension. To explain this behavior, we analyze a random-feature score model under rank constraint with Gaussian data supported on a low-dimensional subspace. We characterize the optimal rank-constrained estimator through a truncated singular value decomposition and derive asymptotically precise limiting training and test errors when sample size $(n)$, feature width $(p)$, data dimension $(D)$, and rank $(r)$ scale proportionally with the ambient dimension $(d)$. The resulting spectral characterization distinguishes eigenmodes that improve the empirical fit from those that improve population accuracy. It also identifies the dimension of the orthogonal complement of the data subspace as a natural rank threshold. The theoretical learning curves qualitatively reproduce the observed rank-dependent overfitting, providing a tractable account of how bottlenecks or rank constraints can regularize score estimation and why their effectiveness depends on data dimension and sample size.

</details>


### [185] [Long-Context Drafting Does Not Require Long-Context Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20865)
*Aref Jafari,Vansh Bhatia,Matt Qin,Subhajit Dutta Chowdhury,Ben Lee,Vikram Appia,Ali Ghodsi,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证在超长上下文（至200万token）下无需训练长上下文drafters。作者冻结已发布的短上下文drafter，通过两种推理时机制（限制可见注意力缓存以固定成本、将RoPE位置索引重映射回训练范围）使其在远超训练长度时仍保持短上下文的加速比，且不修改任何权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码在超长上下文下失效：若drafter需对完整前缀做注意力，就会继承目标模型的上下文相关成本，加速比先被侵蚀继而反转。常见应对是构建长上下文drafter（训练/微调，或使用目标检查点内置的多token预测头）。作者认为这没必要。

Method: 核心洞察：现用drafter是目标条件化的（target-conditioned），读取的是目标模型在整个前缀上算出的隐状态，因此远距离上下文无需自身注意力即可到达drafter，验证环节会纠正其遗漏。故冻结已发布的短上下文drafter，在两种推理时机制下让其远超训练长度运行：(1) 限制drafter可见的注意力缓存以固定其成本；(2) 将其旋转位置索引重新基准化回训练范围内。不触碰任何权重，唯一可调参数很早饱和，故单一vLLM侧配置即可覆盖4k至200万token。作者还证明了某种公共索引偏移使drafter计算精确不变的条件，并将接受率分解为两个可测差距并给出紧界。

Result: 在三种target-drafter配对与五个语料库上，冻结drafter在短上下文所达到的任意加速比可一直保持到200万token；短长度下至多损失0.16倍的加速比。结论关乎加速比的"平坦性"而非具体倍数，且移除任一机制都会使该平坦性消失。收益的条件是：每生成token的投机成本须低于匹配的纯目标成本。

Conclusion: 在超长上下文下维持投机解码加速无需构建长上下文drafter；冻结短上下文drafter配合"缓存受限+RoPE位置重基准"两种推理时机制，即可将短上下文加速比平坦地外推至200万token，前提是投机成本低于纯目标成本。

Abstract: Speculative decoding pairs a cheap drafter with parallel verification against the target. At ultra-long context it stops paying, because a drafter that attends over the full prefix inherits the target's context-dependent cost, so the speedup it was meant to provide first erodes and then reverses. The standard response is to build a long-context drafter, by training or fine-tuning one, or by using a multi-token-prediction head shipped inside the target checkpoint. We argue this is unnecessary, because the drafters in use are target-conditioned, reading hidden states the target computed over the entire prefix, so distant context already reaches them without their own attention, and verification corrects what they miss. We therefore freeze released short-context drafters and run them far past their training length under two inference-time mechanisms, one bounding the attention cache they see, which fixes their cost, and one re-basing their rotary position indices into the trained range. No weight is touched and the only tunable saturates early, so one vLLM-side configuration spans 4k to two million tokens, costing at most 0.16x of speedup at short length. We prove when a common index shift leaves the drafter's computation exactly unchanged, and decompose acceptance into two measurable gaps with sharp bounds. Whatever speedup a frozen drafter delivers at short context, it retains out to two million tokens, across three target-drafter pairs and five corpora. The claim concerns the flatness rather than the multiple, and removing either mechanism eliminates it. The gain is conditional on the speculative cost per emitted token staying below the matched target-only cost.

</details>


### [186] [SHRED: Self-Play for Heterogeneous Road-Users without Expert Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20866)
*Wei-Jer Chang,Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHRED框架，使车、行人、骑行者通过大规模异构自博弈从随机初始化学习真实交互行为，无需人类驾驶示范，在WOSAC和InterPlan上均超越已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交通仿真与自主规划需建模异构道路使用者之间的交互，但现有方法主要依赖大规模人类驾驶示范来学习真实交互行为，存在数据依赖问题。作者追问：这种真实交互行为能否仅通过自博弈自发涌现，从而摆脱对示范数据的依赖。

Method: SHRED在随机初始化下联合训练车辆、行人和骑行者进行大规模异构自博弈，不使用示范数据或日志轨迹回放。其将各类型专属的动力学与目标函数，同程序化场景生成与逐智能体随机化相结合，使多样行为通过闭环交互中自然涌现。

Result: 在WOSAC上，SHRED联合控制三类道路使用者，性能超过最接近的无示范自博弈基线及此前的自博弈方法（后者使用示范训练的参考策略）；仅在nuPlan地图几何上训练的策略在Waymo零样本评测中仍保持可比真实性。在规划方面，SHRED在交互密集的InterPlan基准上超过所有对比规划器。消融实验表明自博弈的异构性同时提升交通真实性与规划性能，且在交互密集场景中规划增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 结果表明异构程序化自博弈是一种可扩展、免示范的范式，可用于学习真实且具交互性的交通行为，兼具仿真真实性与规划有效性的双重优势。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation and autonomous planning both depend on modeling interactions among heterogeneous road users whose dynamics, constraints, and behaviors differ fundamentally. Existing approaches predominantly learn realistic interaction behavior from large-scale human driving demonstrations. In this work, we ask whether such behavior can instead emerge purely through self-play. We present SHRED, a framework that jointly trains vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists through large-scale heterogeneous self-play from random initialization, without demonstrations or logged-trajectory replay. SHRED combines type-specific dynamics and objectives with procedural scenario generation and per-agent randomization, allowing diverse behaviors to emerge through closed-loop interaction. On the Waymo Open Sim Agents Challenge (WOSAC), SHRED jointly controls all three road-user types and outperforms both the closest demonstration-free self-play baseline and prior self-play methods that use demonstration-trained reference policies. Policies trained only on nuPlan map geometry retain comparable realism under zero-shot evaluation on Waymo. For planning, SHRED outperforms all compared planners on the interaction-heavy InterPlan benchmark. Controlled ablations show that heterogeneity in self-play improves both traffic realism and planning performance, with particularly large planning gains in interaction-heavy scenarios. Together, these results establish heterogeneous procedural self-play as a scalable, demonstration-free paradigm for learning realistic and interactive traffic behavior.

</details>


### [187] [Clinically Interpretable Biological Age Estimation Using CatBoost and SHAP with Continuous and Categorical Features from NHANES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20868)
*Yanni Sun,Zheng Qu,Kevin Ren,Zhanglin Lin Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于NHANES大数据构建了可临床解释的CatBoost生物年龄估计框架，并通过自动化SHAP依赖图量化分析实现可复现的多层次可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物年龄（BA）机器学习模型多依赖连续实验室生物标志物，且SHAP依赖图通常仅作视觉解读，缺乏可复现的定量指标，限制了模型的透明度与临床应用。

Method: 使用NHANES 2011—2020年3月共24983名成人数据，纳入44个人口学、实验室、体格检查及生活方式预测变量，构建CatBoost回归模型以估计实际年龄（CA），将模型估计年龄定义为BA；采用SHAP进行多层次解释，并在依赖图上实施预设的自动化程序，量化与最低/最高BA贡献相关的特征值范围、相应调整幅度及零点交叉阈值。

Result: 模型估计BA与CA在留出测试集上强相关（Pearson r = 0.798，P < 0.001）。群体层面SHAP显示血尿素氮、血清葡萄糖、剧烈休闲活动、红细胞分布宽度和丙氨酸氨基转移酶为主要预测因子，且有3个分类变量进入前25位。自动化定量SHAP分析刻画了各预测因子的特定范围、效应量与拐点，瀑布图支持个体层面解释。

Conclusion: 该框架将定性的可视化解释扩展为可复现的定量指标，适用于异质性临床数据，提升了透明度，并为生物衰老研究与精准健康提供了可扩展的方法学基础。

Abstract: Biological age (BA) captures inter-individual variation in physiological aging beyond chronological age (CA). Although machine-learning models have advanced BA estimation, many rely primarily on continuous laboratory biomarkers, and SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) dependence plots are typically interpreted visually. We developed a clinically interpretable machine-learning framework for BA estimation using heterogeneous clinical data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES 2011-March 2020, N = 24,983 adults aged >= 18). A CatBoost regression model incorporated 44 demographic, laboratory, physical examination, and lifestyle predictors to estimate CA, defining model-estimated age as BA. SHAP provided multi-level interpretability. Crucially, building on standard visual inspection, we implemented a prespecified automated procedure directly on SHAP dependence plots to quantify feature-value ranges associated with lowest and highest BA contributions, corresponding adjustment magnitudes, and zero-crossing thresholds. Model-estimated BA strongly correlated with CA in held-out testing data (Pearson r = 0.798, P < 0.001). Population-level SHAP identified blood urea nitrogen, serum glucose, vigorous recreational activity, red cell distribution width, and alanine aminotransferase among the leading predictors, with three categorical variables ranking among the top 25. Automated quantitative SHAP analysis mapped predictor-specific ranges, effect sizes, and inflection points, while waterfall plots enabled individual explanations. By extending qualitative visual interpretation to reproducible quantitative metrics across heterogeneous clinical data, this framework enhances transparency and provides a scalable methodological foundation for biological aging research and precision health.

</details>


### [188] [CogDrift-4D: Hierarchical Multimodal Modeling of Temporal Behavioral Decay for MCI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20877)
*Sankalp Vaish,Sumukh Veeramalla,Rishank Singh,Caroline Summerour,John A. Batsis,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨18个月、7次非脚本多模态交互的行为数据集，并将其形式化为四维张量，进而提出分层4D Transformer框架CogDrift-4D用于轻度认知障碍（MCI）早期检测，分类准确率0.733±0.13、F1为0.716±0.14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻度认知障碍的早期检测是痴呆护理的关键瓶颈，亟需可扩展工具以捕捉细微的行为衰退；同时认知衰退具有细微的时序变化特征，需要能够同时保留会话内局部动态与跨会话长期变化的建模方式。

Method: 构建18个月内7次非脚本多模态交互的行为数据集，将其形式化为Batch×Session×Time×Feature的四维稠密张量；提出分层4D Transformer框架CogDrift-4D，采用双层行为疲劳与漂移建模：会话内层用局部最小二乘回归斜率量化帧级面部动作单元与声学能量的急性"衰减"，会话间层用递归交叉注意力反馈编码器作为累积疲劳累加器以映射行为漂移；并通过局部Quality Gate动态过滤低质量特征，结合语义信息与原始物理动态的早融合。

Result: 在结构化言语与语言交互上评估，MCI检测分类准确率为0.733±0.13，F1分数为0.716±0.14，优于先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为家庭化、实时痴呆筛查提供了数学严谨、可扩展且鲁棒的基础。

Abstract: Early detection of mild cognitive impairment remains a critical bottleneck in dementia care, demanding scalable tools capable of capturing subtle behavioral decay. To capture the subtle temporal changes of cognitive decline, we introduce a behavioral dataset collected across seven unscripted multimodal interactions over an 18-month horizon. This dataset formalizes continuous, multi-session interactions into a dense 4D tensor (Batch * Session * Time * Feature) that explicitly preserves concurrent local dynamics alongside long-term changes. To effectively process these complex behavioral patterns, we propose CogDrift-4D: a novel, hierarchical 4D Transformer framework. To capture the complex trajectory of cognitive decline, CogDrift-4D implements a dual-level behavioral fatigue and drift modeling architecture. At the intra-session level, the model computes localized least-squares regression slopes to quantify the acute ``fading'' of frame-level facial action units and acoustic energy. At the inter-session level, a recursive cross-attention feedback encoder acts as a cumulative fatigue accumulator, mapping the patient's continuous behavioral drift across successive sessions. To mitigate environmental noise and track failures across sessions, a localized Quality Gate dynamically filters low-integrity features before final fusion, also an early fusion that integrates semantic insights with raw physical dynamics. Evaluated on structured verbal and linguistic interactions, our architecture successfully detects MCI with a classification accuracy of 0.733 +- 0.13 and an F1-score of 0.716 +- 0.14, outperforming advanced baselines. The framework provides a mathematically rigorous, scalable, and robust foundation for home-based, real-time dementia screening.

</details>


### [189] [Do Emergent Misalignment Models Share a Circuit?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20887)
*Muhammad Haseeb,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过方向性激活追踪，在三种有害任务LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct中发现三个跨模型共享的注意力头位置；替换这些头的贡献可将自由生成中的涌现性失配率大幅降低，并证明这些共享组件具有因果相关性且部分可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作发现，在狭义有害任务上微调语言模型会引发跨无关领域的涌现性失配（EM），且独立微调的模型之间存在共享的失配相关表征。然而，这些模型是否依赖共同的计算组件仍不清楚，本文旨在探究该问题。

Method: 对Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct进行三个LoRA微调，分别针对有害医疗建议、高风险财务建议和极限运动推荐。采用基于方向的激活追踪，识别在三个模型中均位列前五的三个共享注意力头位置，并进行替换干预与跨模型激活迁移实验。

Result: 将各模型选定的五个注意力头贡献替换为基座贡献后，自由生成EM率分别从医疗38.9%降至22.9%、财务50.7%降至12.5%、运动41.7%降至11.1%，优于层级匹配的随机干预且未降低连贯性评分。跨模型激活迁移显示共享头在固定响应偏好上具有部分可互换效应。

Conclusion: 结果表明，EM中的表征收敛不仅体现于相关表征，还延伸到同一模型架构内共享且具因果作用的注意力头组件。

Abstract: Fine-tuning language models on narrowly defined harmful tasks can induce emergent misalignment (EM) across unrelated domains. While prior work has identified shared misalignment-associated representations across independently fine-tuned models, it remains unclear whether these models rely on common computational components. We investigate this question using three LoRA fine-tunes of Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct trained on harmful medical advice, risky financial advice, and extreme-sports recommendations. Using direction-based activation tracing, we identify three attention-head locations shared among the five highest-ranked heads of all three organisms. Replacing each organism's five selected head contributions with Base contributions reduces free-generation EM from 38.9% to 22.9% for medical, 50.7% to 12.5% for finance, and 41.7% to 11.1% for sports, outperforming layer-matched random interventions without reducing judged coherence. Cross-organism activation transfer further shows that the shared heads exhibit partially interchangeable effects on fixed-response preference. These findings provide evidence that representational convergence in EM extends to shared, causally relevant attention-head components within a common model architecture.

</details>


### [190] [Wrong Votes, Right Gradients: Why Majority-Vote Rewards Track Verifiable RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20898)
*Dylan P. Wilson,Chengsong Huang,Jiaxin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 自生成标签的强化学习之所以能恢复大部分可验证奖励的收益，关键在批量级更新的误差相干性：只要各提示的标签误差方向不相干，误导项随批量增大消失，即使标签错误率很高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从提示级视角看，多数投票只返回模型的众数答案，无法提供正确性信息，因此自生成标签能恢复大部分可验证奖励收益这一现象难以解释。作者试图把分析单位从提示级转向批量级更新，以解释该现象。

Method: 将多数投票更新相对黄金更新分解为收缩项与误导项；在五个模型上测量逐响应梯度；以随机奖励为对照检验误差是否不相干；比较多数投票更新与黄金更新的一致程度，并计算噪声校正后的余弦相似度；再做标签随机翻转实验（随机翻转最多30%黄金标签，与由共享规则翻转同样比例作对比）。

Result: 参数共享使得在实践中批量规模下，单个提示自身梯度对该提示输出变化的贡献不足1%；多数投票误差与随机奖励零假设一样不相干；多数投票更新与黄金更新的一致程度，与两次独立黄金更新彼此的一致程度相当，噪声校正余弦在误差范围内为1；随机翻转最多30%黄金标签不改变更新方向，而同一比例由共享规则翻转则破坏方向；偏好某类响应的奖励（如学习到的奖励模型）可测地表现不足。该等价在每个检查点都成立，但只是局部的：黄金奖励训练在留出多数准确率上仍领先。

Conclusion: 决定自生成标签能否替代验证的不是错误率，而是误差相干性：误差跨提示不相干时，误导项随批量增大消失。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with self-generated labels, such as majority vote over a model's own samples, often recovers most of the gain of training with verifiable rewards. This is puzzling from the usual prompt-level view, in which majority vote only returns a model's modal answer and cannot add information about correctness. We argue that the relevant unit is the batch-level update. Because parameters are shared, a prompt's own gradient accounts for less than 1\% of the change in that prompt's output at practical batch sizes, so a wrong label matters only to the extent that label errors share a direction across prompts. We decompose the majority-vote update into shrinkage and misdirection relative to the gold update, and show that misdirection vanishes with batch size whenever errors are incoherent across prompts, however often the labels are wrong. Measuring per-response gradients on five models, we find that majority-vote errors are as incoherent as a random-reward null, that the majority-vote update agrees with the gold update as closely as two independent gold updates agree with each other, and that its noise-corrected cosine with the gold update is 1 within error. Randomly flipping up to 30\% of gold labels leaves the update direction intact, while the same fraction flipped by a shared rule destroys it, and rewards that favor a kind of response on every prompt, such as a learned reward model, fall measurably short. The equivalence holds at every checkpoint we examine but is local: gold-reward training still ends ahead on held-out majority accuracy. Error coherence, not error rate, determines when self-generated labels can stand in for verification.

</details>


### [191] [Sign Betting for Misspecified Regression: Variance Adaptivity and Optimal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20903)
*Yinan Li,Han Han,Kwang-Sung Jun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究误设定下有界回归的 L1 风险，分析 sign-betting regression 的极小极大估计，证明其高概率 L1 oracle 不等式，并刻画恰当学习的最优通用逼近因子为 3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准回归常以 L2(P_X) 度量误差，但下游决策任务（如赌博机、强化学习）更关注对条件均值 η(x) 的 L1(P_X) 距离。因此需要在误设定情形下研究 L1 目标的回归估计与学习理论性质。

Method: 分析 Baraud & Maillard (2025) 的极小极大估计器（称为 sign-betting regression）：证明其高概率 L1 oracle 不等式；在可实现性下使上界适应平均条件方差；通过下界论证刻画恰当学习的通用逼近因子；并将上界分析推广到非有限函数类。

Result: sign-betting regression 在误设定下获得高概率 L1 oracle 不等式；可实现时上界适应平均条件方差，并恢复 Li et al. (2026) 的方差自适应速率。恰当学习的最优通用逼近因子为 3：sign betting 可达 3，而不存在分布无关、高概率 L1 oracle 不等式下通用逼近因子严格小于 3 的恰当学习器。上界分析扩展到非有限类，并用合成与真实模型选择实验验证理论。

Conclusion: L1 视角下 sign-betting regression 在误设定与可实现两种情形均具近优或自适应保证，恰当学习的通用逼近因子 3 是最优的；理论可推广到非有限类并有实验支持。

Abstract: We study bounded regression under model misspecification, evaluating a function $f$ by its $L_1(P_X)$ distance to the conditional mean $η(x)=\mathbb{E}[Y\mid X=x]$, rather than the standard $L_2(P_X)$. This $L_1$ metric is natural for downstream decision problems such as bandits and reinforcement learning. We analyze the min--max estimator of Baraud & Maillard (2025), which we call \emph{sign-betting regression}. Our main results show that sign-betting regression achieves high-probability $L_1$ oracle inequalities under misspecification. Under realizability, our bound adapts to the average conditional variance and recovers the variance-adaptive rate of Li et al. (2026). We further characterize the optimal universal approximation factor for proper learning by showing that sign betting achieves factor 3, while no proper learner can satisfy a distribution-free, high-probability $L_1$ oracle inequality with a universal approximation factor strictly below 3. We extend the upper-bound analysis beyond finite classes and complement the theory with experiments in synthetic and real-world model-selection tasks.

</details>


### [192] [Task-Centric Multimodal Learning for Cognitive Impairment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20906)
*Sankalp Vaish,Rishank Singh,Sumukh Veeramalla,Caroline Summerour,John A. Batsis,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于Amazon Alexa结构化语音交互的无接触多模态筛查框架，融合视频、音频与文本信号，用于区分轻度认知障碍（MCI）与健康老龄化，并验证多模态融合优于单模态模型的稳定性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痴呆影响全球数百万老年人，但临床场景之外的早期检测仍然困难；传统模型主要依赖文本或语音回答，仅能捕捉人类认知的有限侧面，未能体现人类表达本身的多模态特性，因此需要更贴近真实世界的多模态认知评估方式。

Method: 构建无接触多模态筛查框架，采集参与者与Amazon Alexa进行结构化语音交互过程中的视频、音频和文本信号，涵盖语音模式、嗓音特征、面部表情和语言结构。参与者完成三项分别针对自发言语、理解与回忆的会话任务。研究设定三个目标：（1）评估三项Alexa任务中哪一项提供最具信息量的认知信号；（2）比较单模态模型的效果；（3）评估多模态融合模型的收益。

Result: 任务层面分析显示：视频模型在Command-Reading任务上表现最佳，准确率0.65（AUC 0.69）；音频模型在Question-Answering任务上最佳，准确率0.67（AUC 0.73）。多模态模型在各任务上表现稳定，在Command-Reading上准确率0.76（AUC 0.75），在Question-Answering上准确率0.76（AUC 0.73）。

Conclusion: 通过自然交互捕捉的多模态行为线索能够可靠区分MCI与健康老龄化，凸显传统诊断范式的局限，支持向多模态、真实世界认知评估框架转变。

Abstract: Dementia affects millions of older adults worldwide, yet early detection remains difficult outside clinical settings. Traditional models primarily rely on text or voice-based responses, capturing only a limited view of human cognition despite the inherently multimodal nature of human expression. This work proposes a contactless, multimodal screening framework that leverages video, audio, and text signals collected during structured voice-based interactions with Amazon Alexa. By incorporating these modalities, our approach captures a more holistic representation of human behavior encompassing speech patterns, vocal characteristics, facial expressions, and linguistic structure, which may enable more accurate detection of cognitive decline. Different participants completed three conversational tasks designed to probe spontaneous speech, comprehension, and recall. We pursued three objectives: (1) evaluate which of the three Alexa tasks provides the most informative cognitive signals, (2) compare the effectiveness of unimodal models, and (3) assess the benefits of multimodal fusion models. Task-level analysis shows that the video model achieves its best performance on Command-Reading, with an accuracy of 0.65 (AUC 0.69), while the audio model performs best on Question-Answering, reaching an accuracy of 0.67 (AUC 0.73). In contrast, the multimodal model demonstrates consistently stable performance across tasks, achieving an accuracy of 0.76 (AUC 0.75) on Command-Reading and 0.76 (AUC 0.73) on Question-Answering. These findings demonstrate that multimodal behavioral cues captured through naturalistic interactions can reliably distinguish MCI from healthy aging, highlighting the limitations of traditional diagnostic paradigms and supporting the shift toward multimodal, real-world cognitive assessment frameworks.

</details>


### [193] [Replicable Dueling Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20908)
*Rohan Deb,Aadirupa Saha,Udaya Ghai,Karan Singh,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究可复现对偶赌博机，首次提出在共享内部随机性下以≥1-ρ概率复现相同比较对的亚线性遗憾算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶赌博机每轮依据历史噪声结果选择比较对，同一偏好下两次运行可能比较不同臂对；论文引入"可复现性"要求，需在共享内部随机、比较结果独立时保证每轮选出相同有序对，同时维持亚线性遗憾。

Method: 基于Beat-the-Mean框架（其分数随臂淘汰变化，且不保证最优臂得分最高）：(1) 在松弛随机传递性(γ≥1)与随机三角不等式下设计可复现算法；(2) 在强随机传递性下采用同步淘汰(simultaneous elimination)改进K依赖；(3) 针对线性对偶赌博机设计可复现算法。

Result: 松弛随机传递性下期望遗憾为Õ(γ^8 K^4 H_p/ρ^2)，其中H_p=Σ_{i=2}^K (P_{1i}-1/2)^{-1}；强随机传递性下提升为Õ(K^2 H_p/ρ^2)；线性对偶赌博机下为Õ(d^3√T/(c_μρ))。合成数据与语言模型偏好实验均验证可复现性与亚线性遗憾。

Conclusion: 论文给出首批具亚线性遗憾的可复现对偶赌博机算法，覆盖一般、强随机传递性及线性偏好三类设定，并通过实验验证可复现性与遗憾的亚线性增长。

Abstract: Dueling bandits choose each comparison based on earlier noisy outcomes, so two runs on the same preferences can compare different pairs. We study replicable dueling bandits, where two executions with shared internal randomness and independent comparison outcomes must select the same ordered pair in every round with probability at least $1-ρ$. Building on Beat-the-Mean, whose scores change as arms are eliminated and need not rank the best arm highest, we design the first such algorithms with sublinear regret. Under relaxed stochastic transitivity with parameter $γ\ge 1$ and the stochastic triangle inequality, we design an algorithm that achieves expected regret $\widetilde{O}(γ^8 K^4 H_{\mathrm{p}}/ρ^2)$, where $H_{\mathrm{p}}=\sum_{i=2}^{K}(P_{1i}-1/2)^{-1}$. Under strong stochastic transitivity, we design an algorithm based on simultaneous elimination that improves the dependence on $K$ and achieves $\widetilde{O}(K^2 H_{\mathrm{p}}/ρ^2)$ regret. For linear dueling bandits, we design an algorithm that achieves regret $\widetilde{O}(d^3\sqrt{T}/(c_μρ))$. Experiments on synthetic and language-model preferences show replicability, with sublinear regret.

</details>


### [194] [Recursive Offline Distillation via Entropy Gain Sampling and Answer Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20914)
*Andrey Goncharov,Daniil Vyazhev,Simon Epanov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种递归离线蒸馏方法，结合基于熵的采样与答案混合，在无法访问教师模型内部表示的情况下，将LLM推理链蒸馏至小模型，性能优于朴素蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者通常无法完整访问教师模型及其内部表示，这限制了蒸馏性能。因此需要一种不依赖教师内部信息的离线蒸馏方案。

Method: 递归离线蒸馏，包含两个组件：一是基于熵的采样（按与辅助中等模型70-72B的熵差加权采样推理链），二是答案混合（用单token真实答案补充思维轨迹）。在三个3-4B开源模型上按epoch执行。

Result: 在相当数据预算下，平均得分57.15±1.01，优于朴素蒸馏的51.61，且学习曲线更陡峭。同时给出蒸馏轨迹生成与归一化方法的对比分析。

Conclusion: 递归离线蒸馏结合熵采样与答案混合，可在受限访问条件下有效提升小模型推理能力，并为轨迹生成与归一化提供实证参考。

Abstract: Distillation of reasoning chains from Large Language Models (LLMs) into their smaller siblings is a standard training approach. Frequently, researchers do not have full access to a teacher model and its internal representations, which limits the resulting performance. We propose a recursive offline distillation method combining two components: entropy-based sampling for selective distillation and answer mixing. Specifically, for three open models (3-4B), at each epoch, we sample reasoning chains weighted by entropy difference with an auxiliary medium model (70-72B) and, next, supplement thinking traces with single-token ground truth answers. Our approach consistently outperforms naive distillation with comparable data budgets ($57.15 \pm 1.01$ vs 51.61 on average) and offers a much steeper learning curve. Additionally, we present a comparative analysis of methods to generate and normalize distillation traces.

</details>


### [195] [How to Train Your Little Dragon: Teaching a Small Language Model to Construct and Refine GPU Optimization Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20980)
*Annabelle Sujun Tang,Wei Tang,Songyao Jiang,Tianhui Li,Shengzhe Xu,Jiin Woo,Kaan Ozkara,Youngsuk Park,Zhen Jia,Hadi Esmaeilzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HOW TO TRAIN YOUR LITTLE DRAGON训练方法，教小语言模型生成优化脚本而非内核代码，通过监督微调与强化学习，8B模型在GPU内核优化上超越多个前沿大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译器变换之间存在交互作用，GPU内核优化需要在保持计算语义不变的前提下搜索能提升性能的变换组合。现有方法（如候选排序或直接生成内核代码）难以高效表达和复用这些优化变换，且任务特定训练对大型模型而言代价高昂。
