Method: 让模型生成"优化脚本"而非内核代码或候选排序；设计紧凑且无损的表示，其确定性解码可重建精确的编译器trace，保留变换参数与依赖关系；基于TVM MetaSchedule在54,116个不同workload上构建108,594个训练样本（60,236个用于从无优化内核生成优化脚本，48,358个用于在给定内核与部分优化脚本下改进脚本）；先监督微调，再加入带塑形奖励的强化学习，奖励结合正确性与实测性能，仅对数值正确的内核给予基于延迟的奖励。

Result: 在8B参数模型上，经监督微调与强化学习后，构造任务单次尝试正确率91.27%，改进任务92.01%；强化学习在102个构造任务中的88个、170个改进提示中的145个上产生比监督训练更快的内核。相比GPT-6 Astra、Opus 5、Kimi-K3、GLM-5.3等六个拥有数百亿至数万亿参数的前沿大模型，8B的LITTLE DRAGON实现了更高的单次尝试正确率与更好的内核性能。

Conclusion: 通过生成紧凑无损的优化脚本表示并结合监督微调与带正确性约束的强化学习，小型语言模型可在GPU内核优化任务上以更低成本超越远大于其规模的前沿大模型，表明任务特定训练与合适表示对编译器优化具有显著价值。

Abstract: Compiler transformations interact, making GPU kernel optimization a search for combinations that preserve the computation while improving performance. We introduce HOW TO TRAIN YOUR LITTLE DRAGON, a training method that teaches a small language model both to construct complete optimization scripts and to improve existing ones. Instead of ranking candidates or generating kernel code, our model generates optimization scripts. We devise a compact, lossless representation whose deterministic decoding reconstructs the exact compiler trace, preserving its transformation parameters and dependencies. We use a compiler framework TVM MetaSchedule to build training data on 54,116 distinct workloads, and construct 108,594 examples in two training sets: 60,236 teach the model to generate an optimized script from an unoptimized kernel, while 48,358 teach it to produce a better script given the kernel and a somewhat optimized script. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the model to construct and improve optimization scripts from these search-derived examples. We then add reinforcement learning with a shaped reward that combines correctness with measured performance, awarding a latency-based bonus only to numerically correct kernels. We apply this training method to a model with 8 billion parameters. After supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, the model achieves per-attempt correctness of 91.27% for construction and 92.01% for refinement, while reinforcement learning produces faster kernels than supervised training on 88 of 102 construction tasks and 145 of 170 refinement prompts. With only 8B parameters, our LITTLE DRAGON achieves higher per-attempt correctness and better kernel performance than six frontier LLMs: GPT-6 Astra, Opus 5, Kimi-K3, and GLM-5.3 among them, each with hundreds of billions to trillions of parameters, for which task-specific training is unavailable or expensive.

</details>


### [196] [Do As I Do, Not As I Say: On-Policy Distillation Inherits the Teacher in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20989)
*Frank F. Yang,Kefei Yan,Yiren Wang,Yiyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究受控比较了在策略蒸馏（OPD）与过滤式监督微调（SFT）在视觉语言模型后训练中的表现，发现OPD的有效性取决于教师模型在学生自生成轨迹上是否可靠，且会传递教师的持续失败模式（如不完整回答）给学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD被认为比SFT在数据效率上更有优势，因为它在学生自生成轨迹上施加教师监督；但教师在每个状态的监督如何影响学生学习行为尚不清楚，需要控制变量地探究。

Method: 在多个数据预算和教师可靠性不同的任务上，对OPD与过滤式SFT进行受控比较，分析两者如何影响学生准确率与完成行为；重点对比ChartQA（教师完成率99%）与Geometry3K（教师未完成率22%），并尝试从SFT学生初始化OPD。

Result: OPD仅在教师在学生生成轨迹上保持可靠时有效：ChartQA上仅用10%数据训练即接近全量SFT（差1.4个百分点）；Geometry3K上OPD比SFT低7.9个百分点，差距主要来自不完整回答；从SFT学生启动OPD会将完成率从76%降至62%，抹消SFT学到的完成行为；仅对教师示范做完整性过滤即可恢复SFT的大部分优势。

Conclusion: 在策略监督不仅传递教师知识，也会在学生会访问到的状态上传递持久的失败模式；OPD的可靠性高度依赖教师在其自身生成轨迹上的可靠性，数据效率优势需以教师可靠性为前提。

Abstract: Improving the reasoning capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) often relies on post-training with supervision from stronger models. Two common strategies are supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which trains on teacher-generated demonstrations, and on-policy distillation (OPD), which provides teacher feedback on trajectories generated by the student itself. While OPD can be more data-efficient by supervising student-generated trajectories, it is unclear how the teacher's supervision at each state affects the student's learned behavior. To study this question, we conduct a controlled comparison of OPD and filtered SFT across data budgets and tasks where the teacher exhibits different levels of reliability, and analyze how these differences shape student accuracy and completion behavior. Across the two tasks, we find that OPD is effective only when the teacher remains reliable on student-generated trajectories. On ChartQA, where the teacher completes 99% of its solutions, OPD trained on only 10% of the data comes within 1.4 accuracy points of full-data SFT. In contrast, on Geometry3K, where the teacher fails to complete 22% of its solutions, OPD underperforms SFT by 7.9 points, with most of the gap explained by incomplete responses. More strikingly, starting OPD from an SFT student reduces its completion rate from 76% to 62%, effectively erasing the completion behavior learned through SFT. Filtering teacher demonstrations for completeness alone recovers much of SFT's advantage. These results show that on-policy supervision can transfer not only teacher knowledge but also persistent failure modes on student-visited states. Code is available at https://github.com/ffy208/opd-inherits-teacher-vlm.

</details>


### [197] [Halve-Then-Greedy: Adaptive Sampling for Posterior Success with Near-Optimal Instance Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20997)
*Yinan Li,Kwang-Sung Jun,Kapilan Balagopalan,Tuan Nguyen,Yao Zhao,Abbas Zaidi,Houssam Nassif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定预算下的自适应实验，目标是在高斯bandit（高斯先验）中最大化"推荐设计的均值超过目标"的后验证据充分概率。作者提出Halve-Then-Greedy（HTG）算法，结合大规模初始均衡分配、逐次减半与后验贪心采样，并给出实例依赖的有限预算指数界与近最优样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的工作多聚焦于best-arm identification（最优臂识别），要求找到最优设计；但许多实际场景只关心推荐设计的均值是否超过某个目标阈值，无需找出最优臂。因此需要针对这一更弱但更实用的目标设计固定预算下的自适应实验方法，并刻画其样本复杂度。

Method: 针对高斯bandit与高斯先验，提出Halve-Then-Greedy（HTG）算法：先进行大规模初始均衡分配，随后执行逐次减半（successive halving），并进行后验贪心（posterior-greedy）采样。作者对HTG推导了实例依赖、有限预算下的后验失败概率指数上界，并作为对照分析了均匀采样（uniform sampling）与序贯减半（sequential halving）。

Result: 证明的指数界同时刻画了两个因素：与不利臂的分离度（separation from unfavorable arms）以及多个有利臂的存在（multiple favorable arms）。在single-positive-arm族上建立了渐近样本复杂度下界，HTG可匹配该下界至对数因子。进一步分析表明，均匀采样与序贯减半分别需要比HTG多线性因子与对数因子的样本。仿真实验验证了HTG的样本效率优势及其对实例结构的依赖。

Conclusion: HTG在固定预算自适应实验目标下具有样本效率优势，匹配近最优样本复杂度（差一个对数因子），且优于均匀采样和序贯减半。

Abstract: We study fixed-budget adaptive experimentation aimed at maximizing the probability of obtaining sufficient posterior evidence that a recommended design's mean performance exceeds a target. Unlike best-arm identification, this objective does not require finding the best design. For Gaussian bandits with a Gaussian prior, we propose \emph{Halve-Then-Greedy} (HTG), which combines a large initial balanced allocation, successive halving, and posterior-greedy sampling. We prove instance-dependent, finite-budget exponential bounds on posterior-failure probability that capture both separation from unfavorable arms and the presence of multiple favorable arms. On one-positive-arm families, we establish an asymptotic sample-complexity lower bound that HTG matches up to a logarithmic factor. We further analyze uniform sampling and sequential halving, revealing regimes where they require more samples than HTG by linear and logarithmic factors, respectively. Simulations illustrate HTG's sample-efficiency advantages and their dependence on instance structure.

</details>


### [198] [Imperfect Is Perfect: Learned Column Prefilling for Branch-Price-and-Cut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21001)
*Haoran Liu,Yu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"预填充+学习中心"的列生成加速流程：用神经网络生成非最优但有用的初始列池并预测Wentges平滑中心，从而大幅加速分支定价割平面求解器的根节点定价过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，初始列（热启动）会给分支定价割平面带来负面影响，可能使对偶产生偏差并拖慢根节点求解。作者希望挑战这一"民间共识"，探索在"列以正确方式不完美"时，预填充是否反而能加速根节点求解。

Method: 作者对比两种预填充策略：直接预载最优根LP支撑（理论上最好的已知列）与预载普通路径池（可不必属于任何最优解，存放于主问题之外并按当前平滑对偶定价）。由于列无需最优，生成列本身成为学习任务：一个神经边打分器引导构造式生成器在数秒内构建列池；第二个网络从实例文件预测Wentges平滑的初始中心及平滑定价查询方向，求解器自身更新不变。此外，围绕支配性检验为GPU重建桶图标记算法，并在CPU上做认证。

Result: 在RouteOpt（CVRP最先进精确求解器）上，该流程在300至1000个客户的95个实例中，达到相同认证根界所需根定价时间仅为原版RouteOpt的13.2%；仅预填充为26.1%，最优情况加速17.5倍。在不相关并行机调度的精确分支定价割平面求解器中，预填充加平滑与学习中心可认证全部25个基准实例，比求解器自身列生成快12倍（后者仅认证20个）。

Conclusion: 只要初始列以合适方式"不完美"，预填充非最优列池配合学习到的平滑中心可显著加速根节点求解；该方法在CVRP与调度两类问题上均获得显著加速，并提升了认证成功率。

Abstract: Column generation folklore warns that initial columns hurt: a warm start may bias the dual and slow the root of branch-price-and-cut. We show the opposite once the columns are imperfect in the right way. Preloading the support of the optimal root LP, the best columns one could know in advance, fails to finish on half of our hardest instances; a pool of ordinary routes that need not belong to any optimal solution, held outside the master and priced at the current smoothed dual, speeds the root up. Because the columns need not be optimal, producing them becomes a learning task: a neural edge scorer guides a constructive generator that builds the pool in seconds. A second network predicts the initial center of Wentges smoothing from the instance file, and with it the direction of the smoothed pricing queries; the solver's own updates run unchanged. With prefill and center in place, exact pricing dominates root pricing time, so we rebuild bucket-graph labeling around its dominance test for the GPU and certify on the CPU. Inside RouteOpt, the state-of-the-art exact solver for capacitated vehicle routing, the pipeline reaches the same certified root bound in 13.2% of vanilla RouteOpt's root pricing time over 95 instances with 300 to 1000 customers, prefill alone in 26.1%, and 17.5 times faster in the best case. In an exact branch-price-and-cut solver for unrelated parallel machine scheduling, prefill with smoothing and the learned center certifies all 25 benchmark instances, 12 times faster than the solver's own column generation, which certifies 20 of them.

</details>


### [199] [CausalEvo: Causal Skill Self-Evolution for Health LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21016)
*Yuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CausalEvo，一种通过因果估计刻意变更技能集相对效应来实现 LLM 智能体技能自演化的方法，在 MedCalc-Bench-Verified 上以更少训练 token 取得更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 健康智能体可从任务反馈中积累可复用文本技能（计算准则、测量时点、公式单位等），但决定保留哪些技能至关重要：有用规则改善后续回答，错误泛化会传播误差。因此需要一种能区分技能真实效用、避免错误泛化的保留机制。

Method: CausalEvo 在固定模型权重、反馈案例与规则数量的条件下，估计刻意变更技能集的相对效应。每步由冻结 LLM 提出候选规则，与现有技能池合并，使当前技能集保持为可行选项；在共享背景规则下进行配对技能替换，并在相同反馈案例上打分。用最小二乘法将分数差异转换为相对技能价值，保留排名靠前的规则，平局时偏向 incumbent。理论上给出决策损失界，分解为候选支持误差、近似误差与估计误差。

Result: 在六个 MedCalc-Bench-Verified 任务、120 个留出案例上，CausalEvo 达到 60.00% 准确率，高于 SkillOpt 的 55.83% 与无技能的 51.67%。在已记录训练配置中（使用不同优化预算），其训练 token 消耗比 SkillOpt 少 55.6%。收益集中于部分计算任务，同时在另一些任务上出现回归，配对准确率区间包含零。

Conclusion: CausalEvo 将任务反馈转化为对技能保留与替换的可控决策；尽管提升存在任务异质性与不确定性，其在准确率与训练成本上均优于基线，为 LLM 智能体技能自演化提供了因果化框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents for health can accumulate reusable textual skills from task feedback, such as calculation criteria, measurement time points, and formula units. Deciding which skills to retain is consequential: useful rules improve later answers, while faulty generalizations propagate errors. We propose CausalEvo, a method for causal skill self-evolution that estimates the relative effects of deliberately changed skill sets while holding the model weights, feedback cases, and rule count fixed. At each evolution step, a frozen LLM proposes candidate rules, which are pooled with the incumbent skills so that the current skill set remains a feasible choice. Paired skill replacements against shared background rules are scored on the same feedback cases; least squares converts the score differences into relative skill values, and the top-ranked rules are retained, favoring incumbents on ties. A decision-loss bound separates candidate support, approximation, and estimation errors. On six MedCalc-Bench-Verified tasks with 120 held-out cases, CausalEvo achieves 60.00% accuracy, compared with 55.83% for SkillOpt and 51.67% without skills. Across the recorded training configurations, which use different optimization budgets, it consumes 55.6% fewer training tokens than SkillOpt. Gains concentrate in particular calculations alongside regressions on others, and paired accuracy intervals include zero. CausalEvo turns task feedback into controlled decisions about skill retention and replacement. Project repository: https://github.com/txbrook/CausalEvo

</details>


### [200] [INCONVENIENT: A Benchmark to Assess LLM Generalization Capabilities from Convenience Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21038)
*Sara Rezaeimanesh,Chad Klochko,Kundan Thind,Mohammad Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 INCONVENIENT 基准，通过在训练分布中显式扰动特征—目标关系、同时保留原始总体分布作为部署环境，系统评估表格预测模型（含 LLM）对便利抽样偏差的鲁棒性，涵盖 200 个数据集、约 15,500 个偏移训练分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格预测模型通常假设训练数据能代表部署总体，但实际数据常因便利性、可得性或成本而被非随机采集，产生系统性抽样偏差，扭曲特征—目标关系并损害泛化。现有表格分布偏移基准多依赖自然域划分构造 OOD 场景，其偏移方向与强度不受控制、未被隔离，评估分布本身也未必代表目标总体，难以系统研究便利抽样偏差的影响。

Method: 提出 INCONVENIENT 基准：不依赖自然域划分，而是直接扰动训练分布中的特征—目标关系，并将原始总体分布作为部署环境。该设计可对关系偏移的方向与严重程度进行显式控制，从而系统评估模型（尤其可能编码总体级知识的 LLM）在便利抽样偏差下的鲁棒性。

Result: 基准包含 200 个表格分类与回归数据集，覆盖多种应用领域，生成约 15,500 个偏移训练分布，并导致显著的下游性能退化。

Conclusion: INCONVENIENT 更贴近“用非代表性样本开发模型、再部署于更广泛总体”的真实场景，为表格预测及 LLM 在便利抽样偏差下的鲁棒性提供了可控、可量化的评估平台。

Abstract: Machine learning models for tabular prediction typically assume that training data are representative of the population on which they will be deployed. In practice, however, data are often collected based on convenience, availability, or cost, resulting in systematic sampling biases that can distort feature--target relationships and undermine real-world generalization. Existing tabular distribution-shift benchmarks primarily construct out-of-distribution (OOD) settings by partitioning observations across naturally occurring domains. While such partitions may induce changes in P(Y | X), the resulting shifts are neither explicitly controlled nor isolated, and the evaluation distributions themselves are often not representative of the broader target population. We introduce INCONVENIENT, a benchmark designed to study robustness to convenience-sample bias. Rather than relying on naturally occurring domain partitions, INCONVENIENT directly perturbs feature--target relationships in the training distribution while preserving the original population distribution as the deployment environment. This setting more closely reflects real-world scenarios in which models are developed from unrepresentative samples and subsequently deployed on a broader population. The distinction is particularly relevant for large language models (LLMs), whose pretraining may encode population-level knowledge that extends beyond the relationships observed in a biased training dataset. Unlike existing distribution-shift benchmarks, INCONVENIENT provides explicit control over both the direction and severity of relationship shifts, enabling a systematic assessment of robustness to convenience sampling. The benchmark comprises 200 tabular classification and regression datasets spanning diverse application domains and generates around 15,500 shifted training distributions which lead to substantial downstream degradation.

</details>


### [201] [SALTO: Solver-Anchored Lifted Transfer Operators for Nonlinear Event Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21049)
*Qi Wang,Rylie Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SALTO框架，基于Koopman算子理论构建可复用的提升伴随模型，用于推断有限振幅源，在多个非线性偏微分方程系统上显著优于经典伴随方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型动力学模型在预测轨迹时较为准确，但微分或转置时产生的灵敏度不可靠，限制了其在逆问题和科学决策中的应用。经典伴随方法能提供精确的局部导数，但单一伴随无法表征系统对一族有限振幅扰动的非线性响应。

Method: SALTO在提升空间中将非线性动力学近似为线性传播算子，其转置可在单次求解中将各传感器反向传播，形成可复用的提升伴随。为获得模拟器的精确局部行为，SALTO施加三项结构约束：将物理状态动力学锚定到模拟器的线性化传播算子；对学习到的可观测量进行中心化，使其在参考状态处二阶消失；使用块三角提升传播算子。

Result: 在三个非线性偏微分方程系统上，SALTO对有限振幅源测量的预测误差为2-8%，而经典伴随误差为41-165%；从稀疏传感器中恢复源位置和强度的精度接近通过求解器反演的效果；对两个相互作用源的联合后验采样在反应扩散问题上以二十分之一的成本匹配求解器后验。单个训练模型即可服务新的传感器布局和测量时刻，仅需一次反向求解。

Conclusion: SALTO通过锚定模拟器线性化动力学并让学习观测仅承担有限振幅修正，构造出可复用的提升伴随，在保持与经典伴随一致的低振幅极限的同时，有效处理有限振幅源推断，兼顾精度与计算效率。

Abstract: Learned dynamical models can accurately predict trajectories while producing unreliable sensitivities when differentiated or transposed, limiting their use in inverse problems and scientific decision-making. Classical adjoints provide exact local derivatives, but a single adjoint cannot represent the nonlinear response of a system to a family of finite-amplitude perturbations. We introduce SALTO (Solver-Anchored Lifted Transfer Operators), a Koopman-based framework for inferring finite-amplitude sources. In the lifted space, the nonlinear dynamics are approximated by a linear propagator, and its transpose carries each sensor backward in a single solve. The result is a reusable lifted adjoint that predicts the measurement produced by any candidate localized event with one encoder pass and an inner product. To give the model the simulator's exact local behavior, SALTO imposes three structural constraints: it anchors the physical-state dynamics to the simulator's linearized propagator, centers the learned observables so that they vanish to second order at the reference state, and uses a block-triangular lifted propagator. These constraints guarantee that the model reduces to the simulator's tangent dynamics and classical adjoint as the amplitude vanishes, while the learned observables carry only the finite-amplitude correction. On three nonlinear PDE systems, SALTO predicts the measurements of finite-amplitude sources to within 2-8% where the classical adjoint is in error by 41-165%, recovers source position and intensity from sparse sensors nearly as accurately as inversion through the solver, and samples the joint posterior of two interacting sources, matching the solver's posterior on reaction-diffusion at a twentieth of its cost. One trained model serves new sensor layouts and measurement times with a single backward solve.

</details>


### [202] [Bellman-Consistent Trajectory RL for LLM Arithmetic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21060)
*Lianfa Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bellman一致性的轨迹强化学习方法TBRM-RLOO，将稀疏终局奖励转为稠密token级信号，结合RLOO组策略降低梯度方差并用hindsight重标记加速中间学习，在Countdown任务上显著优于GRPO及SFT/DPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM算术推理中终端奖励稀疏，导致优化瓶颈与梯度方差大，学习信号难以有效传导至中间推理步骤。

Method: 提出Trajectory Bellman Residual Minimization (TBRM)，将稀疏奖励转化为稠密token级信号；引入Reinforce Leave-One-Out (RLOO)组策略降低Bellman残差的梯度方差；采用hindsight goal relabeling加速中间阶段学习。

Result: 在Countdown任务上，缓解优化瓶颈、稳定梯度方差优于GRPO；Pass@1提升至0.55–0.64，Pass@16达SOTA的76.0–84.0%，超过SFT、IPO/DPO、RLOO基线，并展现优异的单样本效率，通过test-time scaling突破性能平台期。

Conclusion: Bellman一致的轨迹RL框架能有效将稀疏奖励稠密化并稳定训练，在算术推理任务上取得更优精度与效率，并可借助推理时扩展进一步突破性能上限。

Abstract: To address sparse terminal rewards in LLM arithmetic reasoning, we propose a Bellman-consistent trajectory RL method. Our framework introduces Trajectory Bellman Residual Minimization (TBRM) to transform sparse rewards into dense token-level signals. We integrate a Reinforce Leave-One-Out (RLOO) group strategy to reduce gradient variance of Bellman residuals and use hindsight goal relabeling to accelerate intermediate learning. Evaluated on the Countdown task, our method alleviates optimization bottlenecks and stabilizes gradient variance significantly better than GRPO. The TBRM-RLOO framework boosts Pass@1 to 0.55--0.64 and achieves a state-of-the-art Pass@16 accuracy of 76.0--84.0\%, outperforming the SFT, IPO/DPO, and RLOO baselines. Our approach demonstrates exceptional single-sample efficiency and successfully circumvents standard performance plateaus via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [203] [StochLOB: Heavy-Tailed Score Matching for Robust Limit Order Book Trend Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21081)
*Arshia Abolghasemi,Seyed Reza Tavakoli,Parsa Naderi,Nazanin Mirzaei,Behrad Jahani,Saba Yousefzadeh,Tahereh Javaheri,Mohammad Hosein Rohban,Gholamali Aminian,Ali Habibnia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 STOCHLOB，一种仅用于训练的得分匹配框架，通过将前向扩散的加噪分布由布朗高斯噪声替换为跳跃-莱维噪声与截断 α-稳定噪声，使模型在不改变推理阶段确定性预测器的前提下适应重尾、突发且带跳跃的限价订单簿数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于得分的扩散方法通常使用布朗高斯噪声扰动数据，对应轻尾前向随机微分方程。但订单簿订单流具有重尾、突发和跳跃特征，高斯假设与市场时间序列的统计特性不匹配，构成较差的抽象建模。

Method: STOCHLOB 仅改变前向扩散的污染律，部署预测器保持确定性。加噪过程采用两种非高斯、受 SDE 启发的替代方案：通过复合泊松方差混合实现的跳跃-莱维噪声，以及通过正稳定高斯尺度混合实现的截断 α-稳定噪声，从而在训练中注入有限跳跃与重尾残差，而无需在推理时进行反向时间扩散采样。所得得分目标的径向因子 h_t(r) 可解释为后验期望污染精度。

Result: 在按时间顺序留出的 Coinbase 限价订单簿数据上，所提变体在未污染的 BTC 与山寨币测试时段保持竞争力，并改善了若干已实现尾部窗口压力诊断指标。此外，一项探索性的飞书 A 股迁移研究进一步检验了概率输出在交易成本与交易规则约束下是否仍然可用。

Conclusion: 改变前向扩散的污染律（引入跳跃与重尾噪声）可在不增加推理期扩散采样的条件下提升模型对订单簿重尾、跳跃特征的适应性，在分布内测试保持竞争力并改善尾部压力诊断，其向 A 股等市场的迁移可用性仍需在交易成本与规则约束下进一步验证。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion methods usually perturb data with Brownian Gaussian noise, corresponding to a light-tailed forward stochastic differential equation (SDE). For market time series this SDE can be a poor abstraction: limit order book (LOB) order flow is heavy-tailed, bursty, and occasionally jump-like. We study STOCHLOB, a training-only score-matching framework that changes the forward diffusion corruption law while leaving the deployed predictor deterministic. Instead of Brownian-only Gaussian corruption, the noising process uses two non-Gaussian SDE-inspired alternatives: jump-Lévy noise through a compound-Poisson variance mixture, and clipped $α$-stable noise through a positive-stable Gaussian scale mixture. These corruptions inject finite jumps and heavy-tailed residuals into training without requiring reverse-time diffusion sampling at inference. The resulting score target has a useful interpretation: its radial factor $h_t(r)$ is the posterior expected corruption precision, so large residuals are weighted according to the latent corruption scale that most plausibly generated them. On chronologically held-out Coinbase LOB data, the proposed variants remain competitive on uncorrupted BTC and altcoin test horizons and improve several realized tail-window stress diagnostics. An exploratory Feishu A-share transfer study further tests whether the probability outputs remain usable under transaction costs and trading-rule constraints. Code and configuration files are available at https://github.com/ArshiAbolghasemi/Penny.

</details>


### [204] [SIEVE: Overcoming Topological Obstruction in Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21087)
*Jongann Lee,Sun Woo Park,Yun Young Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了3D点云掩码自编码与SE(3)等变网络之间的理论不兼容性，并提出SIEVE框架，通过球面投影掩码构建等变自监督学习，避免拓扑坍缩并提升下游任务泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码自编码应用于3D点云时会与SE(3)等变网络产生理论冲突：掩码会引发拓扑坍缩，迫使模型在缺乏参考系的情况下"幻觉"出朝向信息。作者希望解决这一根本性障碍，实现兼具等变性与自监督能力的点云表示学习。

Method: 提出SIEVE（Scalar Invariant Extraction Via Equivariance），一个SE(3)等变自监督框架。其核心是球面投影掩码（spherical projection mask），在保留全局位姿的同时混淆局部语义，从构造上规避拓扑坍缩问题，将点云几何压缩为不变的标量嵌入。

Result: 实验验证SIEVE成功规避了所预测的坍缩现象（而恒定掩码与噪声掩码均失败）。在下游点云任务中，所生成嵌入优于基于坐标的基线方法与手工几何描述子，且在对未见形状类别的泛化上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 点云掩码自编码与SE(3)等变网络存在理论不兼容，标准掩码导致拓扑坍缩；通过球面投影掩码可从构造上绕过该障碍，SIEVE框架有效实现了等变自监督表示学习并带来更好的泛化性能。

Abstract: We identify a theoretical incompatibility between 3D point cloud masked autoencoding and SE(3)-equivariant networks. We prove that standard masking triggers a topological collapse, effectively forcing the model to hallucinate orientation without a reference frame. To resolve this, we introduce SIEVE (Scalar Invariant Extraction Via Equivariance), an SE(3)-equivariant self-supervised framework that compresses point cloud geometry into invariant scalar embeddings. At its core is the spherical projection mask, which preserves global pose while obfuscating local semantics, sidestepping the obstruction by construction. Experiments confirm that SIEVE circumvents the predicted collapse where constant and noise masks fail. On downstream point cloud tasks, the resulting embeddings improve over coordinate-based baselines and hand-crafted geometric descriptors, with the largest gains on generalization to unseen shape categories.

</details>


### [205] [Spectral-Spatial Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21114)
*Haotian Ma,Ruqi Yang,Philip Townsend*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出光谱-空间解释方法SSI，为多光谱数据在位置-通道网格上提供全网格归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事后图像解释多将空间热图与全局通道评分分离，难以刻画多光谱数据中通道相关性随空间位置局部变化且具有物理意义的特点。

Method: SSI对每个空间位置固定其周围观测上下文，在该位置通道上构建条件合作博弈，并应用Shapley值求解，得到位置-通道(g,c)的全网格归因矩阵；与对外部空间联盟取平均的Owen式分层核算不同。

Result: 在HLS Burn Scar上，SSI在多项保留归因指标上优于MC-Owen估计器，且评估预算仅约6%；在CUB-200、EuroSAT、HLS Burn Scar上，在逐通道均值与零基线两种设定下，SSI在密集、平坦和rank-1基线上取得最佳联合删除/插入AUC，光谱命中率与通道删除最强，在两个带标注数据集上空间定位领先。

Conclusion: SSI以局部Shapley博弈实现全光谱-空间归因，兼具位置内Shapley公平性，且其通道归因聚合可精确还原位置级上下文对比，为多光谱模型提供更精细且高效的解释。

Abstract: Post-hoc image explanations usually show where a model looks using spatial heatmaps or indicate which channels matter using global channel scores. These separate views are limiting for multispectral data because channel relevance is often spatially localized and physically meaningful. We introduce Spectral-Spatial Interpretation (SSI), a full-grid attribution method over position-channel players (g,c). For each spatial position, SSI fixes the observed surrounding context, defines a conditional game over the channels at that position, and applies the Shapley value to this local game. The result is a full spectral-spatial attribution matrix rather than a single heatmap or a global channel ranking. SSI has a clear local interpretation. It satisfies Shapley fairness within each position, and aggregating its channel attributions exactly recovers the position-level contextual contrast obtained by replacing that position with the baseline. SSI also differs from context-averaged Owen-style hierarchical accounting, which uses the same position-channel grouping but averages over external spatial coalitions. On HLS Burn Scar, SSI outperforms an MC-Owen estimator across retained attribution metrics while using only about 6% of its evaluation budget. Across CUB-200, EuroSAT, and HLS Burn Scar under both per-channel mean and zero baselines, SSI achieves the best joint deletion and insertion AUC over dense, flat, and rank-1 baselines, yields the strongest spectral hit rates and channel deletion, and leads spatial localization on both annotated datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/AtheavenMa/Spectral-Spatial-Interpretation.

</details>


### [206] [DST-Net: Decomposed Spatio-Temporal Network for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21123)
*Yaohua Zha,Hai Wu,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于序列分解的差异化建模架构，针对趋势与季节分量分别设计潜图模块与高斯多尺度注意力，在多真实基准上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度时序预测模型虽普遍采用序列分解，但对所有子序列使用同质化建模策略，忽视了不同分量的固有归纳偏置。作者识别出趋势分量具有跨通道主导的空间耦合性但存在明显时间冗余，季节分量具有局部时间演化特征却易受注意力分散影响。

Method: 提出解耦架构并按分量特性定制建模：趋势分支采用潜图模块（Latent Graph Block），通过潜图建模捕获全局拓扑依赖与系统级协同变化；季节分支引入高斯多尺度注意力机制，注入可学习高斯先验施加结构约束，使模型聚焦多粒度局部上下文，过滤全局噪声并捕捉局部演化相位。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验证明了所提模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 差异化、分量定制的建模策略优于同质化序列分解范式，为时序预测中趋势与季节分量的解耦建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Series decomposition has emerged as a widely acknowledged paradigm in deep time series forecasting models. However, existing approaches predominantly employ a homogeneous modeling strategy for all subsequences, thereby overlooking the distinct inductive biases inherent in different components. We identify that the trend component exhibits dominant spatial coupling across channels but carries significant temporal redundancy, whereas the seasonal component is characterized by local temporal evolution but remains susceptible to attention distraction. To address this, we propose a novel disentangled architecture that tailors specific modeling strategies to align with the intrinsic properties of each component. For the trend branch, we design a Latent Graph Block, employing latent graph modeling to capture global topological dependencies and system-wide co-movements. Conversely, for the seasonal branch, we introduce a Gaussian Multi-scale Attention mechanism to mitigate attention distraction. By injecting learnable Gaussian priors, we impose structural constraints that compel the model to focus on multi-granular local contexts, effectively filtering out global noise and accurately capturing local evolutionary phases. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed model.

</details>


### [207] [Noise-Space Reward Alignment for Few-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21125)
*Woocheol Shin,Taeyoung Yun,Jiwon Sung,Hyeongyu Kang,Jeongmin Park,Jaewoo Lee,Kyuil Sim,Dohyun Kim,Yutong He,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Noise Policy Optimization (NPO)，将少步生成模型的对齐问题转化为在初始噪声空间上学一个摊销流策略，冻结生成器，使高斯噪声映射到奖励倾斜噪声分布，从而在训练中吸收搜索成本，推理仅增加轻量噪声采样步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步生成模型虽能一次或少数几次采样生成高质量样本，但难以在仅有黑盒奖励的情况下引导其生成符合目标属性的样本。直接 RL 微调会导致严重的奖励过优化，测试时扩展又会牺牲少步生成的效率优势，两者在少步情形下均不适用。

Method: 学习初始噪声空间上的摊销流策略，将高斯噪声输运至奖励倾斜的噪声分布；由于生成器冻结，该噪声通过生成器的推前分布恰为奖励倾斜的输出分布。训练采用基于 ELBO 的似然估计，仅需采样的端点而非模拟轨迹，提升样本效率。推理时在预训练模型之上仅增加轻量噪声采样。

Result: 在图像、语言和蛋白质结构生成任务上，NPO 持续取得优于直接微调的奖励—质量权衡，并以比测试时搜索方法低 16 至 549 倍的推理成本达到相当效果。

Conclusion: 冻结生成器可有效利用预训练模型中编码的知识，获得比直接微调更好的奖励—质量权衡；摊销策略将搜索成本转移到训练阶段，兼顾对齐效果与少步生成效率，是少步生成模型黑盒对齐的有效方案。

Abstract: Few-step generative models produce high-quality samples in one or a few evaluations. However, steering them toward desired properties remains difficult, especially when only black-box reward evaluations are available. While one could apply approaches developed for iterative diffusion and flow-based models, such as direct RL fine-tuning or test-time scaling, we find that neither transfers well to the few-step regime: direct fine-tuning suffers from severe reward over-optimization, and test-time scaling sacrifices the efficiency advantage of few-step generation. We propose Noise Policy Optimization (NPO), which explicitly targets the alignment problem for few-step generative models. The key idea is to learn an amortized flow policy over the initial noise space of the generator. The trained policy transports Gaussian noise to a reward-tilted noise distribution, whose pushforward distribution through the frozen generator is exactly the reward-tilted output distribution. By keeping the generator frozen, we can effectively utilize the knowledge encoded in the pretrained model, yielding a better reward-quality trade-off than direct fine-tuning. The amortized policy moves the search cost into training, so inference adds only a lightweight noise sampling step on top of the pretrained model. To improve sample efficiency during training, we use ELBO-based likelihood estimation that requires only sampled endpoints rather than simulated trajectories. Across image, language, and protein structure generation, NPO consistently achieves a better reward-quality trade-off than direct fine-tuning, and matches test-time search methods with 16 to 549 times lower inference cost.

</details>


### [208] [Predicting Live Birth After Fresh Embryo Transfer in IVF/ICSI: Differential ICM/Trophectoderm Morphology Encoding and a Hybrid Attention--Logistic Regression Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21134)
*Abdul Raheem Shahzad,Jinxin Chen,Ming Zhang,Juan Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建单中心IVF/ICSI精选数据集，提出HAN+LR集成模型预测鲜胚移植活产，测试集AUC-ROC达0.710，优于经典ML、TabPFN与文本化语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲜胚移植前预测活产可支持IVF/ICSI的共同决策，但常规临床数据规模小、异质性强，且含路径依赖变量，易导致结局泄漏。研究旨在构建精选的单中心数据集，并开发适用于小样本表格临床数据的预测模型。

Method: 纳入616个鲜胚移植周期（251活产、365非活产），含临床、实验室与胚胎学预测因子，胚胎形态采用内细胞团与滋养层差异化加权编码。经KNN插补、稳健缩放并筛选50个特征后，构建混合注意力网络（HAN，即可学习特征注意力门控加紧凑MLP），以及验证集调权的HAN+逻辑回归（LR）集成。在留出测试集上与经典机器学习、TabPFN和文本序列化语言模型对比，以AUC-ROC为主要指标。

Result: HAN+LR集成取得最高测试AUC-ROC（0.710；准确率0.710，F1 0.630），优于HAN主干（0.700）、逻辑回归（0.667）、TabPFN+LR混合（0.666）和TabPFN（0.650）。文本序列化语言模型接近随机水平（AUC≤0.61）。差异化加权的滋养层特征在模型间共识重要性中排名最高。

Conclusion: 紧凑注意力网络与线性模型的组合在小样本临床队列上优于各单一组件及强基础模型基线。精选数据集与HAN+LR集成可为IVF/ICSI活产预测提供透明、可复现的基础，下一步为外部验证。

Abstract: Predicting live birth before fresh embryo transfer could support shared decision-making in IVF/ICSI, but routine clinical data are small, heterogeneous, and contain pathway-dependent variables prone to outcome leakage. We aimed to construct a curated single-centre IVF/ICSI dataset and develop a prediction model suited to small tabular clinical data. \textit{Materials and Methods:} We assembled 616 fresh embryo-transfer cycles (251 live birth, 365 non-live birth) with clinical, laboratory, and embryological predictors, and embryo morphology encoded with a novel differential weighting of the inner cell mass and trophectoderm. After KNN imputation, robust scaling, and selection of 50 features, we developed a Hybrid Attention Network (HAN), a learnable feature-attention gate followed by a compact multilayer perceptron, and a HAN + logistic-regression (LR) ensemble with a validation-tuned weight. Models were benchmarked against classical machine learning, TabPFN, and text-serialized language models on a held-out test set; AUC-ROC was the primary metric. \textit{Results:} The HAN + LR ensemble achieved the highest test AUC-ROC (0.710; accuracy 0.710, F1 0.630), ahead of the HAN backbone alone (0.700), logistic regression (0.667), the TabPFN + LR hybrid (0.666), and TabPFN (0.650). Text-serialized language models performed near chance (AUC $\le$ 0.61). The differentially weighted trophectoderm feature ranked highest by consensus importance across models. \textit{Discussion:} A compact attention network combined with a linear model outperformed both components and strong foundation-model baselines on a small clinical cohort. \textit{Conclusion:} The curated dataset and HAN + LR ensemble offer a transparent, reproducible basis for IVF/ICSI live-birth prediction; external validation is the next step.

</details>


### [209] [Language Models Share a Readout Bottleneck in Working Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21176)
*Dongyu Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究区分了LLM在N-back任务中工作记忆限制的两种解释，发现限制源于干扰而非注意力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在N-back任务上复现了人类工作记忆的特征（表现随记忆负载N增加而下降，错误集中在lure项上）。作者想探究这种共享的行为特征是否反映共享的机制，并区分两种候选解释：精度账户（softmax注意力无法集中于N个位置前的项目）与干扰账户（注意力选对了目标，但竞争项目的内容破坏了决策）。

Method: 在15个来自三个家族（7000万至140亿参数）的预训练LLM以及从头训练的最小Transformer上进行解离实验。通过分析注意力头指向、线性探针解码目标与lure位置信息、锐化注意力、抑制非目标项目的字母身份等操作进行检验。

Result: 在所有三个家族中，承担任务的注意力头在大多数漏报时也关注目标；线性探针能以近乎完美精度解码匹配字母出现在目标还是lure位置，即使模型对lure误报时亦然，尽管模型自身决策几乎无法区分二者。锐化注意力带来的增益可忽略，而抑制非目标项目的字母身份在几乎所有模型-负载条件下都提升了敏感度d'。较大模型中任务分散到更多注意力头，去除干扰帮助更大。在最小Transformer中，精度限制单个注意力层，但第二层可移除该约束。

Conclusion: LLM的工作记忆限制在于在干扰下读出项目内容，而非定位项目位置，这与人类工作记忆的干扰账户一致。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) reproduce a human working-memory signature on the N-back task: performance declines as memory load N increases, and errors concentrate on lures, items that repeat the current stimulus at a neighboring but incorrect serial position. We ask whether this shared behavioral signature reflects a shared mechanism. The literature offers two candidate accounts. On a precision account, softmax attention cannot concentrate its weight on the item N positions back, so adjacent items are retrieved instead. On an interference account, attention selects the correct item, but the content of competing items corrupts the decision. We dissociate these accounts in 15 pretrained LLMs from three families (70 million to 14 billion parameters) and in minimal transformers trained from scratch. In all three families, the attention heads that carry the task attend to the target on most missed matches. Linear probes decode near-perfectly whether a matching letter occurred at the target or at a lure position, even on trials where the model false-alarms to the lure, although its own decisions barely distinguish the two. Sharpening attention yields negligible gains, whereas suppressing the letter identity of non-target items improves sensitivity (d') in nearly all model-by-load conditions. In larger models the task is spread over more heads, and removing interference helps more. In minimal transformers, precision limits a single attention layer, but a second layer removes this constraint. The working-memory limit of LLMs therefore lies in reading items out under interference rather than in locating them, consistent with interference accounts of human working memory.

</details>


### [210] [Judging the Judges: Confidence and Disagreement in Rubrics as Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21179)
*Michael Tian-Yue Liu,Anne Harrington,Trevor Darrell,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究RaR训练中评判者（judge）选择的影响，并提出RaRE评判者集成策略（软Likert奖励+分歧丢弃掩码），在HealthBench和GPQA Diamond上优于所有单评判者RaR变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rubrics as Rewards（RaR）借助LLM评判者按结构化评分标准打分，将强化学习扩展到不可验证领域。既有工作多聚焦于改进评分标准或强化单一评判者，而对RaR训练中如何使用不同单一或多个评判者关注不足。作者首先发现：仅评判者身份不同就会导致训练得分出现显著差异，因此评判者集成值得系统研究。

Method: 作者在RaR-Medicine和RaR-Science上训练Qwen3-4B-Base，比较五类前沿与开源评判者，验证评判者身份带来的分数差异。随后提出RaRE（Rubrics as Rewards with Ensembles），包含两个机制：（1）软Likert奖励，利用评判者logprobs对更有信心的评判者赋予更高权重；（2）分歧丢弃掩码，将高分歧的rollout从GRPO更新中剔除。

Result: 评判者身份单独即可造成训练得分的显著波动。简单地平均多个评判者分数无法超过最佳单评判者，而RaRE在HealthBench和GPQA Diamond上均优于所有单评判者RaR变体；按主题分析显示RaRE继承了各组成评判者的互补优势。

Conclusion: 评判者集成是提升RaR的一个有前景的方向，且集成设计（置信度加权与分歧过滤）是关键，而非简单地聚合评判者分数。

Abstract: Rubrics as Rewards (RaR) extends reinforcement learning to non-verifiable domains by using an LLM judge to score responses against a structured rubric. Prior work has focused on improving the rubric or strengthening the single judge, with comparatively little attention to how different single or multiple judges might be used during RaR training. We first show that judge identity alone produces a substantial spread in scores when training Qwen3-4B-Base on RaR-Medicine and RaR-Science across five frontier and open-weight judges. Building on this, we propose RaRE (Rubrics as Rewards with Ensembles), a novel judge ensemble strategy for RaR with two mechanisms: a soft-Likert reward that uses judge logprobs to weight more confident judges more heavily, and a drop-on-disagreement mask that excludes high-disagreement rollouts from the GRPO update. We find that this design is critical: while a naive average of judge scores fails to outperform the best single judge, RaRE outperforms every single-judge RaR variant on both HealthBench and GPQA Diamond, with per-theme analysis showing that it inherits the complementary strengths of its constituent judges. Our findings establish judge ensembling as a compelling axis for improving RaR.

</details>


### [211] [Sensor Diagnosis for Fuel and Emissions Monitoring in Heavy-Duty Diesel Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21188)
*Tejas Dahiya,Saurabh Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于健康数据学习关系的柴油机传感器故障诊断与燃油修正流程，在重型柴油机217个稳态工况点上用5种合成故障类型验证，诊断组覆盖率77.3%（正确率92.1%），选择性燃油修正使燃烧CO2计算的平均绝对误差降低83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障传感器读数会扭曲柴油机燃油消耗与CO2排放估计，需要自动诊断故障并修正燃油读数，以提升排放核算准确性且不干扰正常传感器读数。

Method: 利用健康测量数据学习传感器间关系。诊断流程：将疑似故障读数替换为估计的健康值，检验该替换是否能使模型更准确预测另一传感器记录值，据此判定故障类型并决定是否报告；在重型柴油机217个稳态工况点、5种合成故障类型上评估，训练时剔除选定转速的全部测量，共测试31,500个含20工况点的故障注入组。燃油修正流程：在单工况点采用不同传感器选择规则，检验每个替换对全部6个传感器最大预测误差的影响；需满足故障报警且转速扭矩接近训练条件，仅当替换使最大预测误差的降低幅度显著优于其他传感器时才替换燃油读数。

Result: 在31,500个故障注入组中，77.3%的组给出诊断，其中92.1%诊断正确。假设1%的一小时场景存在故障、传感器与故障类型等概率、各测量工况时间相等时，所选燃油修正使计算燃烧CO2的平均绝对误差降低83.8%。

Conclusion: 在该发动机图谱上，学习得到的关系可支持完整的传感器诊断与燃油修正，在保留未受影响读数的同时改善排放核算。

Abstract: Faulty sensor readings can distort estimates of fuel use and carbon dioxide emissions from diesel engines. We evaluate two procedures that use relationships learned from healthy measurements to diagnose faults and guide fuel correction. The diagnosis procedure tests whether replacing a suspected faulty reading with an estimated healthy value helps a model predict another sensor's recorded reading more accurately. It then predicts the fault type and decides whether to report the diagnosis. We evaluate five synthetic fault types on 217 steady-state operating points from a heavy-duty diesel engine, excluding all measurements at selected engine speeds from training. Across 31,500 fault-injected groups of 20 operating points, it issues diagnoses for 77.3% of groups, with 92.1% of diagnoses issued on these groups correct. The fuel correction procedure uses a different sensor-selection rule at individual operating points, testing how each replacement changes the largest prediction error across all six sensors. Fuel correction requires a fault alarm and engine speed and torque close to the training conditions. A fuel reading is replaced only when its replacement reduces the largest prediction error sufficiently more than replacements for the other sensors. Assuming faults in 1% of one-hour scenarios, equally likely sensors and fault types, and equal time at each measured condition, selected fuel corrections reduce mean absolute error in calculated combustion CO2 by 83.8%. On this engine map, learned relationships support complete sensor diagnoses and fuel corrections that improve emissions accounting while preserving unaffected readings.

</details>


### [212] [Resisting Harmful Instructions through Training-Free Weight Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21189)
*Ali Ebrahimpour-Boroojeny,Xiaocong Yang,Samuel Schapiro,Zahra Borhani,Francisco R. Ortega,Yujia Zheng,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的对比式权重编辑方法，使指令跟随模型降低对特定不良指令的顺从性，且提示层面的改动无法撤销该权重修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令跟随语言模型会顺从与预期行为和安全对齐相冲突的请求，而基于提示的防护可被修改或删除指令绕过，因此需要一种更持久的干预手段。

Method: 免训练的权重编辑：每个目标行为仅需对少量提示对做前向传播，计算对比信号后直接写入模型权重；无需梯度训练。

Result: 在五个指令微调模型、两类目标行为上评估：说谎基准上诚实回答率较最强基线分别提升6.2与4.6个百分点；七种提示格式下优于单层激活引导；性别歧视回答率从57.9%降至24.3%，叠加公平提示后进一步降至11.9%。

Conclusion: 该对比式权重编辑是跨两个行为域有效的免训练干预，可独立使用，也可与基于提示的防护协同。

Abstract: Instruction-following language models can comply with requests that conflict with intended behaviors and safety alignment. We introduce a training-free weight-editing approach for reducing compliance with targeted undesirable instructions. Our method needs only forward passes on a small set of prompt pairs per target behavior, and because the edit is stored in the model's weights, changing or deleting instructions in the prompt cannot undo the weight modification. We evaluate the approach on two target harmful behaviors across five instruction-tuned models: instructed falsehood and instructed sexism. On two instructed-lying benchmarks, it improves mean honest response rates by 6.2 and 4.6 percentage points over the strongest alternative, significantly on three of the five models and within noise on the other two. In a controlled study of seven prompt formats, it maintains higher honest-response rates than single-layer activation steering when orders to lie arrive in single-turn formats not used to construct the edit. For instructed sexism, it reduces the mean sexist-response rate from 57.9% to 24.3%; combining the edit with a fairness prompt reduces it further to 11.9%. These results establish our contrast-based weight editing approach as an effective training-free intervention in two distinct behavioral domains, supporting its use both independently and alongside prompt-based safeguards.

</details>


### [213] [Poisoning the corpora of protein design systems during pre-training and retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21200)
*Charalampos Koilakos,Kimon-Antonios Provatas,Michail Patsakis,Candace SY Chan,Georgios A. Pavlopoulos,Ilias Georgakopoulos-Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究针对蛋白质设计系统的两种纯数据投毒攻击：一是污染自回归蛋白质语言模型的预训练语料，二是污染文本引导蛋白质生成器的检索语料库；结果显示攻击可在不改变模型权重或常规行为的前提下植入持久、序列特异的后门行为或重定向生成结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质基础模型日益支持新生物序列生成，但其对大规模公开数据集的依赖为对抗性操纵其学习与检索素材提供了可乘之机，需要评估数据投毒对生成式蛋白质设计系统的安全威胁。

Method: （1）预训练投毒：自行训练3亿参数的ProtGPT2与ProGen2变体，注入含两种触发子之一加三种载荷之一的有毒蛋白质序列，在均匀与递增（ramped）投毒调度下考察后门生成。（2）检索投毒：在不修改检索系统权重与解码过程的前提下插入记录，测试单条查询感知记录与1500条通用查询无关记录的检索劫持效果。

Result: 预训练攻击中两种架构均获得高度可靠的触发依赖生成；递增调度下最早多数激活仅需约200条污染样本，均匀调度下约5000条。检索攻击中，单条查询感知记录在58%的模板化请求和97%的自由文本请求中进入top-16；成功投毒检索后62%请求生成改变序列，19–21%通过profile-HMM同源获得插入结构域；1500条通用记录亦实现显著检索捕获与结构域转移，同时普通模型行为基本保持。

Conclusion: 对抗性蛋白质序列贡献可在预训练阶段建立持久、序列特异的行为，或在推理阶段重定向蛋白质生成，因此蛋白质序列数据的完整性与生物学来源可追溯性应被视为生成式蛋白质设计系统的关键安全保障。

Abstract: Protein foundation models increasingly support the generation of novel biological sequences, yet their reliance on large public datasets creates opportunities for adversarial manipulation of the material they learn from and retrieve. We investigate two data-only poisoning attacks against protein design systems, targeting the pre-training corpus of autoregressive protein language models and the retrieval corpus of a text-guided protein generator. For the first, we pre-trained ProtGPT2 and ProGen2 variants of 300 million parameters, introducing poisoned protein sequences containing one of two triggers followed by one of three payloads. Across all poisoned runs, both architectures acquired highly reliable trigger-dependent generation under uniform and ramped poisoning schedules. Under ramped exposure, the earliest majority activation occurred after only 200 contaminated examples, while for the uniform schedule earliest activation occurred around 5000 poisoned samples. For the second attack, we inserted records into a retrieval corpus system without modifying its weights or decoding procedure. A single query-aware record captured a top-16 retrieval position for 58\% of templated requests and 97\% of free-text requests. Conditional on successful poisoned retrieval, 62\% of requests produced altered sequences, while 19-21% acquired the inserted domain by profile-HMM homology. A universal query-unaware attack using 1,500 records also achieved substantial retrieval capture and domain transfer. Together, these findings demonstrate that adversarial protein sequence contributions can establish persistent, sequence-specific behaviour during pre-training or redirect protein generation at inference, while leaving ordinary model behaviour largely preserved. Our work establishes the integrity and biological provenance of protein sequence data as critical safeguards for generative protein design systems.

</details>


### [214] [Hard Way, The Only Way: Provable Merits of Training on Hardest Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21201)
*Elvis Dohmatob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过加权经验风险最小化（ERM）建立数据剪枝的高维学习理论，刻画了测试误差曲线，并证明最优剪枝策略是仅保留2N个"最难"样本，可将分类误差率从 sqrt(N/P) 加速到 N/P。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据剪枝在AI系统中表现出"不合理的有效性"，但缺乏统一的理论解释，尤其缺乏对最优剪枝策略及其与损失函数、标签噪声交互作用的精确刻画。

Method: 在输入维度N与样本量P同时趋于无穷且N/P固定的高维极限下，沿输入空间的一维子空间进行剪枝，用加权ERM分析极限测试误差曲线，并求解高样本情形（P远大于N）下的最优剪枝策略。

Result: 该理论涵盖若干已有结果作为特例，给出评估剪枝策略与损失函数、标签噪声交互的通用框架；在高样本情形下证明最优策略为极端剪枝——丢弃几乎全部数据，仅保留严格为2N个最难的训练样本，使分类测试误差从经典 sqrt(N/P) 衰减加速至贝叶斯最优的 N/P 量级。

Conclusion: 战略性剪枝可突破无指数尾损失函数（如二次损失）下普通ERM的 sqrt(N/P) 速率，达到 N/P 的贝叶斯最优尺度，且该最优性能由仅保留2N个最难样本的极端剪枝实现。

Abstract: Despite a surge of empirical interest in the ``unreasonable effectiveness'' of drastic data pruning for AI systems, a unified theoretical understanding of this phenomenon remains elusive. In this work, we provide a precise high-dimensional theory of learning under data pruning via weighted empirical risk minimization (ERM). As in previous work, we analyze a setting where pruning is performed along a one-dimensional subspace of the input space. Our analysis precisely characterizes the limiting test error curves in the regime where the input dimension N and sample size P tend to infinity with a fixed ratio N/P. The resulting theory recovers several recent results in the literature as special cases and provides a general lens to evaluate how pruning strategies interact with the loss function and label noise. Crucially, we characterize the optimal pruning strategy in the high-sample regime where P greatly exceeds N. We demonstrate that strategic pruning can accelerate the decay of classification test error from the classical sqrt(N/P) rate, typical of vanilla ERM with loss functions without exponential tail (e.g., quadratic loss), to the much faster Bayes-optimal scaling of order N/P. In this setup, we prove that, surprisingly, this optimal performance is achieved by a drastic pruning strategy: discarding almost all training data except for a small proportion, consisting strictly of the 2N hardest training examples.

</details>


### [215] [Learning and Forecasting Multiscale Spatiotemporal Epidemic Dynamics with Lagrangian-Eulerian Graph Neural ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21223)
*Jinyi Duan,Qian Li,Biao Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LE-EpiGNODE，用过程特定、机制引导的神经算子替代通用消息传递，将传染病中长期预测解耦为拉格朗日感染生成与欧拉人口输运，并嵌入保结构的普适微分方程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中长期流行病预测需从部分观测外推高维、异质、非平稳动态；现有混合模型往往隐含“何种观测驱动何种流行病过程”以及“学到的图交互如何改变系统状态”，缺乏可检验的机制解释。

Method: LE-EpiGNODE 以机制引导的神经算子替换通用消息传递：年龄-接触信息驱动拉格朗日感染生成，流动性驱动保守的欧拉人口输运，二者统一在保结构的普适微分方程（universal differential equation）框架内。评测涵盖14种受控合成机制（时间调制、状态依赖、非线性、耦合动态）、3个互补的COVID-19场景、21个对照模型与5项针对性消融。

Result: 预测增益随预测时长增加而更明显；在最长预测期，LE-EpiGNODE 在三个真实数据集上均取得最低 WMAPE，较最优基线降低17.7%–40.0%。消融实验将增益归因于拉格朗日-欧拉分解与过程对齐先验；合成真值显示所学算子与诱导的状态通量能够跟踪已知生成动态。

Conclusion: 机制组织的图神经动力学是实现可靠长时程外推的可行路径，可在轨迹精度之外提供直接可检验的过程级机制解释。

Abstract: Accurate medium- to long-range epidemic forecasting requires extrapolating high-dimensional, heterogeneous and nonstationary dynamics from partial observations. Yet hybrid models often leave implicit which observations inform which epidemic processes and how learned graph interactions modify system state. We introduce LE-EpiGNODE, which replaces generic message passing with process-specific, mechanism-guided neural operators: age-contact information drives Lagrangian infection generation, whereas mobility drives conservative Eulerian population transport within a structure-preserving universal differential equation. We evaluated LE-EpiGNODE across 14 controlled synthetic mechanisms spanning time-modulated, state-dependent, nonlinear and coupled dynamics, three complementary COVID-19 settings, 21 comparator models and five targeted ablations. Forecasting gains tended to become more pronounced at longer horizons; at the longest horizon, LE-EpiGNODE achieved the lowest WMAPE in all three real-world datasets, reducing error by 17.7-40.0% relative to the best baseline. Controlled ablations linked these gains to the Lagrangian-Eulerian decomposition and process-aligned priors, while synthetic ground truth showed that learned operators and induced state fluxes track known generating dynamics. These results support mechanism-organized graph neural dynamics as a route to reliable long-horizon extrapolation with directly testable, process-level mechanistic interpretation beyond trajectory accuracy.

</details>


### [216] [Not All Change Is Damage: Update-Matched Unlearning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21230)
*Jie Li,Youming Tao,Bowei He,Meng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"更新匹配遗忘"（UMU）方法，通过引入与受防御模型经历相同模拟再学习更新的参考模型，在抑制指定知识再学习的同时更好保留模型可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法旨在移除大模型中选定知识以降低隐私与安全风险，但仅衡量"再学习是否被抑制"无法区分"选择性抵抗"与"模型可塑性整体退化"。同时，如何在更新后评估可塑性保留存在参考点选取难题：以更新前模型为参考，会错误惩罚更新带来的有益变化。

Method: 提出"参考时机假设"：在目标知识抑制程度相当时，使用"更新匹配参考"比"更新前参考"更能保留可塑性。据此设计UMU：对受防御模型和一个从未防御的、由同一遗忘模型初始化的参考模型施加相同的模拟再学习更新，并用新任务学习效果指导模型选择。

Result: 在TOFU与FaithUn数据集及Llama、Qwen模型上，UMU在三种基础遗忘方法上均同时提升再学习抵抗与可塑性：有害与良性再学习分别平均降低30.4和9.9个百分点，新任务准确率平均提升3.5个百分点。当参考模型不接收更新时，再学习抑制程度相近，但新任务准确率低于基础方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持参考时机假设，表明鲁棒遗忘应实现选择性地阻止知识恢复，而非压制模型的学习能力本身；更新匹配参考是可塑性保留评估与优化的关键。

Abstract: Unlearning aims to remove selected knowledge from large language models to mitigate privacy and safety risks while preserving useful capabilities. For models that continue to be fine-tuned after release, this goal is insufficient: they must also resist relearning the deleted knowledge while preserving plasticity, the ability to learn new tasks. Existing defenses train against simulated relearning while trying to preserve useful capabilities, but low relearning alone cannot distinguish selective resistance from a general loss of plasticity. Evaluating preservation after an update requires deciding what the updated model should remain close to. A pre-update reference penalizes useful changes induced by the update simply because they depart from earlier outputs. We therefore formulate the reference-timing hypothesis that, at comparable target suppression, an update-matched reference better preserves plasticity than a pre-update one. To test and address this hypothesis, we propose Update-Matched Unlearning (UMU), which applies the same simulated relearning update to the defended model and to an undefended reference initialized from the same unlearned model, and uses new-task learning to guide model selection. Extensive experiments on TOFU and FaithUn with Llama and Qwen models show that UMU improves both relearning resistance and plasticity across all three base unlearning methods, lowering harmful and benign relearning by 30.4 and 9.9 points on average while raising new-task accuracy by 3.5 points. When the reference does not receive the update, relearning remains similarly low but new-task accuracy falls below the base method, supporting the reference-timing hypothesis and showing that robust unlearning should prevent recovery selectively without suppressing learning itself.

</details>


### [217] [Compatibility Matters: A Framework for Token-Level Teacher Selection in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21234)
*Stanley Wei,Jiin Woo,Kaan Ozkara,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在on-policy蒸馏（OPD）中引入token级教师路由（teacher routing），理论分析教师-学生兼容性并给出教师选择启发式，在多领域实验中pass@k提升最高约10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD通常在单个prompt rollout甚至整个训练中仅使用单一教师，忽略了on-policy特性所带来的token级教师选择这一灵活性维度。作者希望探索何种教师与学生兼容，以及不同路由方案在不同场景下的优劣。

Method: 理论上刻画OPD中教师-学生兼容性的若干要素（推理模式兼容、教师能力、诱导的学生多样性），并对多种教师路由方案进行分类与证明；据此推导经验性教师选择启发式，在数学、代码、kernel生成、视觉推理等任务上进行广泛实验验证。

Result: 在数学、代码、kernel生成和视觉推理等领域的实验中，相比vanilla OPD及教师专家模型，部分任务pass@k提升达10个百分点以上，表明在高质量回答上同时实现了能力提升与多样性。

Conclusion: token级教师选择为OPD提供了有效的灵活性，不同场景下宜采用不同路由方案；该框架能同时提升学生能力与输出多样性，优于单一教师范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently been introduced as a promising paradigm for continual learning, where a student policy can be supervised at the token-level. Typically, OPD is performed using a single teacher per prompt rollout (or even per training run). However, this ignores a whole other axis of flexibility that is available due to the on-policy nature - token-level teacher selection during rollouts. Building on this insight, we theoretically explore what makes a teacher and a student compatible for OPD, and leverage our findings to derive several empirical heuristics for teacher selection. In particular, our framework captures several desiderata for OPD: compatible teacher-student reasoning patterns, teacher competence, and induced student diversity. Theoretically, we categorize several types of teacher routing schemes, and we prove that under different scenarios, different routing schemes are likely to be beneficial for optimization and student diversity. We back up our findings with extensive experimental results in domains such as math, coding, kernel generation, and visual reasoning, where we find upwards of 10 percentage points of improvement on pass@$k$ when compared to vanilla OPD and the teacher experts on certain tasks, indicating both capability improvement and diversity over high-quality responses.

</details>


### [218] [Representation Redistribution in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21240)
*Ziye Mei,Haotian Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出重分布敏感度分数（RSS），识别在提示变换下方向一致的稀疏特征变化，并将其与线性读出贡献解耦，实现对下游失败的定量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）可将语言模型激活分解为稀疏特征，但保持请求语义不变的提示变换会重分布特征活动。由此产生两个问题：单一变换上观察到的特征变化未必可复现；即使复现，也未必影响下游计算。因此需要区分"表征变化"与"失效"。

Method: 提出重分布敏感度分数（RSS），筛选出方向一致且反复出现的大幅特征响应，并在未参与选择的变体上验证其支撑集。随后将反复出现的表征变化与带符号的读出贡献、失效区分开：对固定线性读出给出精确分解，表明响应幅值、读出系数、方向对齐度与干净间隔共同决定下游后果。在两类冻结安全读出上，将RSS支撑侵蚀与完整干净间隔结合进行评测。

Result: 在覆盖八个语言模型的27个预训练SAE上，所测提示变换在留出变体上产生可复现的重分布画像。两类冻结安全读出中，RSS支撑侵蚀结合干净间隔的复发失效AUROC为0.935与0.958，仅用中位数20和19个特征；而使用完整侵蚀则为0.965与0.964。

Conclusion: RSS能够识别可复现的稀疏表征变化，并量化其对下游读出的贡献，而无需将表征变化等同于失效。代码开源。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose language model activations into sparse features, but prompt transformations designed to preserve the underlying request can redistribute activity across them. A feature change observed on one transformed prompt need not recur, and even a recurring change need not affect a downstream computation. We introduce the redistribution sensitivity score (RSS), which identifies large feature responses that recur in a consistent direction, and evaluate the resulting supports on variants excluded from selection. Across 27 pretrained SAEs spanning eight language models, the tested prompt transformations produce a reproducible redistribution profile on these excluded variants. We then separate recurring representation change from signed readout contribution and decision failure. For a fixed linear readout, an exact decomposition shows how response magnitude, readout coefficients, directional alignment, and the clean margin determine downstream consequences. In two frozen safety readouts, combining RSS support erosion with the full clean margin yields recurrent failure AUROCs of 0.935 and 0.958 using median supports of 20 and 19 features, compared with 0.965 and 0.964 using full erosion. RSS therefore provides a way to identify recurring sparse representation changes and quantify their contribution to a downstream readout without equating representation change with failure. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenSAE/Redistribution-Sensitivity-Score

</details>


### [219] [Isolating a Language Model's Judgment with a Label-Free Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21256)
*Chaemin Jang,Donggyu Lee,Jeesoo Kim,Dongman Lee,Jihee Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将语言模型的"内部判断"与"表达"分离的方法：在不同指令下同一输入的隐状态差异集中于低维"表达子空间"，无需基准标签即可识别并剔除该子空间对线性预测器的影响，从而恢复在指令扰动下依然可靠的内部判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常被要求做出判断（答案是否正确、语句是否逻辑蕴含、回答是否安全），但仅改变指示判断表达方式的指令，就可使最终答案变得不可靠。作者要探究这种不稳定性究竟反映内部判断本身发生变化，还是模型未能正确表达既有判断，即判断与表达是否可分离。

Method: 对同一输入在不同指令下的隐藏状态向量进行比较，发现其差异集中于一个低维表达子空间；在无基准标签的条件下识别该子空间，并从线性预测器中移除其对表示的贡献，得到"孤立判断"（isolated judgment）。在正确性、蕴含、安全三类任务上，用未参与子空间识别的指令进行验证；同时考察判断在不同层可读性以及判断问题被否定的情形。

Result: 在未用于识别子空间的指令下，孤立判断保持可靠，即便模型的最终答案与原始预测器出现退化；当判断问题被否定时，隔离将蕴含任务的AUROC从0.504提升到0.825，将安全任务从0.480提升到0.891。此外发现判断在更早的层即可读出，早于表达子空间开始干扰预测器之处。

Conclusion: 研究提供了将内部判断与表达相分离的方法，可在模型最终答案不可靠时恢复其内部判断，表明指令导致的答案波动主要来自表达层面而非判断本身。

Abstract: Language models are often asked to make judgments: whether a proposed answer is correct, whether one statement follows logically from another, or whether a response is safe. Yet changing the instructions that specify how the judgment should be expressed can make the final answer less reliable, even for the same input. We ask whether this reflects a change in internal judgment or a failure to express it. Comparing hidden-state vectors for the same input under different instructions, we find that their differences concentrate in a low-dimensional expression subspace. We identify this subspace without benchmark labels and remove its contribution from a linear predictor to obtain an isolated judgment. Across correctness, entailment, and safety, the isolated judgment remains reliable under instructions not used to identify the subspace, even when the model's final answer and the original predictor degrade. When the judgment question is negated, isolation raises the predictor's AUROC from 0.504 to 0.825 for entailment and from 0.480 to 0.891 for safety. We also find that judgment is readable in earlier layers, before the expression subspace begins to disrupt the predictor. Together, these results provide a way to separate judgment from expression and recover internal judgments even when the model's final answers are unreliable.

</details>


### [220] [Fractional Power-of-Two Quantization for Efficient and Effective Multiplier-Free LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21257)
*Sunghyun Wee,Geunjae Choi,Hyeonjin Kim,Suyoung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FPoT提出基-√2的半倍频程分数幂次量化网格，在保持整数移位-加法数据通路的同时实现实用的4位权重量化，显著优于传统PoT基线并接近均匀整数量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理中线性投影的乘累加（MAC）运算主导计算与能耗。训练后量化（PTQ）可降低成本，而幂次（PoT）权重量化能通过移位-加法逻辑实现无乘法器的高效推理，但其基-2粗量化网格在低比特下精度严重下降。

Method: 提出Fractional PoT（FPoT）：(i) 采用基-√2（半倍频程）分数权重网格配合均匀整数激活；(ii) 校准阶段的网格对齐细化，吸收进静态逐通道缩放因子；(iii) 轻量1加法器√2近似，其误差通过近似网格量化在权重校准中吸收，得到门数远少于4位整数乘法器的处理单元（PE）。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B的4位权重与激活量化下，FPoT相对PoT基线将零样本常识推理性能提升8.72%，同时接近均匀整数基线（仅0.4%p退化）。结论在7个模型、3种比特宽度等多种配置下均成立，并可集成到最先进的LLM PTQ方法中。

Conclusion: FPoT在保持全整数移位-加法硬件效率的同时，大幅缩小了PoT与均匀整数量化之间的精度差距，且具备框架无关的通用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demand efficient inference, where the multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations in linear projections dominate compute and energy cost. Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces this cost by mapping weights and activations to low-bit integers. Power-of-Two (PoT) weight quantization promises efficient multiplier-free LLM inference via shift-add logic, but suffers significant accuracy degradation at low bitwidths due to its coarse base-2 quantization grid. We introduce Fractional PoT (FPoT), a base-$\sqrt{2}$ (half-octave) fractional PoT grid that achieves practical 4-bit weight quantization while preserving a fully integer shift-add datapath. The method combines (i) the base-$\sqrt{2}$ weight grid with uniform integer activations, (ii) a calibration-time grid-alignment refinement absorbed into the static per-channel scale, and (iii) a lightweight 1-adder $\sqrt{2}$ approximation whose error is absorbed during weight calibration via approximate-grid quantization, yielding a processing element (PE) with significantly fewer gates than a 4-bit integer multiplier. On Llama-3-8B with 4-bit weight and activation quantization, FPoT improves zero-shot commonsense reasoning performance by 8.72% relative to the PoT baseline, while maintaining performance close to the uniform integer baseline, with only a negligible 0.4%p degradation. These results hold across various configurations spanning seven models and three bit-widths. Furthermore, FPoT can be integrated with state-of-the-art LLM PTQ methods, demonstrating framework-agnostic applicability.

</details>


### [221] [Behavioral Compositionality of Sparse Autoencoder Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21261)
*Haotian Ma,Ziye Mei,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAE加性残差（SAR），用于度量在选定交互阶数之上未被解释的行为变异，并区分SAE表示本身的组合性与下游计算引入的组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）用稀疏、线性可解码的特征表示模型激活，但这些特征对下游行为的效应未必是加性组合的，需要量化其非加性程度。

Method: 提出SAE加性残差（SAR），在独立特征子集采样下度量高于选定交互阶数的交互能量；通过比较学习到的SAE坐标与保持和不变的随机旋转，区分表示带来的组合性与下游计算的作用。

Result: 在全部18个模型与行为设置中，学习到的坐标一阶残差更低；但坐标并非完全加性，二阶项在所有设置中提升留出联盟排序，优于参数匹配的随机三阶项模型。联合引导实验中将秩保真度从0.5874提升到0.7790，排序误差从0.1444降至0.0606。

Conclusion: SAE坐标支持相对更具组合性的行为描述，但忠实的联合解释与干预仍需超越单个特征效应的交互项。代码见 https://github.com/OpenSAE/SAE-Behavioral-Compositionality。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) represent model activations with sparse, linearly decoded features, but the downstream effects of those features need not compose additively. We introduce SAE Additivity Residuals (SAR), which measure the behavioral variation left unexplained above a chosen interaction order. Under independent feature subset sampling, population SAR is exactly the normalized energy of interactions above that order and bounds coalition ordering error for the optimal low order approximation. To distinguish compositionality associated with the SAE representation from that induced by downstream computation, we compare learned SAE coordinates with sum preserving random rotations of the same selected subspace and find lower first order residuals for the learned coordinates across all 18 model and behavior settings. The coordinates are nevertheless not fully additive: pairwise terms improve held out coalition ordering in every setting and outperform parameter matched models using randomly selected third order terms. In a combined steering experiment, pairwise modeling raises rank fidelity from 0.5874 to 0.7790 and reduces ordering error from 0.1444 to 0.0606. Together, these results show that SAE coordinates support comparatively compositional behavioral descriptions while faithful joint explanations and interventions still require interactions beyond individual feature effects. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenSAE/SAE-Behavioral-Compositionality.

</details>


### [222] [Rethinking Learning from Label Proportions via Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21262)
*Tianhao Ma,Wei Wang,Yivan Zhang,Dong-Dong Wu,Takashi Ishida,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLP-MM方法，利用多阶阶乘矩作为训练目标，在更现实假设下解决标签比例学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签比例学习（LLP）方法大多假设实例-标签对独立同分布且随机组袋，该假设在现实中常不成立，导致非随机场景下性能不佳。

Method: 采用更现实的假设：袋独立同分布采样，实例标签在给定实例下条件独立；由此证明标签计数服从泊松多项分布，并提出LLP via moment matching（LLP-MM），以多阶阶乘矩为训练目标，促使分类器预测匹配这些矩。

Result: 在多个基准数据集及不同组袋策略下的大量实验表明，该方法有效且保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: LLP-MM通过矩匹配更充分利用数据底层统计结构，在非随机组袋的现实场景下优于依赖i.i.d.假设的传统方法。

Abstract: Learning from label proportions (LLP) is a weakly supervised setting where training data are grouped into bags with only bag-level label proportions observed, and the goal is to learn a classifier that predicts labels for individual instances. Recently, a large body of methods has emerged; however, most of them rely on the assumption that instance-label pairs are sampled i.i.d. and randomly assembled into bags, an assumption often violated in real-world scenarios. Our experimental results indicate that these methods perform suboptimally under non-random settings. In this work, we adopt a more realistic assumption: bags are sampled i.i.d., and instance labels are conditionally independent given the instances. Under this setting, we show that the label counts follow a Poisson multinomial distribution. Motivated by this observation, we propose LLP via moment matching (LLP-MM), a simple yet effective approach which leverages multi-order factorial moments as training objectives, encouraging the classifier predictions to match these moments and thereby more fully exploit the underlying statistical structure of the data. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets under various bag construction strategies demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach while maintaining high computational efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/TianhaoMa5/LLP-MM.

</details>


### [223] [Regret Lower Bound for Online Learning of Quantum States with Logarithmic Loss, with Applications to Quantum State Tomography and Confidence Sequences for Matrix Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21273)
*Yen-Huan Li,Wei-Fu Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态的对数损失在线学习，证明Exponential Weights及Tseng等(2025)算法的遗憾在维度上具有最优多项式依赖，并由此导出样本量多对数因子内最优的量子态层析算法，以及矩阵值随机变量均值的置信序列构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线投资组合选择的量子推广可应用于量子态层析，即从测量结果估计未知量子态。本文希望厘清在线量子态学习中对数损失的遗憾下界，并探究其在下游任务中的算法意义。

Method: 首先建立遗憾下界，据此分析Exponential Weights和对Tseng等(2025)算法的维度依赖。进一步将下界证明转化为量子态层析算法并分析样本复杂度，同时开发用于矩阵值随机变量均值的置信序列构造算法，并开展数值实验。

Result: 遗憾下界表明Exponential Weights和Tseng等(2025)算法的遗憾率在维度上达到最优多项式依赖。由下界证明得到的量子态层析算法在样本量多对数因子意义下样本最优；所提置信序列构造算法在数值实验中与现有方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了在线量子态学习对数损失问题的最优维度遗憾，并将理论工具应用于量子态层析与矩阵值随机变量均值的置信序列，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of online learning of quantum states with logarithmic loss, which is a quantum generalization of online portfolio selection and has applications to quantum state tomography, the task of estimating an unknown quantum state from measurement outcomes. We first establish a regret lower bound, showing that the regret rates of Exponential Weights and the algorithm of Tseng et al. (2025) have optimal polynomial dependence on the dimension. Our analysis leads to two further results. First, our lower-bound proof leads to a quantum state tomography algorithm that is sample optimal up to polylogarithmic factors in the sample size. Second, we develop an algorithm for constructing confidence sequences for the mean of a matrix-valued random variable. A numerical experiment shows that the latter algorithm is competitive with existing methods.

</details>


### [224] [TimeCap: A Scalable Open-Source Pipeline for Captioning Wearable Health Time-Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21274)
*Max Rosenblattl,Milana Gurbanova,Karl Aurel Deck,Narayan Schuetz,Thomas Kaar,Manuel Milling,Nicolas Zumarraga,Ning Wang,Kelly W. Zhang,Anders Johnson,Robert Jakob,Björn W. Schuller,Elgar Fleisch,Daniel Seung Kim,Allan Lawrie,Patrick Langer,Maxwell A. Xu,Euan Ashley,Paul Schmiedmayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeCap：确定性、无需语言模型的时序字幕生成流水线，产出迄今最大开放可穿戴时序字幕语料MHCap，并验证其对TSLM健康预测的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态时序信号规模大、异质性强，处理与解读困难；时序语言模型（TSLM）受限于缺乏信号对齐、可复现、可跨模态扩展的文本标注配对语料。现有配对语料要么闭源，要么规模与模态覆盖有限。用大语言模型扩标注虽可扩容，但输出不可精确复现，导致无法被他人重新推导、扩展或审计。需要一种确定性、可复用的语料生成机制。

Method: 提出TimeCap：开放、确定性的字幕生成流水线，包含三个可复用抽取器层级（extractor tiers）与跨通道合成层，直接基于信号运算、循环中不引入语言模型，仅需编写通道定义即可扩展到新schema。将其应用于OpenMHC（MyHeart Counts可穿戴数据集），生成MHCap字幕层；再用MHCap训练时序语言模型OpenTSLM，并在健康结局预测上做基准评测。

Result: MHCap为目前最大的开放多模态可穿戴时序字幕层，覆盖19个Apple HealthKit通道、908,944人日、7,005,763条字幕。在8项任务、32项按用户二分类评测中，带字幕增强训练的OpenTSLM-Cap在其中29项优于仅标签基线。

Conclusion: TimeCap提供可复现的数据集生成机制而非一次性发布，可作为未来语料构建的基础设施；MHCap与OpenTSLM共同证明信号锚定字幕能有效提升时序语言模型在健康结局预测上的表现。

Abstract: Time-series data generated continuously across industrial, environmental, and especially health domains produce multimodal signals whose scale and heterogeneity make their processing and interpretation particularly challenging. Time-Series Language Models (TSLMs) promise scalable interpretation of these signals, yet remain bottlenecked by a fundamental lack of text-annotated time series pairs that are signal-grounded, reproducible, and extensible across modalities and schemas. Existing paired corpora are either closed or limited in scale and modality coverage. Annotation with large language models could scale them up, but its outputs cannot be regenerated exactly, which prevents others from re-deriving, extending, or auditing the resulting corpus. To address this, we release TimeCap, an open deterministic captioning pipeline with three reusable extractor tiers and a cross-channel synthesis layer that operates directly on the signal with no language model in the loop, extensible to new schemas by writing channel definitions alone. This positions TimeCap as a reproducible mechanism for future dataset generation rather than a one-off release. Applying TimeCap to OpenMHC, the MyHeart Counts wearable dataset, yields MHCap, the largest open caption layer for multimodal wearable time-series data. MHCap spans 19 Apple HealthKit channels and 7,005,763 captions over 908,944 person-days. To showcase the impact of the corpus, we train OpenTSLM, a time-series language model, on MHCap and benchmark on health-outcome prediction. Caption-augmented training (OpenTSLM-Cap) outperforms a label-only baseline on 29 of 32 per-user binary evaluations across 8 tasks.

</details>


### [225] [Can Capability Enhancement be Localized?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21294)
*Anh Tuan Nguyen,Anna Bair,Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"能力增强定位（CEL）"问题：在给定模型与任务下，是否存在一小组注意力头，其移除反而能提升任务性能。作者先从经验层面发现此类稀疏头组合，再从理论上证明该现象可由训练动力学自然产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明注意力头的重要性并不均等，多数冗余头可安全删除，少数重要头被消融则严重损害性能。本文关注一个互补方向：是否存在一小组头，其移除能提升特定任务表现，即能力增强定位（CEL）。

Method: 经验上：跨多个模型家族，采用两阶段流程（先筛选候选头，再评估其联合消融），识别无需微调即可提升任务性能的稀疏头组合。理论上：构造简化多头 softmax 注意力模型与两个特征冲突的任务，证明从小随机初始化出发的梯度下降可证明地将冲突特征集中到特定头上。

Result: 经验上识别出可提升任务性能的稀疏头组合；理论上证明消融此类头会在一项任务上提升性能、同时损害另一任务，尽管训练已收敛到联合目标的全局最优。

Conclusion: 该工作为 CEL 提供了由训练诱导的理论解释：冲突特征的头内集中化是训练动力学的自然结果，因此消融"冲突头"可带来任务特定增益，同时以另一任务为代价。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models have shown strong capabilities across many tasks and domains. However, prior work shows that, for a given task, not all attention heads matter equally. Specifically, they suggest that many \textit{redundant} heads can be safely removed with little-to-no effect, while a small set of \textit{important} heads, when ablated, severely damages the model's performance on the given task. In this work, we investigate a complementary aspect, called \textit{Capability Enhancement Localization} (CEL): \textit{given a model and a task, does there exist a small set of heads whose removal improves the task performance?} We first investigate this question \textit{empirically} across models from different families. Using a simple two-stage routine that screens candidate heads and evaluates their joint ablations, we identify sparse head combinations whose removal improves task-specific performance without further fine-tuning. Motivated by these observations, we ask \textit{theoretically whether such a phenomenon can naturally arise from training dynamics, and under what conditions}. To this end, we construct a simplified multi-head softmax attention model and two tasks with conflicting features, and show that gradient descent from small random initialization provably concentrates the conflicting features into particular heads. We further show that ablating such heads improves performance on one task while harming the other, even though training converges to a global optimum of the joint objective. This establishes a possible training-induced theoretical explanation for CEL.

</details>


### [226] [Personalized Projected Ascent for Federated Stackelberg Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21295)
*M Yashwanth,Sharannya Ghosh,Saikiran Bulusu,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究个性化联邦极小极大问题：最小化变量x共享聚合、最大化变量y_i各客户端私有。分析投影上升法，证明非凸-凹达标准次线性速率，非凸-PL出现噪声地板；提出VR-SGDA用方差缩减消除噪声地板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦极小极大问题中，各客户端的内层最大化变量y_i含义与维度各不相同，直接跨客户端平均是病态的。因此需要保留y_i的个性化，仅共享聚合最小化变量x。

Method: 针对y_i受限于紧凸集，直接分析投影上升；在非凸-PL情形发现随机y_i更新导致步长不变的噪声地板。提出VR-SGDA：每个客户端维护私有变量快照，在同一采样上评估当前与快照梯度，在双时间尺度下将常数方差替换为消失漂移。

Result: 非凸-凹下投影个性化方案达到标准次线性速率；非凸-PL下出现噪声地板。VR-SGDA消除噪声地板，在保持个性化与投影的同时取得次线性主项速率。子群体鲁棒性与NLP对抗训练实验验证了最差组鲁棒性收益与双时间尺度跟踪机制。

Conclusion: 保留最大化变量的个性化并配合方差缩减的上升步，可有效消除非凸-PL下的噪声地板，同时维持个性化与投影，带来鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Federated minimax problems, such as distributionally robust optimization and adversarial training, include an inner maximization variable built from each client's local data. These variables differ in meaning and dimension from one client to the next, so averaging them across clients, as standard federated minimax methods do, is ill-posed. We therefore study a federated minimax problem where the minimization variable $\mathbf{x}$ is shared and aggregated, while each client retains the maximization variable $\mathbf{y}_i$ (personalized). Because these applications constrain $\mathbf{y}_i$ to a compact convex set, we analyze projected ascent directly. We show that, in the nonconvex-concave regime, this projected personalized scheme attains the standard sublinear rate. In the nonconvex-PL regime, however, it hits a stepsize-invariant noise floor due to stochastic $\mathbf{y}_i$ updates. To mitigate this, we propose VR-SGDA, incorporating a variance-reduced ascent step where each client maintains a private variable snapshot. By evaluating current and snapshot gradients on the same sample, the constant variance is replaced by a vanishing drift under a two-timescale regime. VR-SGDA eliminates the noise floor, attaining sublinear leading rates while preserving personalization and projection. Experiments on subpopulation-robustness and NLP adversarial training confirm both the worst-group robustness benefits and the two-timescale tracking mechanism.

</details>


### [227] [PocketTSLM: Deploying an On-Device Soft-Prompt Time-Series Language Model for Privacy-Preserving Smartphone Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21308)
*Max Rosenblattl,Nikhil Krishnaswamy,Leon Nissen,Manuel Milling,Nicolas Zumarraga,Ning Wang,Thomas Kaar,Robert Jakob,Björn W. Schuller,Elgar Fleisch,Patrick Langer,Paul Schmiedmayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PocketTSLM 是首个完全在消费级手机上本地运行的时间序列语言模型部署方案（OpenTSLM SoftPrompt 的移动端移植），通过将时间序列窗口编码为嵌入而非文本 token 注入模型，实现隐私保护、低延迟的生理信号推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级手机与可穿戴设备持续采集生理时序数据，但现有移动系统多将其处理为摘要或文本，破坏了时间结构，难以满足需要保留时序证据的健康系统需求。云端时序语言模型虽可规避手机算力限制，但需反复上传敏感生理数据，带来隐私风险、延迟和按查询计费的计算成本。而移动端替代方案（统计特征聚合或原始样本序列化为文本）要么压缩时序结构，要么耗尽小模型的上下文与内存预算。

Method: 提出 PocketTSLM——OpenTSLM SoftPrompt（OpenTSLM 时序语言模型的软提示变体）的移动端原生部署。将编码后的时序窗口以嵌入（embedding）形式注入模型，而非将时间序列 token 化为文本。采用数值一致性协议，在 Swift 移植版与基于 Apple MLX 框架的 Python 参考实现之间验证贪心解码 token 序列是否完全一致。同时在设备上开展上下文经济性基准测试，在相同骨干网络上对比软提示路径与文本序列化基线。

Result: 数值一致性协议验证 Swift 移植版与 Python 参考实现（Apple MLX）产生完全相同的贪心解码 token 序列；答案也与 PyTorch 参考一致，仅理由措辞可能不同。在基准测试格式下，软提示所用时间序列 token 数量约为文本序列化基线的 1/12，并降低了 prompt-prefill 成本。PocketTSLM 可处理 30 秒 Sleep-EDF 脑电片段用于睡眠分期，以及来自 ECG-QA 的 1000 样本（10 秒）12 导联心电图片段；相比之下，文本序列化基线在更长时序窗口下会超出手机的上下文或内存预算。

Conclusion: PocketTSLM 实现了全部推理完全在设备上运行，推理过程中不传输任何健康数据，在保证隐私的同时以软提示嵌入方式高效保留时序结构，显著降低 token 开销与预填充成本，为移动端生理时序证据的检索、路由与摘要提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Consumer phones and wearables increasingly collect physiological time-series data, yet most mobile systems treat these streams as summaries or text rather than as temporal evidence. This representation is poorly matched to personal health systems that need to retrieve, route, or summarize sensor evidence while preserving temporal structure. Cloud-hosted time-series language models avoid smartphone limits, but they repeatedly move sensitive physiology off the device and add latency and per-query compute cost. Mobile model alternatives that aggregate time-series data into statistical features or serialize raw samples as text either compress temporal structure or exhaust the context and memory budgets of small on-device models. We present PocketTSLM, a mobile-native deployment of OpenTSLM SoftPrompt, the soft-prompt variant of the OpenTSLM time-series language model. PocketTSLM runs fully on a consumer smartphone by injecting encoded time-series windows as embeddings instead of tokenizing time series as text. A numerical parity protocol verifies identical greedy-decoded token sequences between the Swift port and a Python reference using Apple's MLX machine-learning framework. Answers also match the PyTorch reference, although rationale wording can differ. An on-device context-economics benchmark compares the soft-prompt path with a text-serialized baseline on the same backbone. In our benchmark format, soft-prompting uses approximately 12 times fewer time-series tokens and reduces prompt-prefill cost. PocketTSLM processes 30-second Sleep-EDF electroencephalography clips for sleep staging and a 1,000-sample (10 s) 12-lead electrocardiogram clip from ECG-QA. In comparison, text-serialized baselines exceed the phone's context or memory budget at longer time-series windows. All inference runs entirely on the device, with no health data transmitted during inference.

</details>


### [228] [Spectrum Is Not Enough: A Random-Matrix Account of Data-Free Preconditioning for Differentially Private Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21312)
*Shilin Zhang,Kangning Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将DP-KFC中"探针须匹配私有因子特征值衰减"的验证准则转化为随机矩阵框架，解释无数据预条件中探针究竟能重建什么，并给出失效边界的定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无数据预条件用合成探针替代私有数据构建KFAC的Kronecker因子，但DP-KFC仅凭特征值衰减匹配来验证探针，缺乏对"探针能重建什么、何时失效"的理论理解。

Method: 在 A⊗G 分解下建立二分判据：误差信号因子 G 与结构决定的 A 的体（bulk）可无数据重建，仅 A 的数据诱导尖峰（spike）不可。证明 G 的数据不敏感性并给出显式误差能量界；用BBP阈值刻画bulk-only探针受尖峰损伤的精确形式 s²/(1-s²)；固定探针谱、仅改变特征基做对照实验；推导白化体积闭式律。

Result: G 半部在四种模态与一个自然图像基准的完整训练轨迹上零违例；损伤在尖峰超过 (d/n)^{1/4} 后不可见（注入尖峰代理验证）。谱形近乎匹配（对数比离散0.02）但特征向量随机的探针仍使残差膨胀2–90倍。白化体积律解释该现象并预测谱内临界体积排序，但不预测跨数据集量级。逐层调参不迁移到精度，headroom为小的 oracle-minus-white 差距。隐私机制下源排序在噪声交叉点前保持、之后反转，平坦探针反超匹配探针。

Conclusion: 谱形匹配既非充分亦非真正起作用的对象；可重建性由 G 的数据不敏感性与 A 的尖峰结构二分决定，失效边界由BBP阈值精确刻画，白化体积律为探针设计提供了新的理论指引。

Abstract: Data-free preconditioning builds the Kronecker factors of KFAC from synthetic probes instead of private data.
